# SpecDx Phase P2a (anchored cells, v3.1, no GPU) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Implement the SpecDx v3.1 delta that needs no GPU: anchored cells, the anchored cell model, v3.1 typing, the simulation study with its regime map and `specdx simulate`, and the integration into the loop, gate and screen. Then run G0 and G1 and freeze the v3.1 pre-registration, so that P2b can run G2, G3 and gate v3 on the same code.

**Architecture:**
- **Cells (Tasks 2–3).** `specdx/infer/cells.py` turns behaviours into readings of cells, each with the specification's own declared value as its anchor. There are three kinds:
  - (table, row, column) values;
  - golden events;
  - cross-cutting clauses.

  Readings are normalised syntactically. Rows are matched by natural key, and executor-generated keys by their foreign keys. A cell is read only at the steps that may write it. Lifted cells pool across scenarios in the lifted vocabulary. Each cell carries its difficulty group: its value column, its event kind, or its clause.
- **The anchored model (Tasks 4–5).** `specdx/infer/anchored.py` gives each cell a latent entailment set A_c: one value, a pair, or ∅.
  - Each model's accuracy is σ(α_m − β_{g(c)}), with difficulty shared by the cell's group.
  - Errors fall outside A_c and are pulled towards a flat pool (κ = 10). So silence predicts near-flat readings, and one model separates the forms (Theorem T2′).
  - EM is multi-start: T3's start plus a common α of 0, 1, 2 and 3. It is vectorised over cells on counts per (value slot, model).
- **Typing (Tasks 7, 9).** `specdx/infer/typing.py` has a cell-level core that the simulation and the real diagnosis share:
  - hidden candidates;
  - ambiguities, with no rank-correlation rule and no accuracy-contrast gate;
  - weak checks.

  On an anchored cell an ambiguity and its hidden requirement co-occur (resolution 11). `diagnose_v3` refutes hidden candidates into hidden requirements or contradictions of size 1. It keeps v2's multi-clause contradictions through v2's own candidate path, shared with `diagnose` (Task 9), with the Unobservable list, `known` ordering and `spent`, and returns the existing `Diagnosis` and `Defect` types.
- **Simulation (Tasks 6, 8, 11).** `specdx/sim/` covers:
  - generating synthetic specifications from the §3.2 model, with the A3, A5 and within-group arms;
  - scoring v3.1 against three baselines;
  - §8.1's sweeps and regime map (`specdx simulate`);
  - calibrating from cached base runs, read-only;
  - running G0's grid, selection rule and regime-based criteria.
- **Integration (Task 10).**
  - The loop fits cells instead of v2's stage 2.
  - The ddmin oracle tests P(|A_c| = 2) at fixed parameters.
  - B2 and the refuter use θ_m.
  - The screen admits at act rate 0.3, stops without four acting models, and can re-admit a cached screen.
- **GO steps (Tasks 12–15).** G1 replays the cached base runs read-only. G0 and G1 run on this machine, and the frozen pre-registration closes P2a.

**Tech stack:** Python 3.13, numpy, PostgreSQL 16 (Docker, port 55434) for the existing database tests only, pytest, ruff. No Ollama and no model call anywhere in P2a.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-specdx-v3-design.md`, v3.1 as committed at `5b406a2` (binding).
- It is a delta on `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-specdx-v2-design.md`, which is a delta on `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-specdx-design.md`. Both stay binding where v3.1 does not replace them.
- Before starting, read v3.1 in full, with §13 items 8–14 for the v3.1 amendments. Then read v1 §2.2, §2.4, §3.3, §3.6, §3.7, §4.6 and §11, and v2 §3 and §4.
- Evidence for v3.1: `results/p2-spikes.md`.
- Context:
  - `results/p1b-outcome.md`;
  - `results/p1b-theta-diagnosis.md`;
  - the P1b ledger `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-specdx-p1b-ledger.md`, for the rulings behind `known`, `spent` and the Unobservable list.

## Global Constraints

- `vendor/` is never edited. `vendor/tonomyx/` is byte-identical to TonomyX commit `34c1d2ce70583b2de54573d28ba4f49e0af4cc7f`.
- The plan executes in a git worktree on branch `worktree-specdx-p2a`, with its own `.venv` (Task 1).
- **The P1 run cache.** `/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX-paper/work` is **read-only in P2a**: never write, move or delete anything there.
  - Only Tasks 13 and 14 read it, with `SPECDX_WORK=/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX-paper/work` prefixed to one command at a time. Never `export` it, so tests never see it.
  - Read-only access goes through `specdx/sim/calibrate.py` and `experiments/p2_replay.py`. They read `runs.jsonl` and extract the Tell PDFs into a temporary directory. They open no `Workbench`, no database and no Ollama, and write only their `--out` file.
  - Before and after any command that reads the cache, check:
    - `md5sum /home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX-paper/work/runs.jsonl`, expected `db1e1823c66df102648c69eb6a8789b3` (6,505 lines);
    - `ls -la --time-style=full-iso /home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX-paper/work | md5sum`.

    A change stops the plan.
  - No command that builds mutants (`specdx mutants`, `specdx gate`, `specdx run --variant <mutant>`) runs in P2a.
- **Database.**
  - Port 55434 only (`postgresql://tonomyx:tonomyx@localhost:55434/tonomyx`); never touch 55432 or 55433.
  - Every DB test is marked `@pytest.mark.db`.
  - One pytest process at a time: the advisory lock refuses a second.
  - The `specdx-postgres` container already runs; never `docker compose up` from the worktree.
- **No LLM calls in P2a**: no Ollama, not even a version or tags query. No test calls a real Ollama.
- **Run keys.** `RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION` stays 3 and `interpreter_settings()` is unchanged (spec §0). The P1b screen and smoke runs stay cache hits; Task 1 pins this.
- **Rulings.** The author's rulings F1–F26 (2026-10-15) are binding; the list after the planning resolutions says where each lands. Spec v3.1 stays binding over every ruling.
- **Code.**
  - Python 3.13, typed, `ruff check .` clean at line length 100 (pyproject.toml).
  - Comments are sparse and explain why.
  - Identifiers, comments, tests and documents are in English. Vietnamese appears only in Tell text, clause values and `docs/RUNNING.md`'s quick-start.
- **Parameters.** `V3Params`' defaults are **provisional** until Task 13 freezes G0's choice. Every test whose outcome depends on a parameter value passes explicit `V3Params(...)`. G1 refuses to run on provisional parameters.
- **Pre-registered values (spec v3.1 §9), unchanged from v1 and v2:**
  - temperature 0.7; Tell variants 0–2; seeds 1–3;
  - budgets 200/100/80; scorability k = 3; K_max = 6;
  - the gate rule; dev split (`dev_per_type` 5, `rng_seed` 20261006);
  - harness nudges 2; ω = 0.3, m_min = 3; Rasch λ = 1.0;
  - T-boundary scenarios ≤ 12; workers ≤ `OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL`.
- **New, fixed by spec v3.1 §9:**
  - |A_c| ≤ 2; the candidate rule of §3.1; the normalisation of §2.5;
  - the row-matching rule of §2.6; the cell scope of §2.4; pooling of lifted cells (§2.7);
  - EM tolerance 1e-6 and at most 200 iterations;
  - the screen's act-rate threshold 0.3;
  - κ = 10 with a flat pool u_c;
  - difficulty grouped by value column, event kind and clause (resolution 5);
  - on an anchored cell, an ambiguity and its hidden requirement co-occur (§3.1, §5.1, §5.3; resolution 11);
  - multi-start EM (T3's start plus a common α ∈ {0, 1, 2, 3});
  - G0 with at least 10 replicates, and τ ∈ {1, 2} in the grid; the study with at least 10 replicates per point, 5 on the regime map;
  - at least 4 acting models;
  - the G0–G3 criteria of §8.2.
- **Chosen at G0 by the selection rule, then frozen:** π and the form shares (s_mis, s_amb, s_∅); τ; μ_α, σ_α; τ_H, τ_A, τ_W. κ is not a G0 parameter.
- **Selection rule (§9).** A grid over the free parameters, written in the simulation module before G0 runs. The objective is the mean simulated typed F1 over hidden requirement, ambiguity and weak check, at the calibrated parameters and under the misspecification arms equally weighted. Ties go to the more conservative setting (higher P(|A| = 1), smaller π).
- **GO criteria (spec v3.1 §8.2), copied exactly:**
  - **G0:**
    - (i) mean |θ̂ − θ| ≤ 0.05.
    - (ii) Simulated typed F1 ≥ 0.6 for hidden requirement, ≥ 0.4 for ambiguity, and ≥ 0.4 for weak check (the gate's own bar; v3's 0.6 for weak was reached by no method, §13 item 13).
    - (iii) Regime-based: at the calibrated point, under the A3 and A5 arms, and at the population's own M and Δ, v3.1 is never below the best baseline by more than 0.05 on any type; and at the calibrated point it beats the best baseline on ambiguity by more than 0.05.
    - If G0 fails, stop and report.
    - The criteria are judged on the selection replicates; the next ten seeds give an out-of-sample report for information only, and non-converged fits are counted and reported (rulings F2, F22).
  - **G1:**
    - (i) θ_m ≥ 0.5 for gpt-oss:20b and qwen3:30b-a3b (no collapse).
    - (ii) The real defect R-SO (v1 §4.4: `SO-0031` stays `Chờ duyệt` after `APR-0102` is approved) is reported as a hidden requirement, judged **on the P1b smoke run only** (author ruling, 2026-10-14: P1's two acted readings at that cell equal the anchor). If no acted behaviour reached the cell, G1 records "not observable" for (ii) and does not fail on it.
    - On failure, stop and report; parameters are not re-tuned.
  - **G2:** at least 2 families and at least 4 acting models admitted, otherwise STOP. **G3:** every P1b smoke check passes, and θ_m ≥ 0.5 for each population model; convergence is reported, not a criterion (ruling F15). (Both in P2b.)
- **Gate v3 (§8.3).** The v2 rule, unchanged:
  - contradiction and hidden win when SpecDx's typed F1 exceeds max(B2, B3) untyped F1;
  - ambiguity and weak win when SpecDx's typed F1 is ≥ 0.4;
  - a type with fewer than 3 scorable mutants is a loss;
  - 3 or more wins of 4 pass;
  - exact-arithmetic F1;
  - B2 uses the interpreter with the highest θ_m.
- **Never tune on the development or test split.** P2a runs no mutant.
- **Commands.** Run tests with `.venv/bin/python -m pytest` (pyproject's `addopts = "-q"` prints `N passed`; do not add `-q`). Lint with `.venv/bin/ruff check .`.

## Review Focus

Five conditions the spec implies but the main tests would not exercise. Each has a test in the task that owns the code:

1. **A model with no acted reading, or a cell read by one model only.**
   - The fit must keep the model with no acted reading at the prior mean.
   - A cell read by one model must still be typed: an ambiguity or an unstated subtype needs no accuracy contrast under the flat pool (T2′).
   - *Tests:* Task 5 `test_weights_family_effect_and_a_model_without_readings`; Task 7 `test_an_ambiguity_needs_tau_a_and_a_pair_but_no_accuracy_contrast` and `test_the_subtype_follows_the_most_probable_defect_form_even_for_one_model`; Task 10 `test_a_population_that_never_acts_types_nothing_and_still_reports`.
2. **A pooled lifted cell across scenarios with different parameter values.** This includes happy and discount8, where ordered = received = 15 and `lift_value` returns no placeholder. "Equals this scenario's value of the anchor's parameter" must be one reading everywhere.
   - *Tests:* Task 3 `test_a_lifted_golden_cell_is_one_cell_across_scenarios_in_the_lifted_vocabulary` and `test_lift_reading_keeps_text_and_prefers_the_anchor_on_a_tie`.
3. **Readings that agree, or that are all distinct.**
   - An anchored cell where every reading agrees on one non-anchor value must be a mis-key, not silence.
   - One where every reading is distinct puts the defect mass on ∅.
   - With the flat pool, one model separates all four forms.
   - *Tests:* Task 4 `test_readings_that_all_agree_on_another_value_are_a_mis_key_not_silence`, `test_readings_that_are_all_distinct_put_the_defect_mass_on_silence` and `test_one_model_separates_all_four_forms_when_the_error_pool_is_flat`.
4. **EM that does not converge** must say so in the Report JSON (`converged`, `iterations`) and in G0's record, never silently.
   - *Tests:* Task 5 `test_non_convergence_is_reported`; Task 10 `test_non_convergence_surfaces_in_the_report_json`; Task 11 `test_a_g0_run_writes_its_report_and_the_chosen_parameters`.
5. **Identical input must give a byte-identical fit and report**, whatever the order of readings, so that resume and parallel workers stay exact.
   - *Tests:* Task 3 `test_extraction_does_not_depend_on_the_order_of_behaviours`; Task 5 `test_the_fit_is_deterministic_and_ignores_the_order_of_readings`; Task 9 `test_the_diagnosis_is_deterministic` (a single hidden cell and a multi-clause contradiction, each with its behaviours reversed); Task 10 `test_identical_input_gives_a_byte_identical_report`.

Further cases are pinned in their owning tasks' main tests:
- executor-generated key detection on token boundaries (`SOL-1` against `SOL-10`): Task 2's `test_a_key_occurs_only_on_token_boundaries` and `test_the_rule_reads_t_only`;
- G2's four-model rule: Task 10's `test_the_screen_stops_without_two_families_and_four_acting_models`;
- an ambiguity and its hidden requirement co-occur, in the typing core and in G0's threshold filter: Task 7's `test_on_an_anchored_cell_an_ambiguity_and_its_hidden_requirement_co_occur` and Task 11's `test_the_threshold_filter_keeps_an_ambiguity_and_its_hidden_requirement_together`;
- v2's `diagnose` is unchanged by the shared candidate path: `tests/infer/test_scores.py` and `tests/test_replay.py` run unmodified in Task 9.

## Planning-time resolutions (spec v3.1 left these open)

Each is implemented in the named task and recorded in `experiments/p2-preregistration.md` (Task 15).

1. **Lifted vocabulary (§2.7, clarified 2026-10-14).** *(Task 3)*
   - A pooled value cell's anchor is the clause's placeholder (`{ordered}`).
   - In scenario s, a reading becomes the placeholder `lift_value(reading, column type, s)` returns.
   - `lift_value` returns none when a value equals two parameters at once (happy and discount8 order and receive 15). The reading then becomes the anchor's own placeholder if the anchor's parameter is among them, and otherwise stays as normalised text.
2. **Booleans (§2.5).** The golden CSVs write bool columns as `t`/`f`, while end states, read through the typed database, write `true`/`false`. C's own comparison (`clauses._same`) treats them as equal. A bool column's values are canonicalised to `true`/`false`: a type-level canonicalisation like the numeral rule, not a semantic mapping. *(Task 2)*
3. **Scope of tables no step drafts (§2.4).** *(Task 3)*
   - `draft_tables` covers so, soline, po, grn, inv, vinv and pay.
   - apr rows and events are written through tools (`create_approval`, the persona's decision, `log_event`). Their cells are therefore read at the steps whose work-order slice keys the row (`apr=APR-0102`, `evt=SO-0031`).
   - Under the literal rule, no apr or event cell would ever be read. That contradicts §2.2 (golden events are value cells) and §5.4 (weak checks on event cells).
   - Key and matching columns are row identity, not readings.
4. **One reading per behaviour and cell (§3.2).** A behaviour reads a cell if it acted at any in-scope item of its scenario. Several matching rows give the smallest normalised value; `absent` when no row matches. *(Task 3)*
5. **Difficulty groups (§3.2).** *(Tasks 3, 5)*
   - A value cell's group is its column, pooled over rows and scenarios.
   - An event cell's group is its event kind (`evt:DRAFTED`), the event table's analogue of a column.
   - A constraint cell's group is its clause.
   - `Cell.group` holds the group; the v3 ε̄_col pooling it once served is gone.
6. **The M-step (§3.4).** *(Tasks 4–5)*
   - α and β: Newton steps on Σ [r·η − q·log(1 + e^η)] − priors.
     - r is the faithful probability.
     - q_c = P(A_c ≠ ∅) trials per reading: under ∅ no reading is a trial of accuracy.
     - The sums run over each difficulty group.
   - ε_c: the mode of Dirichlet(1 + κ·u_c + expected error counts), that is (counts + κ·u_c)/(N + κ), with κ = 10 and u_c flat over V_c and OTHER.
     - The literal mode of Dirichlet(κ·u_c) is 0 wherever κ·u + count < 1.
     - A zero error mass makes every reading of that value impossible outside A_c.
7. **Refutation of a pooled hidden-requirement candidate** runs in the scenario with the most acted readings outside d_c, ties broken by the smaller scenario id. *(Task 9)*
8. **Singleton hitting sets defer to the cell path**, the only source of hidden requirements and of contradictions of size 1. Hitting sets of two or more clauses feed multi-clause contradictions. *(Task 9)*
   - `spent` (P1b ruling I2) keeps its purpose: the locations that cost a probe run, or would have, and found nothing.
   - In v3.1 a cell candidate always yields a finding, so these are the multi-clause candidates refuted by a probe run or never attempted.
9. **Defect locations reuse v2's formats**, so `specdx/eval/matching.py` keeps working. *(Task 9)*
   - Hidden and contradiction findings sit at the clause id (`golden:po:PO-0015:trang_thai`, `inv:po-15`, `event:DRAFTED:SO-0031:AI`).
   - Ambiguity and weak findings sit at `table.column` or `evt:KIND:REF`; a constraint cell's ambiguity sits at its clause id.
   - Their note is `scenario|step`: the scenario with the most readings (ties to the smaller id) and the cell's first in-scope step.
   - `Defect` gains an informational `subtype` (mis-key, ambiguous-pick, unstated), which is never scored.
10. **Provisional `V3Params` defaults** are labelled as such in the code, and every parameter-dependent test passes explicit parameters. Task 13 rewrites the marked block with G0's choice (`PARAMS_FROZEN = True`). κ = 10 is a module constant outside that block. *(Tasks 4, 11, 13)*
11. **Ambiguity and the hidden subtype need no accuracy contrast (§5.3, T2′), and they co-occur (§3.1, §5.1, §5.3; ruling F1).** *(Tasks 7, 11)*
    - The ambiguity rule is P(|A_c| = 2) ≥ τ_A, and the subtype follows the most probable defect form (§5.1's "MAP form"), for any number of models.
    - A cell with one candidate value has no pair, so it is never an ambiguity.
    - On an anchored cell, a reported ambiguity also yields its hidden candidate: subtype ambiguous-pick, score max(P(A_c ≠ {d_c}), P(|A_c| = 2)). A hidden candidate (P(A_c ≠ {d_c}) ≥ τ_H) whose most probable defect form is a pair also yields its ambiguity. An unanchored cell reports the ambiguity alone. G0's threshold filter (`g0.counts_at`) applies the same rule.
12. **The ddmin oracle (§5.3)** re-runs, under each Tell variant at seed 1, every population model with at least one acted behaviour in the specification, highest θ_m first. It then asks the loop's test: P(|A_c| = 2) on the re-runs' readings of the ambiguity's cell, with α, β_{g(c)}, ε_c and V_c held at their fitted values, is at least τ_A. *(Task 10)*
13. **G2 (§7).** `specdx screen --from results/p1b-screen.json --out results/p2-screen` applies the current admission rules to the cached screen's measurements, with no run, database or model. The stop rule is fewer than 2 families or fewer than 4 acting models in the population. *(Task 10)*
14. **Simulation design (§8.1).** *(Tasks 6, 8, 11)*
    - A defect form and a difficulty β_g belong to a column (all its per-scenario cells, or its one pooled cell), as a defect belongs to a rule. The within-group arm spreads cell difficulties around the group's by τ_within = 0.5.
    - Truths:
      - weak-check truths are unanchored cells that T determines (|A_c| = 1);
      - ambiguity truths are pairs;
      - hidden truths are anchored cells with A_c ≠ {d_c}.
    - A behaviour is clean at an item when all its anchored readings there equal their anchors. Faithful readings are uniform over A_c.
    - Arms: the A3 arm gives each model its own concentrated error distribution; the A5 arm lets the strongest model read one guessed value with its accuracy at silent cells.
    - The defect rate is planted at 0.15, not calibrated.
    - Regime map:
      - accuracies are spread evenly by Δ around the mean, with every α shifted, each clipped to 0.51–0.995;
      - act rates follow the calibrated population's order;
      - scenarios stay at the calibrated count.
    - Limitation (ruling F24): a simulated difficulty group is a single-form column whose cells differ only by scenario, while a real value column pools rows of different forms and anchoring under one β. G0 does not exercise that case; the generator is not changed after the preview.
15. **Calibration (§8.1)** reads base runs only. *(Task 11)*
    - Per-model accuracy: measured on the population's readings, at anchored cells whose plurality among the population's readings is the anchor (ruling F12 (a)).
    - Dispersion: the standard deviation of those cells' logit accuracy, from the population's readings, kept within 0.25–3.
    - Error concentration: the nearest of six Dirichlet concentrations that reproduces the mean top-error share.
    - Act rates come from the screen. The cell mix and |V| (observed distinct values plus one, a column's low median over its cells) are shares of columns, as the generator draws them: a pooled cell is a column, and a per-scenario cell's column is its row and column across scenarios; a column is anchored when at least half its cells are (ruling F12 (c)).
    - The planned budget 200 escalates ⌊200 / (models × 9)⌋ scenarios, and readings per cell follow from it; the base runs' readings per cell (`reads`) are reported beside the simulated specification's (`simulated_reads`) (ruling F12 (b)).
    - A statistic without data raises `CalibrationError` instead of falling back to a default (ruling F13).
16. **G0 (iii)'s "population's own M and Δ"** is the regime-map point nearest the calibrated population: its number of models, the grid accuracy nearest its mean accuracy, and the grid spread nearest its accuracy range. For the calibrated population that is M = 4, accuracy 0.95, Δ = 0.1. *(Task 11)*
17. **θ_m and w_b (ruling F17).** B2 and the refuter's interpreter order use θ_m (§8.3, §11: per-model quantities, as v2's refuter order already was); w_b weights the hitting sets (§3.5). *(Tasks 9, 10)*
18. **§5.2's V(b) (ruling F18)** is v2 §3.4's acted violation set, over every clause of C, golden values and events included. The literal "constraint cells" would drop the golden-and-invariant conflicts that are exactly the C2 mutants' truths. *(Task 9)*

## Where the rulings land (F1–F26, 2026-10-15)

| Ruling | Where |
|---|---|
| F1 co-occurrence | resolution 11; Task 7 (`co_occur`, its test); Task 11 (`counts_at`, its test); pre-registration §3 and §6 |
| F2 G0 non-convergence | Task 11 (`search`, `check`, `run`, `render`, the run test) |
| F3 soline assertion | Task 3's test |
| F4 weak suppression test | Task 7's test |

## How code steps are written

Each code step is preceded by an HTML comment that names the file and the operation. The pre-check applied these exactly:
- `<!-- apply: create PATH -->`: the block is the whole file, new or replaced.
- `<!-- apply: append PATH -->`: the block is appended after two blank lines.
- `<!-- apply: replace PATH -->`: the first block is the exact current text, which occurs once in the file; the second (after "with:") replaces it.

```
specdx/
  infer/cells.py          NEW  normalisation, row matching, cells, groups, scope, pooling, readings (T2–3)
  infer/anchored.py       NEW  V3Params, κ = 10, candidate sets, prior, E-step, multi-start EM (T4–5)
```

```python
"""Cells, part 1 (spec v3 §2.5–§2.6): normalisation and row matching on the real base pack."""

import unicodedata

import pytest

from specdx.infer.cells import (
    executor_generated,
    match_columns,
    normalise,
    occurs,
    table_rows,
)
from specdx.spec.columns import ColumnModel
from specdx.spec.tell import Chunk, Sentence


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("raw", "column_type", "expected"), [
    (unicodedata.normalize("NFD", "Nháp"), "text", "nháp"),
    ("  Chờ \t  duyệt\n", "text", "chờ duyệt"),
    ("Draft", "text", "draft"), ("DRAFT", "text", "draft"),
    ("35", "int", "35"), ("35.0", "int", "35"), ("35,0", "numeric", "35"),
    ("0.50", "numeric", "0.5"), ("350", "int", "350"), ("-0", "int", "0"), ("+7", "int", "7"),
    ("2026-09-01", "date", "2026-09-01"),
    ("t", "bool", "true"), ("TRUE", "bool", "true"), ("f", "bool", "false"),
    ("t", "text", "t"),
])
def test_normalisation_is_syntactic(raw: str, column_type: str, expected: str) -> None:
    assert normalise(raw, column_type) == expected


def test_normalisation_applies_no_semantic_mapping() -> None:
    assert normalise("draft") != normalise("Nháp")
    assert normalise("Đã duyệt") != normalise("Da duyet")


def test_a_key_occurs_only_on_token_boundaries() -> None:
    texts = [normalise("Mã dòng SOL-10 và PO-0015-01."), normalise("Đơn PO-0015.")]
    assert not occurs("SOL-1", texts) and occurs("SOL-10", texts)
    assert occurs("PO-0015", texts[1:]) and not occurs("PO-0015", texts[:1])
    assert occurs("po-0015", texts)
    assert not occurs("", texts)


def test_the_executor_generated_keys_of_the_base_pack(pack, tell_corpus) -> None:
    columns = ColumnModel.of(pack)
    generated = executor_generated(pack, columns, tell_corpus)
    assert generated == {"soline", "insp", "ncr", "ship"}
    assert match_columns("soline", pack, columns, generated) == ("so", "ma_sp")
    assert match_columns("po", pack, columns, generated) == ("ma_po",)


def test_the_rule_reads_t_only(pack, tell_corpus) -> None:
    columns = ColumnModel.of(pack)
    told = tell_corpus.with_chunk(
        Chunk("EXTRA:000", "EXTRA", (Sentence("EXTRA:000:00", "Dòng SOLINE-0031-1 của đơn."),)))
    assert "soline" not in executor_generated(pack, columns, told)


def test_soline_rows_match_on_order_and_item_not_on_the_surrogate_id(pack, tell_corpus) -> None:
    rows = table_rows(pack, ColumnModel.of(pack), tell_corpus, ["soline", "po"])
    soline, po = rows["soline"], rows["po"]
    assert soline.name({"ma_soline": "SOLINE-001", "so": "SO-0031", "ma_sp": "ITEM-0005"}) == (
        "SOLINE-0031-1", True)
    assert soline.name({"ma_soline": "SO-0031-L2", "so": "so-0031", "ma_sp": "ITEM-0012 "}) == (
        "SOLINE-0031-2", True)
    assert soline.name({"ma_soline": "SOLINE-0031-1", "so": "SO-0031", "ma_sp": "ITEM-0099"}) == (
        "so-0031/item-0099", False)
    assert po.name({"ma_po": "PO-0015"}) == ("PO-0015", True)
    assert po.name({"ma_po": "PO-0015-01"}) == ("po-0015-01", False)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the implementation**

<!-- apply: create specdx/infer/cells.py -->
```python
"""Anchored cells (spec v3 §2): the unit of evidence.

A cell is one (table, row, column) value, one golden event, or one cross-cutting clause,
read at the items whose step may write it. This module holds the syntactic normalisation
of readings (§2.5) and the matching of rows by natural key (§2.6).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import unicodedata
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal

from tonomyx.canon.pack import CanonPack

from specdx.db import golden_tables, is_background
from specdx.spec.columns import ColumnClass, ColumnModel
from specdx.spec.tell import TellCorpus

ABSENT = "absent"
_NUMERAL = re.compile(r"^[+-]?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?$")
_TRUE = frozenset({"t", "true", "1"})
_FALSE = frozenset({"f", "false", "0"})


def normalise(value: str, column_type: str = "text") -> str:
    """§2.5, in order: Unicode NFC, trim, inner whitespace collapsed, casefold, numerals
    canonicalised (35, 35.0 and 35,0 are 35). No semantic mapping: draft and Nháp stay
    distinct. A bool column's t/f (the golden CSVs) and true/false (end states read through
    the typed database) are one value, as C's own comparison already treats them."""
    text = " ".join(unicodedata.normalize("NFC", value).split()).casefold()
    if column_type == "bool":
        if text in _TRUE:
            return "true"
        if text in _FALSE:
            return "false"
    if _NUMERAL.match(text):
        number = Decimal(text.replace(",", "."))
        return "0" if number == 0 else format(number.normalize(), "f")
    return text


def tell_texts(corpus: TellCorpus) -> list[str]:
    """Every sentence of T, normalised once for token search."""
    return [normalise(corpus.sentence(s)) for s in corpus.sentence_ids()]


def occurs(value: str, texts: Iterable[str]) -> bool:
    """Whether the normalised value occurs in a normalised text as a whole token: neither
    preceded nor followed by a word character or a hyphen, so SOL-1 is not found in SOL-10
    and PO-0015 is not found in PO-0015-01."""
    needle = normalise(value)
    if not needle:
        return False
    pattern = re.compile(rf"(?<![\w-]){re.escape(needle)}(?![\w-])")
    return any(pattern.search(text) for text in texts)


def _case_rows(pack: CanonPack, table: str) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    return [r for r in golden_tables(pack).get(table, []) if not is_background(table, r)]


def executor_generated(
    pack: CanonPack, columns: ColumnModel, corpus: TellCorpus
) -> frozenset[str]:
    """§2.6: the tables whose canon key T never states. A key is executor-generated when
    none of its golden values occurs anywhere in T; the test reads T only. Events have no
    key of their own (they are identified by kind, ref and actor)."""
    texts = tell_texts(corpus)
    out: set[str] = set()
    for table in sorted(golden_tables(pack)):
        keys = columns.keys.get(table, ())
        rows = _case_rows(pack, table)
        if table == "evt" or not keys or not rows:
            continue
        if not any(occurs(":".join(r[k] for k in keys), texts) for r in rows):
            out.add(table)
    return frozenset(out)


def match_columns(
    table: str, pack: CanonPack, columns: ColumnModel, generated: frozenset[str]
) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """The columns that identify a row: its key, or for an executor-generated key the
    remaining LOCKED foreign-key columns (soline rows match on so and ma_sp)."""
    keys = columns.keys[table]
    if table not in generated:
        return keys
    return tuple(c.name for c in pack.columns[table]
                 if c.fk and c.name not in keys
                 and columns.classes[(table, c.name)] is ColumnClass.LOCKED)


def identity(row: Mapping[str, str], match: tuple[str, ...]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    return tuple(normalise(str(row.get(c, ""))) for c in match)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TableRows:
    """How one table's rows are named. golden: a golden row's identity → its canon key
    (SOLINE-0031-1). A row matching no golden row is named by its normalised identity."""

    table: str
    keys: tuple[str, ...]
    match: tuple[str, ...]
    golden: dict[tuple[str, ...], str]

    def name(self, row: Mapping[str, str]) -> tuple[str, bool]:
        """The row's name and whether it matches a golden row."""
        ident = identity(row, self.match)
        if ident in self.golden:
            return self.golden[ident], True
        return "/".join(ident), False


def table_rows(
    pack: CanonPack, columns: ColumnModel, corpus: TellCorpus, tables: Iterable[str]
) -> dict[str, TableRows]:
    generated = executor_generated(pack, columns, corpus)
    out: dict[str, TableRows] = {}
    for table in tables:
        keys = columns.keys[table]
        match = match_columns(table, pack, columns, generated)
        golden = {identity(r, match): ":".join(r[k] for k in keys)
                  for r in _case_rows(pack, table)}
        out[table] = TableRows(table, keys, match, golden)
    return out
```

### Task 3: Cells, anchors, difficulty groups, scope, pooling and readings (spec v3.1 §2.2–§2.4, §2.7, §3.2)

**Files:**
- Modify: `specdx/infer/cells.py` (header, imports and constants; part 2 appended)
- Modify: `tests/infer/test_cells.py` (imports; part 2 appended)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's `normalise`, `table_rows`, `TableRows`; `specdx.infer.items.SpecLens` (`steps`, `relevant`, `clauses`, `columns`, `acted_steps(b)`, `violations(b)`); `specdx.spec.clauses` (`GOLDEN_TABLES`, `Clause`, `evaluate`, `ClauseSet.applies`); `specdx.spec.lifting` (`column_type`, `lift_value`).
- Produces (in `specdx/infer/cells.py`):
  - `PRESENT = "present"`, `SATISFIED = "satisfied"`, `VIOLATED = "violated"`
  - `CONSTRAINT_KINDS: frozenset[str]` = invariant, expectation, blank, reconcile, guard_pay, step
  - `lift_reading(value: str, ctype: str, scenario: Scenario, anchor: str) -> str`
  - `Cell(id: str, kind: str, anchor: str | None, clause: str | None, location: str, group: str, steps: tuple[str, ...])` (frozen). `kind` ∈ value, event, constraint. Ids: a pooled lifted golden cell or a constraint cell is its clause id; any other cell is `"{scenario}|{table}:{row}:{column}"` or `"{scenario}|evt:{kind}:{ref}:{actor}"`. `group` is the difficulty group g(c) of spec v3.1 §3.2: `table.column` for a value cell, `evt:KIND` for an event cell, the clause id for a constraint cell (resolution 5).
  - `Reading(cell: str, run_key: str, model: str, family: str, scenario: str, value: str, clean: bool = False, variant: int = 0)` (frozen)
  - `CellData(cells: dict[str, Cell], readings: list[Reading])` with `.by_cell() -> dict[str, list[Reading]]`
  - `draft_tables(pack: CanonPack) -> dict[str, frozenset[str]]`
  - `CellLens(lens: SpecLens, pack: CanonPack, corpus: TellCorpus)` with `.rows: dict[str, TableRows]`, `.scope(table: str, key: str) -> tuple[str, ...]`, `.value_columns(table: str) -> list[str]`, `.extract(behaviours: Sequence[Behaviour]) -> CellData` (readings sorted by cell then run key).

```python
# Part 2 (spec v3 §2.2–§2.4, §2.7): cells, anchors, scope, pooling, readings.

GRN12 = Scenario("grn12", 15, 12, 0)
HAPPY = Scenario("happy", 15, 15, 0)
BIG = Scenario("o16-r16-d0-g1", 16, 16, 0)


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def cell_lens(pack, tell_corpus) -> CellLens:
    steps = load_work_orders()
    background, _ = split_background(golden_tables(pack))
    lens = SpecLens(ClauseSet.from_pack(pack, steps), steps, ColumnModel.of(pack),
                    drafting=drafting_steps(pack, steps), initial=background)
    return CellLens(lens, pack, tell_corpus)


def _run(key: str, model: str, scenario: Scenario, edit=None, idle=()) -> Behaviour:
    """A behaviour whose end state is the canon's golden state (every case row written, so
    it acts at every interpreter step but those in idle), after edit(tables)."""
    tables = {t: [dict(r) for r in rows] for t, rows in golden_tables(CanonPack(BASE_PACK)).items()}
    if edit is not None:
        edit(tables)
    steps = {s.id: ("error" if s.id in idle else "done") for s in load_work_orders()}
    return Behaviour(key, InterpreterId(model, model[0], 0, 1), scenario,
                     State(tables, {"fn_reconcile:PO-0015": "Cần kiểm"}, steps))


def _set(table: str, column: str, value: str, where=None):
    def edit(tables):
        for row in tables[table]:
            if where is None or all(row[k] == v for k, v in where.items()):
                row[column] = value
    return edit


def _values(data: CellData, cell: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {r.run_key: r.value for r in data.by_cell()[cell]}


def test_a_golden_value_is_anchored_where_its_clause_applies_and_unanchored_elsewhere(
    cell_lens,
) -> None:
    data = cell_lens.extract([
        _run("a", "m1", GRN12), _run("b", "m2", GRN12, _set("so", "trang_thai", "Draft")),
        _run("c", "m1", HAPPY)])
    anchored = data.cells["grn12|so:SO-0031:trang_thai"]
    assert (anchored.kind, anchored.anchor, anchored.clause, anchored.location) == (
        "value", "chờ duyệt", "golden:so:SO-0031:trang_thai", "so.trang_thai")
    assert anchored.group == "so.trang_thai"  # difficulty per column, over rows and scenarios
    assert anchored.steps == ("DE-SM-02:draft_quote",)
    assert _values(data, anchored.id) == {"a": "chờ duyệt", "b": "draft"}
    elsewhere = data.cells["happy|so:SO-0031:trang_thai"]
    assert elsewhere.anchor is None and elsewhere.clause is None


def test_a_lifted_golden_cell_is_one_cell_across_scenarios_in_the_lifted_vocabulary(
    cell_lens,
) -> None:
    data = cell_lens.extract([
        _run("a", "m1", GRN12),                                        # 15 = ordered
        _run("b", "m1", HAPPY),                                        # 15 = ordered = received
        _run("c", "m1", BIG, _set("po", "sl_dat", "16")),              # 16 = ordered
        _run("d", "m2", GRN12, _set("po", "sl_dat", "12")),            # 12 = received
        _run("e", "m2", BIG, _set("po", "sl_dat", "15")),              # no parameter
        _run("f", "m2", HAPPY, lambda t: t.update(po=[]))])            # no row
    cell = data.cells["golden:po:PO-0015:sl_dat"]
    assert cell.anchor == "{ordered}" and cell.clause == cell.id
    assert _values(data, cell.id) == {"a": "{ordered}", "b": "{ordered}", "c": "{ordered}",
                                      "d": "{received}", "e": "15", "f": "absent"}
    assert not [c for c in data.cells if c.endswith("|po:PO-0015:sl_dat")]


def test_lift_reading_keeps_text_and_prefers_the_anchor_on_a_tie() -> None:
    assert lift_reading("15", "int", HAPPY, "{ordered}") == "{ordered}"
    assert lift_reading("15", "int", HAPPY, "{received}") == "{received}"
    assert lift_reading("12", "int", GRN12, "{ordered}") == "{received}"
    assert lift_reading("7", "int", GRN12, "{ordered}") == "7"
    assert lift_reading("absent", "int", GRN12, "{ordered}") == "absent"
    assert lift_reading("draft", "text", GRN12, "{ordered}") == "draft"


def test_cell_scope_excludes_rows_inherited_through_a_shared_slice(cell_lens) -> None:
    # Only P6:decide_po acted: its slice holds the whole po table, but P6 drafts nothing,
    # so it reads no po value cell; po-15 is relevant to it, so it reads that clause.
    drafting = [s.id for s in load_work_orders() if s.kind in ("de", "persona")
                and s.id != "P6:decide_po"]
    data = cell_lens.extract([_run("a", "m1", GRN12, idle=drafting)])
    assert {r.cell for r in data.readings if ":PO-0015:" in r.cell} == set()
    assert "inv:po-15" in {r.cell for r in data.readings}
    assert data.cells["grn12|apr:APR-0155:trang_thai"].steps == (
        "DE-FN-04:draft_purchase_order", "P6:decide_po")


def test_soline_cells_follow_the_order_and_item_not_the_surrogate_id(cell_lens) -> None:
    def invented(tables):
        for i, row in enumerate(tables["soline"]):
            row["ma_soline"] = f"SOL-{i + 1:03d}"
        tables["soline"][1]["sl"] = "14"

    data = cell_lens.extract([_run("a", "m1", GRN12, invented)])
    assert _values(data, "grn12|soline:SOLINE-0031-1:sl") == {"a": "20"}
    assert _values(data, "golden:soline:SOLINE-0031-2:sl") == {"a": "14"}
    # key and matching columns are row identity, not readings (§2.6, resolution 3)
    assert not [c for c in data.cells
                if "soline:" in c and c.rsplit(":", 1)[1] in ("ma_soline", "so", "ma_sp")]
    assert [c for c in data.cells if c.startswith("grn12|soline:")]


def test_an_unmatched_row_gives_unanchored_cells(cell_lens) -> None:
    def extra(tables):
        tables["po"].append({**tables["po"][0], "ma_po": "PO-0016", "sl_dat": "5"})

    data = cell_lens.extract([_run("a", "m1", GRN12, extra), _run("b", "m2", GRN12)])
    cell = data.cells["grn12|po:po-0016:sl_dat"]
    assert cell.anchor is None and cell.location == "po.sl_dat"
    assert _values(data, cell.id) == {"a": "5", "b": "absent"}


def test_events_read_present_or_absent_and_a_golden_event_is_anchored(cell_lens) -> None:
    def logged(tables):
        tables["evt"].append({**tables["evt"][0], "actor": "DE-SM-02"})

    def dropped(tables):
        tables["evt"] = [e for e in tables["evt"] if e["kind"] != "DRAFTED"]

    data = cell_lens.extract([_run("a", "m1", GRN12, logged), _run("b", "m2", GRN12, dropped)])
    golden = data.cells["grn12|evt:DRAFTED:SO-0031:AI"]
    assert (golden.kind, golden.anchor, golden.clause, golden.location, golden.group) == (
        "event", "present", "event:DRAFTED:SO-0031:AI", "evt:DRAFTED:SO-0031", "evt:DRAFTED")
    assert _values(data, golden.id) == {"a": "present", "b": "absent"}
    observed = data.cells["grn12|evt:DRAFTED:SO-0031:DE-SM-02"]
    assert observed.anchor is None and _values(data, observed.id) == {"a": "present",
                                                                       "b": "absent"}


def test_constraint_cells_are_pooled_and_read_once_per_behaviour(cell_lens) -> None:
    data = cell_lens.extract([_run("a", "m1", GRN12), _run("b", "m1", HAPPY),
                              _run("c", "m2", HAPPY, _set("po", "sl_dat", "9"))])
    cell = data.cells["inv:po-15"]
    assert (cell.kind, cell.anchor, cell.location, cell.group) == (
        "constraint", "satisfied", "inv:po-15", "inv:po-15")  # difficulty per clause
    assert _values(data, "inv:po-15") == {"a": "satisfied", "b": "satisfied", "c": "violated"}
    assert all(c.kind != "constraint" or c.id == c.clause for c in data.cells.values())
    assert not [c for c in data.cells if c.startswith("vocab:")]


def test_only_acted_behaviours_read_and_clean_marks_a_violation_free_item(cell_lens) -> None:
    every = [s.id for s in load_work_orders()]
    data = cell_lens.extract([
        _run("idle", "m1", GRN12, idle=every),
        _run("ok", "m1", GRN12),
        _run("bad", "m2", GRN12, _set("so", "trang_thai", "Draft"))])
    assert {r.run_key for r in data.readings} == {"ok", "bad"}
    clean = {r.run_key: r.clean for r in data.by_cell()["grn12|so:SO-0031:trang_thai"]}
    assert clean == {"ok": True, "bad": False}


def test_extraction_does_not_depend_on_the_order_of_behaviours(cell_lens) -> None:
    runs = [_run("a", "m1", GRN12), _run("b", "m2", HAPPY, _set("so", "trang_thai", "x")),
            _run("c", "m2", GRN12, _set("po", "sl_dat", "12"))]
    assert cell_lens.extract(runs) == cell_lens.extract(runs[::-1])
```

<!-- apply: replace specdx/infer/cells.py -->
```python
A cell is one (table, row, column) value, one golden event, or one cross-cutting clause,
read at the items whose step may write it. This module holds the syntactic normalisation
of readings (§2.5) and the matching of rows by natural key (§2.6).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import unicodedata
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal

from tonomyx.canon.pack import CanonPack

from specdx.db import golden_tables, is_background
from specdx.spec.columns import ColumnClass, ColumnModel
from specdx.spec.tell import TellCorpus

ABSENT = "absent"
_NUMERAL = re.compile(r"^[+-]?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?$")
_TRUE = frozenset({"t", "true", "1"})
```

with:

```python
A cell is one (table, row, column) value, one golden event, or one cross-cutting clause,
read at the items whose step may write it. Each carries the specification's own declared
value as its anchor, when C declares one. Only behaviours that acted at an in-scope item
read a cell, and each reads it at most once.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import unicodedata
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal

from tonomyx.canon.pack import CanonPack

from specdx.db import golden_tables, is_background
from specdx.infer.items import SpecLens
from specdx.spec.clauses import GOLDEN_TABLES, Clause, evaluate
from specdx.spec.columns import ColumnClass, ColumnModel
from specdx.spec.lifting import column_type, lift_value
from specdx.spec.tell import TellCorpus
from specdx.types import Behaviour, Scenario

ABSENT = "absent"
PRESENT = "present"
SATISFIED = "satisfied"
VIOLATED = "violated"
# The cross-cutting clauses read as constraint cells (§2.2). Golden values and events are
# value cells; the event vocabulary is relevant to no step (items._relevant).
CONSTRAINT_KINDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {"invariant", "expectation", "blank", "reconcile", "guard_pay", "step"})
_PLACEHOLDER = re.compile(r"^\{([a-z_]+)\}$")
_NUMERAL = re.compile(r"^[+-]?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?$")
_TRUE = frozenset({"t", "true", "1"})
```

<!-- apply: append specdx/infer/cells.py -->
```python
def lift_reading(value: str, ctype: str, scenario: Scenario, anchor: str) -> str:
    """§2.7: a pooled cell's reading in the lifted vocabulary: the placeholder lift_value
    gives in this scenario, else the text. When the value equals several parameters at once
    (happy and discount8 order and receive 15), lift_value gives none; the reading is then
    the anchor's own placeholder if the anchor's parameter is among them, so "equals this
    scenario's value of the anchor's parameter" is one reading in every scenario."""
    if value == ABSENT:
        return value
    placeholder = lift_value(value, ctype, scenario)
    if placeholder is not None:
        return placeholder
    m = _PLACEHOLDER.match(anchor)
    if (m and _NUMERAL.match(value) and "." not in value
            and int(value) == scenario.params().get(m.group(1))):
        return anchor
    return value


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Cell:
    """kind: value, event or constraint. anchor: d_c, normalised (a lifted placeholder for a
    pooled value cell), or None when C declares nothing. clause: the clause d_c comes from,
    the location of a hidden finding and the target of its refutation. location: the
    ambiguity and weak-check location (table.column, evt:KIND:REF, or the clause id).
    group: the difficulty group g(c) of v3.1 §3.2: a value cell's column (pooled over rows
    and scenarios), an event cell's kind (evt:KIND), a constraint cell's clause. steps: the
    in-scope interpreter steps, in work-order order."""

    id: str
    kind: str
    anchor: str | None
    clause: str | None
    location: str
    group: str
    steps: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Reading:
    """One acted behaviour's reading of one cell. clean: the behaviour violates no clause
    relevant at some in-scope item where it acted (the weak-check test of §5.4)."""

    cell: str
    run_key: str
    model: str
    family: str
    scenario: str
    value: str
    clean: bool = False
    variant: int = 0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CellData:
    cells: dict[str, Cell]
    readings: list[Reading]

    def by_cell(self) -> dict[str, list[Reading]]:
        out: dict[str, list[Reading]] = {c: [] for c in self.cells}
        for r in self.readings:
            out[r.cell].append(r)
        return out


def draft_tables(pack: CanonPack) -> dict[str, frozenset[str]]:
    """Each digital employee's draft scope (draft_tables in de_scope.csv)."""
    return {r["ma_de"]: frozenset(t for t in r["draft_tables"].split("|") if t)
            for r in pack.tables.get("de_scope", [])}


# One acted behaviour of a scenario: the behaviour, the steps where it acted, and the acted
# steps where it violates no relevant clause.
_Acted = tuple[Behaviour, frozenset[str], frozenset[str]]


class CellLens:
    """Extracts cells and readings from behaviours, on top of the v2 lens (items, acts,
    relevance) and the column model. corpus: the specification's own T, which decides the
    executor-generated keys."""

    def __init__(self, lens: SpecLens, pack: CanonPack, corpus: TellCorpus) -> None:
        self.lens, self.pack, self.clauses = lens, pack, lens.clauses
        self.columns = lens.columns
        self.rows = table_rows(pack, self.columns, corpus,
                               [t for t in GOLDEN_TABLES if t in self.columns.keys])
        drafts = draft_tables(pack)
        self.drafters: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
        for step in lens.steps:
            for table in sorted(drafts.get(step.actor, frozenset())):
                self.drafters[table] = (*self.drafters.get(table, ()), step.id)
        self.golden: dict[tuple[str, str, str], Clause] = {}
        self.events: dict[tuple[str, str, str], Clause] = {}
        for c in self.clauses:
            if c.kind == "golden":
                ident = ":".join(str(v) for v in c.payload["key"].values())
                self.golden[(c.payload["table"], ident, c.payload["column"])] = c
            elif c.kind == "golden_event":
                self.events[_triple(*c.payload["event"])] = c
        self.constraints = [c for c in self.clauses if c.kind in CONSTRAINT_KINDS]
        self.constraint_steps = {
            c.id: tuple(s.id for s in lens.steps if c.id in lens.relevant[s.id])
            for c in self.constraints}

    def scope(self, table: str, key: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        """§2.4: the steps that may write the row. A drafted table is written by the steps
        whose actor drafts it (draft_tables). A table no step drafts (apr, evt) is written
        through tools; its row is written by the steps whose slice keys it (apr=APR-0102,
        evt=SO-0031)."""
        if table in self.drafters:
            return self.drafters[table]
        wanted = normalise(key)
        return tuple(s.id for s in self.lens.steps
                     if any(e.partition("=")[0] == table and e.partition("=")[2]
                            and normalise(e.partition("=")[2]) == wanted for e in s.slice))

    def value_columns(self, table: str) -> list[str]:
        """The LOCKED columns that are read: neither the key nor the matching columns."""
        rows = self.rows[table]
        return [c.name for c in self.pack.columns[table]
                if self.columns.classes[(table, c.name)] is ColumnClass.LOCKED
                and c.name not in rows.keys and c.name not in rows.match]

    def extract(self, behaviours: Sequence[Behaviour]) -> CellData:
        groups: dict[str, list[_Acted]] = {}
        for b in sorted(behaviours, key=lambda x: x.run_key):
            if b.status != "ok":
                continue
            acted = frozenset(s.id for s in self.lens.acted_steps(b))
            if not acted:
                continue
            bad = self.lens.violations(b)
            clean = frozenset(s for s in acted if not bad & self.lens.relevant[s])
            groups.setdefault(b.scenario.id, []).append((b, acted, clean))
        cells: dict[str, Cell] = {}
        readings: list[Reading] = []
        for sid in sorted(groups):
            self._values(groups[sid], cells, readings)
            self._events(groups[sid], cells, readings)
        self._constraints(groups, cells, readings)
        readings.sort(key=lambda r: (r.cell, r.run_key))
        return CellData(dict(sorted(cells.items())), readings)

    def _values(self, group: list[_Acted], cells: dict[str, Cell],
                readings: list[Reading]) -> None:
        scenario = group[0][0].scenario
        for table, rows in sorted(self.rows.items()):
            names: dict[str, bool] = {name: True for name in rows.golden.values()}
            held: list[dict[str, list[Mapping[str, str]]]] = []
            for b, _, _ in group:
                mine: dict[str, list[Mapping[str, str]]] = {}
                for row in b.state.tables.get(table, []):
                    if is_background(table, row):
                        continue
                    name, gold = rows.name(row)
                    mine.setdefault(name, []).append(row)
                    names.setdefault(name, gold)
                held.append(mine)
            for name in sorted(names):
                steps = self.scope(table, name)
                if not steps:
                    continue
                for col in self.value_columns(table):
                    ctype = column_type(self.pack, table, col)
                    clause = self.golden.get((table, name, col)) if names[name] else None
                    if clause is not None and not self.clauses.applies(clause, scenario):
                        clause = None
                    lifted = clause is not None and clause.scope == "lifted"
                    anchor = None if clause is None else normalise(
                        str(clause.payload["value"]), ctype)
                    cell = Cell(
                        clause.id if lifted and clause is not None
                        else f"{scenario.id}|{table}:{name}:{col}",
                        "value", anchor, None if clause is None else clause.id,
                        f"{table}.{col}", f"{table}.{col}", steps)
                    for (b, acted, clean), mine in zip(group, held, strict=True):
                        here = acted.intersection(steps)
                        if not here:
                            continue
                        found = mine.get(name, [])
                        value = (min(normalise(str(r.get(col, "")), ctype) for r in found)
                                 if found else ABSENT)
                        if lifted and anchor is not None:
                            value = lift_reading(value, ctype, scenario, anchor)
                        readings.append(_reading(cell.id, b, value, bool(here & clean)))
                        cells[cell.id] = cell

    def _events(self, group: list[_Acted], cells: dict[str, Cell],
                readings: list[Reading]) -> None:
        scenario = group[0][0].scenario
        shown: dict[tuple[str, str, str], tuple[str, str, str]] = {}
        for t, c in self.events.items():
            kind, ref, actor = c.payload["event"]
            shown[t] = (str(kind), str(ref), str(actor))
        held: list[frozenset[tuple[str, str, str]]] = []
        for b, _, _ in group:
            mine = set()
            for e in b.state.tables.get("evt", []):
                raw = (str(e.get("kind", "")), str(e.get("ref", "")), str(e.get("actor", "")))
                t = _triple(*raw)
                mine.add(t)
                shown[t] = min(shown.get(t, raw), raw)
            held.append(frozenset(mine))
        for t in sorted(shown):
            kind, ref, actor = shown[t]
            steps = self.scope("evt", ref)
            if not steps:
                continue
            clause = self.events.get(t)
            if clause is not None and not self.clauses.applies(clause, scenario):
                clause = None
            cell = Cell(f"{scenario.id}|evt:{kind}:{ref}:{actor}", "event",
                        None if clause is None else PRESENT,
                        None if clause is None else clause.id, f"evt:{kind}:{ref}",
                        f"evt:{kind}", steps)
            for (b, acted, clean), mine in zip(group, held, strict=True):
                here = acted.intersection(steps)
                if here:
                    value = PRESENT if t in mine else ABSENT
                    readings.append(_reading(cell.id, b, value, bool(here & clean)))
                    cells[cell.id] = cell

    def _constraints(self, groups: Mapping[str, list[_Acted]], cells: dict[str, Cell],
                     readings: list[Reading]) -> None:
        for clause in self.constraints:
            steps = self.constraint_steps[clause.id]
            if not steps:
                continue
            for sid in sorted(groups):
                for b, acted, clean in groups[sid]:
                    here = acted.intersection(steps)
                    if not here or not self.clauses.applies(clause, b.scenario):
                        continue
                    verdict = evaluate(clause, b.state, b.scenario, self.clauses.master)
                    if verdict is None:
                        continue
                    readings.append(_reading(clause.id, b, SATISFIED if verdict else VIOLATED,
                                             bool(here & clean)))
                    cells[clause.id] = Cell(clause.id, "constraint", SATISFIED, clause.id,
                                            clause.id, clause.id, steps)


def _triple(kind: str, ref: str, actor: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    return normalise(str(kind)), normalise(str(ref)), normalise(str(actor))


def _reading(cell: str, b: Behaviour, value: str, clean: bool) -> Reading:
    i = b.interpreter
    return Reading(cell, b.run_key, i.model, i.family, b.scenario.id, value, clean, i.variant)
```

### Task 4: The anchored model, part 1: sets, prior, likelihood, E-step (spec v3.1 §3.1–§3.3)

**Files:**
- Create: `specdx/infer/anchored.py` (part 1)
- Create: `tests/infer/test_anchored.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3's `Cell`, `Reading`.
- Produces (in `specdx/infer/anchored.py`):
  - `K_MAX = 6`, `OTHER = "__other__"`, `EM_TOL = 1e-6`, `EM_MAX_ITER = 200`, `KAPPA = 10.0` (fixed by spec v3.1 §3.3, not a G0 parameter), `FORMS = ("none", "mis", "amb", "empty")`
  - `SETS: tuple[tuple[int, ...], ...]` (22 sets over value slots 0–5, ∅ last), `EMPTY` (its index)
  - the marked block `# --- G0 frozen parameters: begin/end ---` holding `PARAMS_FROZEN = False` and `V3Params(pi, s_mis, s_amb, s_empty, beta_sd, mu_alpha, sigma_alpha, tau_h, tau_a, tau_w)` (frozen dataclass, provisional defaults; `beta_sd` is τ, the prior spread of the group difficulties)
  - `candidate_values(anchor: str | None, observed: Sequence[str], k_max: int = K_MAX) -> tuple[str, ...]`
  - `set_log_prior(n: int, anchored: bool, params: V3Params) -> np.ndarray` (log mass per set, −inf for unusable sets)
  - `CellPosterior(cell, anchor, values, sets: dict[frozenset[str], float], beta: float, error: dict[str, float])` with properties `forms`, `map_set`, `p_defect` (P(A_c ≠ {d_c}), 0 when unanchored), `p_pair`
  - `cell_posterior(cell: Cell, values: tuple[str, ...], readings: Sequence[Reading], *, alpha: Mapping[str, float], beta: float, error: Mapping[str, float], params: V3Params) -> CellPosterior`
  - private, used by Task 5: `_Batch` (`cells`, `values`, `counts[c, slot, model]`, `keys`, `logprior`, `support`, `groups`: each cell's difficulty-group index), `_encode`, `_likelihoods`, `_log_joint(batch, lik) -> np.ndarray` (log prior plus the readings' log-likelihood per cell and set, `[C, S]`; the E-step and Task 5's `_log_posterior` share it, ruling F5), `_e_step` (returns the set posterior `[C, S]` and the faithful probabilities `r[c, slot, model]`, given β per cell), `_posterior`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Every test passes explicit parameters. The posterior test computes §3.2's likelihood by hand. The T2′ test (spec v3.1 §11) shows one model of known accuracy separating all four forms when the errors sit on the flat pool.

<!-- apply: create tests/infer/test_anchored.py -->
```python
"""The anchored cell model (spec v3 §3). Every test passes its parameters explicitly: the
defaults are provisional until G0 chooses them."""

import math

import numpy as np
import pytest

from specdx.infer.anchored import (
    EMPTY,
    OTHER,
    SETS,
    V3Params,
    candidate_values,
    cell_posterior,
    set_log_prior,
)
from specdx.infer.cells import Cell, Reading

P = V3Params(pi=0.2, s_mis=0.5, s_amb=0.3, s_empty=0.2, beta_sd=1.0,
             mu_alpha=0.0, sigma_alpha=1.5, tau_h=0.5, tau_a=0.5, tau_w=0.8)
ANCHORED = Cell("c", "value", "a", "golden:t:K:c", "t.c", "t.c", ("step",))
UNANCHORED = Cell("u", "value", None, None, "t.u", "t.u", ("step",))


def _r(cell: str, model: str, value: str, i: int) -> Reading:
    return Reading(cell, f"{model}|{value}|{i}", model, model, "s", value)


def _sigmoid(x: float) -> float:
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-x))


def test_candidate_values_put_the_anchor_first_then_frequency_then_the_string() -> None:
    assert candidate_values("a", ["c", "b", "c", "b", "d"]) == ("a", "b", "c", "d")
    assert candidate_values(None, ["c", "b", "c"]) == ("c", "b")
    many = [f"v{i}" for i in range(9) for _ in range(9 - i)]
    assert candidate_values("a", many) == ("a", "v0", "v1", "v2", "v3", "v4")


def test_there_are_22_candidate_sets_with_the_empty_set_last() -> None:
    assert len(SETS) == 22 and SETS[EMPTY] == ()
    assert sum(len(s) == 1 for s in SETS) == 6 and sum(len(s) == 2 for s in SETS) == 15


def test_the_anchored_prior_splits_pi_over_the_forms_uniformly_within_each() -> None:
    prior = np.exp(set_log_prior(4, True, P))
    by = {s: prior[i] for i, s in enumerate(SETS)}
    assert by[(0,)] == pytest.approx(0.8)
    assert by[(1,)] == by[(3,)] == pytest.approx(0.2 * 0.5 / 3)
    assert by[(0, 1)] == by[(2, 3)] == pytest.approx(0.2 * 0.3 / 6)
    assert by[()] == pytest.approx(0.2 * 0.2)
    assert by[(4,)] == by[(0, 5)] == 0.0 and prior.sum() == pytest.approx(1.0)
    alone = np.exp(set_log_prior(1, True, P))
    assert alone[0] == pytest.approx(0.8 / (0.8 + 0.04))
    assert alone[EMPTY] == pytest.approx(0.04 / (0.8 + 0.04))


def test_the_unanchored_prior_gives_the_single_values_one_minus_pi() -> None:
    prior = np.exp(set_log_prior(3, False, P))
    by = {s: prior[i] for i, s in enumerate(SETS)}
    assert by[(0,)] == by[(2,)] == pytest.approx(0.8 / 3)
    assert by[(0, 2)] == pytest.approx(0.2 * (0.3 / 0.5) / 3)
    assert by[()] == pytest.approx(0.2 * (0.2 / 0.5))
    assert prior.sum() == pytest.approx(1.0)


def test_the_posterior_at_fixed_parameters_is_the_hand_computed_one() -> None:
    readings = [_r("c", "m1", "a", 0), _r("c", "m2", "b", 1), _r("c", "m2", "z", 2)]
    error = {"a": 0.2, "b": 0.3, OTHER: 0.5}
    post = cell_posterior(ANCHORED, ("a", "b"), readings, alpha={"m1": 1.0, "m2": 0.0},
                          beta=0.0, error=error, params=P)
    p1, p2 = _sigmoid(1.0), 0.5
    lik = {
        frozenset({"a"}): p1 * ((1 - p2) * 0.3 / 0.8) * ((1 - p2) * 0.5 / 0.8),
        frozenset({"b"}): ((1 - p1) * 0.2 / 0.7) * p2 * ((1 - p2) * 0.5 / 0.7),
        frozenset({"a", "b"}): (p1 / 2) * (p2 / 2) * ((1 - p2) * 0.5 / 0.5),
        frozenset(): 0.2 * 0.3 * 0.5,
    }
    prior = {frozenset({"a"}): 0.8, frozenset({"b"}): 0.1, frozenset({"a", "b"}): 0.06,
             frozenset(): 0.04}
    z = sum(prior[s] * lik[s] for s in lik)
    assert set(post.sets) == set(lik)  # OTHER is never in a set
    for s in lik:
        assert post.sets[s] == pytest.approx(prior[s] * lik[s] / z, rel=1e-12)
    assert post.p_defect == pytest.approx(1 - post.sets[frozenset({"a"})])
    assert post.forms["amb"] == pytest.approx(post.sets[frozenset({"a", "b"})])


def test_readings_that_all_agree_on_another_value_are_a_mis_key_not_silence() -> None:
    readings = [_r("c", m, "b", i) for m in ("m1", "m2") for i in range(10)]
    post = cell_posterior(ANCHORED, ("a", "b"), readings, alpha={"m1": 2.0, "m2": 0.0},
                          beta=0.0, error={"a": 1 / 3, "b": 1 / 3, OTHER: 1 / 3}, params=P)
    assert post.map_set == {"b"} and post.forms["mis"] > 0.99
    assert post.forms["mis"] > post.forms["empty"]


def test_readings_that_are_all_distinct_put_the_defect_mass_on_silence() -> None:
    readings = [_r("c", "m1", f"c{i}", i) for i in (1, 2, 3)] + [
        _r("c", "m2", f"c{i}", i) for i in (4, 5, 6)]
    values = candidate_values("a", [r.value for r in readings])
    assert values == ("a", "c1", "c2", "c3", "c4", "c5")
    error = dict.fromkeys((*values, OTHER), 1 / 7)
    post = cell_posterior(ANCHORED, values, readings, alpha={"m1": 2.0, "m2": 0.0}, beta=0.0,
                          error=error, params=P)
    assert post.forms["empty"] > 0.5 and post.map_set == frozenset()
    assert max(p for s, p in post.sets.items() if len(s) == 1 and "a" not in s) < 0.1


def test_one_model_separates_all_four_forms_when_the_error_pool_is_flat() -> None:
    # Theorem T2′: with ε = u flat and the model's accuracy known (p = 0.9), each form has its
    # own reading distribution, so a single model needs no accuracy contrast.
    values = ("a", "x", "b", "c")  # the anchor a first; z falls in OTHER
    flat = dict.fromkeys((*values, OTHER), 0.2)
    shapes = {"none": {"a": 36, "x": 1, "b": 1, "c": 1, "z": 1},
              "mis": {"x": 36, "a": 1, "b": 1, "c": 1, "z": 1},
              "amb": {"a": 18, "x": 18, "b": 2, "c": 1, "z": 1},
              "empty": {"a": 8, "x": 8, "b": 8, "c": 8, "z": 8}}
    for form, counts in shapes.items():
        readings = [_r("c", "m1", v, i) for v, n in counts.items() for i in range(n)]
        post = cell_posterior(ANCHORED, values, readings, alpha={"m1": math.log(9.0)}, beta=0.0,
                              error=flat, params=P)
        assert max(post.forms, key=lambda f: post.forms[f]) == form, (form, post.forms)


def test_an_unanchored_cell_has_no_defect_mass_and_counts_single_values_as_none() -> None:
    readings = [_r("u", "m1", "x", i) for i in range(4)]
    post = cell_posterior(UNANCHORED, ("x",), readings, alpha={"m1": 1.0}, beta=0.0,
                          error={"x": 0.5, OTHER: 0.5}, params=P)
    assert post.p_defect == 0.0 and post.map_set == {"x"}
    assert post.forms["mis"] == 0.0 and post.forms["none"] > 0.5

```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

A reading's likelihood depends only on its value slot and its model, so readings enter as counts per (cell, slot, model): no padding, and no per-reading dimension in the E-step.

<!-- apply: create specdx/infer/anchored.py -->
```python
"""The anchored cell model (spec v3.1 §3): each cell's latent entailment set A_c.

A reading of model m at cell c falls in A_c with probability p_{m,c} = σ(α_m − β_{g(c)}),
uniformly over A_c, where the difficulty β is shared by the cell's group g(c) (its value
column, or its clause). Otherwise the reading is drawn from the cell's error distribution
ε_c restricted to the values outside A_c, and ε_c is pulled towards the flat pool u_c. When
A_c = ∅ every reading comes from ε_c. θ is accuracy, so error mass never sits on the truth;
and since silence predicts near-flat readings, a sharp split identifies a pair and a sharp
mode a value, from one model (Theorem T2′).

Computation is vectorised over cells: each cell's candidate values occupy value slots
0..K_MAX-1 (slot 0 is d_c on an anchored cell) and slot K_MAX is the OTHER bucket.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from itertools import combinations

import numpy as np

from specdx.infer.cells import Cell, Reading

K_MAX = 6  # spec v1 §3.10, kept by v3 §9
OTHER = "__other__"
EM_TOL = 1e-6  # spec v3 §3.4
EM_MAX_ITER = 200
KAPPA = 10.0  # spec v3.1 §3.3: ε_c ~ Dirichlet(κ·u_c), u_c flat over V_c; fixed, not G0's
FORMS: tuple[str, ...] = ("none", "mis", "amb", "empty")
# Every candidate set over the value slots: each single value, each pair, and ∅ last
# (6 + 15 + 1 = 22). OTHER (slot K_MAX) is never in a set.
SETS: tuple[tuple[int, ...], ...] = (
    *((i,) for i in range(K_MAX)), *combinations(range(K_MAX), 2), ())
EMPTY = len(SETS) - 1
_IN = np.array([[k in s for k in range(K_MAX + 1)] for s in SETS])
_SIZE = np.array([len(s) for s in SETS], dtype=float)


# --- G0 frozen parameters: begin ---
PARAMS_FROZEN = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class V3Params:
    """PROVISIONAL defaults, not chosen by G0 (spec v3.1 §9). Task 13 of the P2a plan
    replaces this block with the values G0's selection rule picks. Until then every test
    whose outcome depends on a value passes its parameters explicitly."""

    pi: float = 0.1  # π: prior mass of the defect forms on an anchored cell
    s_mis: float = 0.5  # form shares s_mis + s_amb + s_empty = 1
    s_amb: float = 0.25
    s_empty: float = 0.25
    beta_sd: float = 1.0  # τ of §3.3: β_g ~ N(0, τ²) per difficulty group
    mu_alpha: float = 0.0  # α_m ~ N(μ_α, σ_α²)
    sigma_alpha: float = 1.5
    tau_h: float = 0.5  # hidden candidate: P(A_c ≠ {d_c}) ≥ τ_H
    tau_a: float = 0.5  # ambiguity: P(|A_c| = 2) ≥ τ_A
    tau_w: float = 0.8  # weak check: MAP set {x} with posterior ≥ τ_W
# --- G0 frozen parameters: end ---


def candidate_values(
    anchor: str | None, observed: Sequence[str], k_max: int = K_MAX
) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """§3.1: d_c first when anchored, then the most frequent observed values, ties broken by
    the normalised string, up to k_max in all. Every other value falls in OTHER."""
    counts = Counter(observed)
    ranked = sorted(counts, key=lambda v: (-counts[v], v))
    head = [anchor] if anchor is not None else []
    return tuple([*head, *(v for v in ranked if v != anchor)][:k_max])


def set_log_prior(n: int, anchored: bool, params: V3Params) -> np.ndarray:
    """§3.1's prior over SETS for a cell with n candidate values: log mass, −inf for a set
    that uses an empty slot. Anchored: {d_c} gets 1 − π and each defect form f gets π·s_f,
    uniform over its sets. Unanchored: the single values share 1 − π, the pairs and ∅ share
    π in the ratio s_amb : s_∅. A form with no set (one value has no pair) is dropped and
    the rest renormalised."""
    pairs = n * (n - 1) // 2
    rest = params.s_amb + params.s_empty
    weights = np.zeros(len(SETS))
    for i, s in enumerate(SETS):
        if any(k >= n for k in s):
            continue
        if anchored:
            if s == (0,):
                weights[i] = 1 - params.pi
            elif len(s) == 1:
                weights[i] = params.pi * params.s_mis / (n - 1)
            elif len(s) == 2:
                weights[i] = params.pi * params.s_amb / pairs
            else:
                weights[i] = params.pi * params.s_empty
        elif len(s) == 1:
            weights[i] = (1 - params.pi) / n
        elif rest > 0:
            share = params.s_amb / pairs if len(s) == 2 else params.s_empty
            weights[i] = params.pi * share / rest
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore"):
        return np.log(weights / weights.sum())


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CellPosterior:
    """values: V_c without OTHER (d_c first when anchored). sets: the posterior over the
    candidate sets, ∅ as frozenset(). error: ε_c over the values and OTHER."""

    cell: str
    anchor: str | None
    values: tuple[str, ...]
    sets: dict[frozenset[str], float]
    beta: float
    error: dict[str, float]

    @property
    def forms(self) -> dict[str, float]:
        """§3.5's form masses: none ({d_c}), mis (another single value), amb (a pair), empty
        (∅). On an unanchored cell every single value counts as none."""
        out = dict.fromkeys(FORMS, 0.0)
        for s, p in self.sets.items():
            if not s:
                out["empty"] += p
            elif len(s) == 2:
                out["amb"] += p
            elif self.anchor is None or s == {self.anchor}:
                out["none"] += p
            else:
                out["mis"] += p
        return out

    @property
    def map_set(self) -> frozenset[str]:
        return max(self.sets, key=lambda s: (self.sets[s], -len(s), sorted(s)))

    @property
    def p_defect(self) -> float:
        """P(A_c ≠ {d_c}); 0 on an unanchored cell, which C does not declare."""
        if self.anchor is None:
            return 0.0
        return 1.0 - self.sets.get(frozenset({self.anchor}), 0.0)

    @property
    def p_pair(self) -> float:
        return self.forms["amb"]


@dataclass
class _Batch:
    """Cells and their readings as arrays. A reading's likelihood depends only on its value
    slot and its model, so the readings enter as counts[c, k, m]: model m's readings of
    cell c that fall in slot k (K_MAX = OTHER). keys: each cell's readings as (run key,
    slot, model index), to give each behaviour its faithful probabilities back. groups: each
    cell's difficulty group (Cell.group), as an index."""

    cells: list[Cell]
    values: list[tuple[str, ...]]
    counts: np.ndarray
    keys: list[list[tuple[str, int, int]]]
    logprior: np.ndarray
    support: np.ndarray
    groups: np.ndarray


def _encode(
    cells: Sequence[Cell], by_cell: Mapping[str, Sequence[Reading]], models: Sequence[str],
    params: V3Params, values: Mapping[str, tuple[str, ...]] | None = None,
) -> _Batch:
    index = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(models)}
    counts = np.zeros((len(cells), K_MAX + 1, len(models)))
    logprior = np.zeros((len(cells), len(SETS)))
    support = np.zeros((len(cells), K_MAX + 1), dtype=bool)
    vals: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []
    keys: list[list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = []
    for c, cell in enumerate(cells):
        mine = by_cell[cell.id]
        v = (values or {}).get(cell.id) or candidate_values(cell.anchor, [r.value for r in mine])
        slot = {value: k for k, value in enumerate(v)}
        own = [(r.run_key, slot.get(r.value, K_MAX), index[r.model]) for r in mine]
        for _, k, m in own:
            counts[c, k, m] += 1
        logprior[c] = set_log_prior(len(v), cell.anchor is not None, params)
        support[c, :len(v)] = True
        support[c, K_MAX] = True
        vals.append(v)
        keys.append(own)
    names = sorted({cell.group for cell in cells})
    groups = np.array([names.index(cell.group) for cell in cells], dtype=int)
    return _Batch(list(cells), vals, counts, keys, logprior, support, groups)


def _likelihoods(batch: _Batch, alpha: np.ndarray, beta: np.ndarray,
                 eps: np.ndarray) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """§3.2 for every cell c, set s, value slot k and model m: the faithful part of
    P(y = k | A = s) and the whole of it."""
    p = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-(alpha[None, :] - beta[:, None])))
    inside = _IN[None, :, :, None]
    nonempty = (_SIZE > 0)[None, :, None, None]
    faith = np.where(inside & nonempty,
                     p[:, None, None, :] / np.maximum(_SIZE, 1.0)[None, :, None, None], 0.0)
    outside = 1.0 - eps @ _IN.T.astype(float)
    scale = np.where(nonempty, 1.0 - p[:, None, None, :], 1.0)
    err = np.where(inside, 0.0, scale * eps[:, None, :, None] / outside[:, :, None, None])
    return faith, faith + err


def _log_joint(batch: _Batch, lik: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """log P(A_c = A) + Σ over the cell's readings of log P(y | A), as [c, A]: −inf where a
    set cannot produce a reading. The E-step and the EM starts' comparison share it."""
    read = batch.counts[:, None, :, :] > 0
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        loglik = np.where(read, batch.counts[:, None, :, :] * np.log(lik), 0.0).sum(axis=(2, 3))
    return batch.logprior + loglik


def _e_step(
    batch: _Batch, alpha: np.ndarray, beta: np.ndarray, eps: np.ndarray
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """§3.4's E-step: the posterior over SETS per cell, and the faithful probability
    r = Σ_A post(A)·P(faithful | y, A) of a reading in slot k by model m, as r[c, k, m]."""
    faith, lik = _likelihoods(batch, alpha, beta, eps)
    logpost = _log_joint(batch, lik)
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        top = logpost.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        post = np.exp(logpost - top)
        post /= post.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        share = np.where(lik > 0, faith / lik, 0.0)
    r = (post[:, :, None, None] * share).sum(axis=1)
    return post, r


def _posterior(batch: _Batch, c: int, post: np.ndarray, beta: float,
               eps: np.ndarray) -> CellPosterior:
    v = batch.values[c]
    sets = {frozenset(v[k] for k in s): float(post[c, i])
            for i, s in enumerate(SETS) if all(k < len(v) for k in s)}
    error = {value: float(eps[c, k]) for k, value in enumerate(v)} | {OTHER: float(eps[c, K_MAX])}
    cell = batch.cells[c]
    return CellPosterior(cell.id, cell.anchor, v, sets, beta, error)


def cell_posterior(
    cell: Cell, values: tuple[str, ...], readings: Sequence[Reading], *,
    alpha: Mapping[str, float], beta: float, error: Mapping[str, float], params: V3Params,
) -> CellPosterior:
    """The posterior over A_c at fixed parameters: the E-step for one cell. values: V_c
    (readings outside it are OTHER); error: ε_c over values and OTHER. A model absent from
    alpha reads at the prior mean μ_α."""
    models = sorted({r.model for r in readings} | set(alpha))
    batch = _encode([cell], {cell.id: readings}, models, params, {cell.id: values})
    a = np.array([alpha.get(name, params.mu_alpha) for name in models])
    eps = np.zeros((1, K_MAX + 1))
    for k, value in enumerate(values):
        eps[0, k] = error.get(value, 0.0)
    eps[0, K_MAX] = error.get(OTHER, 0.0)
    eps /= eps.sum()
    post, _ = _e_step(batch, a, np.array([beta]), eps)
    return _posterior(batch, 0, post, beta, eps)

```

### Task 5: The anchored model, part 2: M-step, grouped difficulty, multi-start EM, outputs (spec v3.1 §3.2–§3.5)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4's `_Batch` (with `groups`), `_encode`, `_e_step`, `_log_joint`, `_posterior`, `_likelihoods`, `cell_posterior`, `V3Params`, `EMPTY`, `KAPPA`.
- Produces:
  - `STARTS = (0.0, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0)` (spec v3.1 §3.4's multi-start, with T3's median start)
  - `_flat(batch)`: the flat pool u_c over each cell's slots, where ε_c starts and towards which the ε M-step pulls with κ = 10; β is fitted per difficulty group (one β for all cells sharing `Cell.group`), and each cell's `CellPosterior.beta` is its group's
  - `AnchoredFit(alpha: dict[str, float], theta: dict[str, float], family_effect: dict[str, float], cells: dict[str, CellPosterior], weights: dict[str, float], converged: bool, iterations: int, params: V3Params, start: str = "t3", starts: dict[str, float] = {})`. `weights` holds w_b for the behaviours that read a cell; one that read none is absent, and consumers default it to 0 (ruling F9).
  - `_log_posterior(batch, alpha, beta_groups, eps, prior, params) -> float`: the starts' comparison, through Task 4's `_log_joint`
  - `fit_anchored(cells: Mapping[str, Cell], readings: Sequence[Reading], *, models: Mapping[str, str], params: V3Params, tol: float = EM_TOL, max_iter: int = EM_MAX_ITER) -> AnchoredFit`
  - `posterior_at(fit: AnchoredFit, cell: Cell, readings: Sequence[Reading]) -> CellPosterior` (α, β_c, ε_c and V_c held at their fitted values; the ddmin oracle's test)
