```python
# Part 2 (spec v3 §3.4–§3.5): the fit.

ALPHAS = {"m1": 2.0, "m2": 1.0, "m3": 0.0}
FIT = V3Params(pi=0.2, s_mis=0.4, s_amb=0.3, s_empty=0.3, beta_sd=1.0,
               mu_alpha=1.0, sigma_alpha=1.5, tau_h=0.5, tau_a=0.5, tau_w=0.8)
PLANTED = {"none": ("d",), "mis": ("a",), "amb": ("d", "a"), "empty": ()}
VALUES = ("d", "a", "b", "c", "e")


def _world(seed: int, columns: dict[str, int], reads: int = 24, per_column: int = 4):
    """The v3.1 §3.2 model at T2′'s known pool: columns of anchored cells (one per scenario)
    share a planted form and a difficulty β_g ~ N(0, 0.5²); errors fall on the flat pool u;
    each model reads each cell `reads` times."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cells: dict[str, Cell] = {}
    readings: list[Reading] = []
    truth: dict[str, str] = {}
    plan = [form for form, n in columns.items() for _ in range(n)]
    for col, form in enumerate(plan):
        admitted = PLANTED[form]
        beta = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5)
        for s in range(per_column):
            c = col * per_column + s
            cell = Cell(f"c{c:03d}", "value", "d", f"golden:t:{c}:x", f"t.x{col}",
                        f"t.x{col}", ("s",))
            cells[cell.id], truth[cell.id] = cell, form
            eps = np.full(len(VALUES), 1.0 / len(VALUES))
            outside = np.array([0.0 if v in admitted else e
                                for v, e in zip(VALUES, eps, strict=True)])
            for m, a in ALPHAS.items():
                p = 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-(a - beta)))
                for i in range(reads):
                    if admitted and rng.random() < p:
                        y = admitted[int(rng.integers(len(admitted)))]
                    else:
                        y = VALUES[int(rng.choice(len(VALUES), p=outside / outside.sum()))]
                    readings.append(Reading(cell.id, f"{m}|{c}|{i}", m, m, "s", y))
    return cells, readings, truth


def _form(fit, cell: str) -> str:
    forms = fit.cells[cell].forms
    return max(forms, key=lambda f: forms[f])


def test_the_fit_recovers_accuracy_and_all_four_forms() -> None:
    hits: dict[str, list[bool]] = {form: [] for form in PLANTED}
    for seed in (0, 1, 2):  # a form belongs to a column, so one seed's errors come in fours
        cells, readings, truth = _world(seed, {"none": 30, "mis": 3, "amb": 3, "empty": 3})
        fit = fit_anchored(cells, readings, models={m: m for m in ALPHAS}, params=FIT)
        assert fit.converged
        for m, a in ALPHAS.items():
            assert abs(fit.theta[m] - 1 / (1 + math.exp(-a))) <= 0.05, (m, fit.theta[m])
        for c, form in truth.items():
            hits[form].append(_form(fit, c) == form)
    for form, got in hits.items():
        assert sum(got) / len(got) >= 0.8, (form, sum(got), len(got))


def test_the_fit_is_deterministic_and_ignores_the_order_of_readings() -> None:
    cells, readings, _ = _world(3, {"none": 8, "mis": 1, "amb": 1, "empty": 1}, reads=6)
    one = fit_anchored(cells, readings, models={m: m for m in ALPHAS}, params=FIT)
    two = fit_anchored(cells, readings[::-1], models={m: m for m in ALPHAS}, params=FIT)
    assert one == two


def test_theta_does_not_collapse_when_most_anchored_cells_are_read_alike_off_the_anchor() -> None:
    # Shaped after the P1b smoke run: most anchored cells are rows no model writes (every
    # reading absent), the rest agree with their anchors. T3's median start sits below
    # the MAP; the fit keeps the start with the highest log posterior.
    cells: dict[str, Cell] = {}
    readings: list[Reading] = []
    for c in range(40):
        cell = Cell(f"c{c:02d}", "value", "d", f"golden:t:{c}:x", "t.x", "t.x", ("s",))
        cells[cell.id] = cell
        for m in ("gpt", "qwen"):
            for i in range(9):
                value = "absent" if c < 26 else ("d" if i < 8 else f"e{i}")
                readings.append(Reading(cell.id, f"{m}|{i}", m, m, "s", value))
    fit = fit_anchored(cells, readings, models={"gpt": "gpt", "qwen": "qwen"}, params=FIT)
    assert min(fit.theta.values()) >= 0.5
    assert fit.start != "t3" and fit.starts[fit.start] > fit.starts["t3"]
    assert all(fit.cells[f"c{c:02d}"].p_defect > 0.9 for c in range(26))


def test_non_convergence_is_reported() -> None:
    cells, readings, _ = _world(5, {"none": 3, "mis": 1}, reads=6)
    fit = fit_anchored(cells, readings, models={m: m for m in ALPHAS}, params=FIT, max_iter=1)
    assert not fit.converged and fit.iterations == 1


def test_weights_family_effect_and_a_model_without_readings() -> None:
    cells, readings, _ = _world(11, {"none": 5, "mis": 1}, reads=6)
    families = {"m1": "f", "m2": "f", "m3": "g", "idle": "g"}
    readings = [dataclasses.replace(r, family=families[r.model]) for r in readings]
    fit = fit_anchored(cells, readings, models=families, params=FIT)
    assert fit.alpha["idle"] == pytest.approx(FIT.mu_alpha)
    assert fit.family_effect["f"] == pytest.approx(
        (fit.alpha["m1"] + fit.alpha["m2"]) / 2 - sum(fit.alpha.values()) / 4)
    assert set(fit.weights) == {r.run_key for r in readings}
    assert all(0.0 <= w <= 1.0 for w in fit.weights.values())
    mean = {m: np.mean([w for k, w in fit.weights.items() if k.startswith(m)]) for m in ALPHAS}
    assert mean["m1"] > mean["m3"]


def test_an_empty_input_gives_an_empty_converged_fit() -> None:
    fit = fit_anchored({}, [], models={"m1": "f"}, params=FIT)
    assert fit.cells == {} and fit.converged and fit.iterations == 0
    assert fit.theta["m1"] == pytest.approx(1 / (1 + math.exp(-FIT.mu_alpha)))


def test_posterior_at_the_fitted_parameters_reproduces_the_fit() -> None:
    cells, readings, _ = _world(13, {"none": 3, "amb": 1}, reads=8)
    fit = fit_anchored(cells, readings, models={m: m for m in ALPHAS}, params=FIT)
    cell = cells["c012"]
    again = posterior_at(fit, cell, [r for r in readings if r.cell == cell.id])
    for s, p in fit.cells[cell.id].sets.items():
        assert again.sets[s] == pytest.approx(p, abs=1e-9)
```

```python
_NEWTON_TOL = 1e-10
_NEWTON_MAX = 50
# EM starts besides T3's median: one common α for every model, on the logit scale. On the
# base data more than half of the anchored cells are read alike by every model and differ
# from d_c (apr case rows no tool writes; golden event actors no interpreter writes), past
# T3's breakdown point, and EM from T3's start alone stops ~200 nats below the MAP.
STARTS: tuple[float, ...] = (0.0, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0)


@dataclass
class AnchoredFit:
    """§3.5's outputs. alpha and theta = σ(α) per model (θ: the accuracy at a cell of
    median difficulty); family_effect: each family's mean logit θ minus the population's
    (v2 §3.3); cells: the per-cell posteriors; weights: w_b per run key, the mean faithful
    probability over the cells the behaviour read; a behaviour that read no cell is absent,
    and consumers default it to 0. converged is False
    when EM stopped at max_iter (§3.4: reported, never silent). start: the EM start whose
    fixed point has the highest log posterior (t3, or a common α such as "alpha=2.0");
    starts: every start's final log posterior."""

    alpha: dict[str, float]
    theta: dict[str, float]
    family_effect: dict[str, float]
    cells: dict[str, CellPosterior]
    weights: dict[str, float]
    converged: bool
    iterations: int
    params: V3Params
    start: str = "t3"
    starts: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)


def _flat(batch: _Batch) -> np.ndarray:
    """u_c, the flat pool over each cell's slots (its candidate values and OTHER). Value
    labels mean nothing across columns, so a flat profile is the only pool beyond the
    column; the strong pull keeps ∅ from fitting a sharp split or mode (v3.1 §3.3)."""
    flat = batch.support.astype(float)
    return flat / flat.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)


def _t3_alpha(batch: _Batch, params: V3Params) -> np.ndarray:
    """Theorem T3's median estimator: α_m = median over the anchored cells m read of
    logit P̂_{m,c}(d_c), with P̂ = (hits + ½)/(n + 1) so no logit is infinite; μ_α for a
    model that read no anchored cell."""
    anchored = np.array([cell.anchor is not None for cell in batch.cells], dtype=bool)
    n = batch.counts.sum(axis=1)
    share = (batch.counts[:, 0, :] + 0.5) / (n + 1.0)
    out = []
    for m in range(batch.counts.shape[2]):
        mine = anchored & (n[:, m] > 0)
        logits = np.log(share[mine, m] / (1 - share[mine, m]))
        out.append(float(np.median(logits)) if mine.any() else params.mu_alpha)
    return np.array(out)


def _objective(alpha: np.ndarray, beta: np.ndarray, rsum: np.ndarray, trials: np.ndarray,
               params: V3Params) -> float:
    eta = alpha[None, :] - beta[:, None]
    fit = (rsum * eta - trials * np.logaddexp(0.0, eta)).sum()
    prior = (((alpha - params.mu_alpha) ** 2).sum() / (2 * params.sigma_alpha ** 2)
             + (beta ** 2).sum() / (2 * params.beta_sd ** 2))
    return float(fit - prior)


def _newton(rsum: np.ndarray, trials: np.ndarray, alpha: np.ndarray, beta: np.ndarray,
            params: V3Params) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """§3.4's M-step for α and β: the penalised logistic objective with the faithful
    probabilities as fractional successes. rsum[g, m] sums them over model m's readings of
    the cells of difficulty group g; trials[g, m] counts those readings weighted by each
    cell's q_c = P(A_c ≠ ∅), since under ∅ no reading is a trial of accuracy. Newton steps
    through the Schur complement of the diagonal β block, halved while they would lower the
    objective."""
    for _ in range(_NEWTON_MAX):
        p = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-(alpha[None, :] - beta[:, None])))
        resid = rsum - trials * p
        w = trials * p * (1 - p)
        g_a = resid.sum(axis=0) - (alpha - params.mu_alpha) / params.sigma_alpha ** 2
        g_b = -resid.sum(axis=1) - beta / params.beta_sd ** 2
        h_aa = -w.sum(axis=0) - 1 / params.sigma_alpha ** 2
        h_bb = -w.sum(axis=1) - 1 / params.beta_sd ** 2
        h_ab = w.T
        schur = np.diag(h_aa) - (h_ab / h_bb) @ h_ab.T
        d_a = np.linalg.solve(schur, -g_a + (h_ab / h_bb) @ g_b)
        d_b = (-g_b - h_ab.T @ d_a) / h_bb
        before = _objective(alpha, beta, rsum, trials, params)
        t = 1.0
        while t > 1e-8 and _objective(alpha + t * d_a, beta + t * d_b, rsum, trials,
                                      params) < before - 1e-12:
            t /= 2
        alpha, beta = alpha + t * d_a, beta + t * d_b
        if max(float(np.max(np.abs(t * d_a), initial=0.0)),
               float(np.max(np.abs(t * d_b), initial=0.0))) < _NEWTON_TOL:
            break
    return alpha, beta


def _error_update(batch: _Batch, r: np.ndarray, prior: np.ndarray,
                  params: V3Params) -> np.ndarray:
    """§3.4's M-step for ε_c: the expected error counts, smoothed by the Dirichlet prior
    κ·u_c. The mode of Dirichlet(1 + κ·u + counts), which keeps every value's error mass
    positive (the literal mode of Dirichlet(κ·u) is 0 wherever κ·u + count < 1)."""
    counts = (batch.counts * (1.0 - r)).sum(axis=2)
    out = (counts + KAPPA * prior) * batch.support
    return out / out.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)


def _log_posterior(batch: _Batch, alpha: np.ndarray, beta: np.ndarray, eps: np.ndarray,
                   prior: np.ndarray, params: V3Params) -> float:
    """log P(readings | α, β, ε) with every A_c summed out, plus the log priors of α, β (one
    per group) and ε (up to constants): what the EM starts are compared on."""
    _, lik = _likelihoods(batch, alpha, beta[batch.groups], eps)
    joint = _log_joint(batch, lik)
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        top = joint.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        marginal = float((top[:, 0] + np.log(np.exp(joint - top).sum(axis=1))).sum())
        dirichlet = float(np.where(batch.support, KAPPA * prior * np.log(eps), 0.0).sum())
    return (marginal + dirichlet
            - float(((alpha - params.mu_alpha) ** 2).sum()) / (2 * params.sigma_alpha ** 2)
            - float((beta ** 2).sum()) / (2 * params.beta_sd ** 2))


def _em(batch: _Batch, prior: np.ndarray, alpha: np.ndarray, params: V3Params, tol: float,
        max_iter: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray, bool, int]:
    """EM from one start, with every β_g = 0 and ε_c = u_c (§3.4): α, β per group, ε,
    whether the largest parameter change fell below tol, and the iterations used."""
    groups = int(batch.groups.max()) + 1
    beta = np.zeros(groups)
    eps = prior.copy()
    reads = batch.counts.sum(axis=1)
    for iterations in range(1, max_iter + 1):
        post, r = _e_step(batch, alpha, beta[batch.groups], eps)
        rsum = np.zeros((groups, len(alpha)))
        trials = np.zeros((groups, len(alpha)))
        np.add.at(rsum, batch.groups, (batch.counts * r).sum(axis=1))
        np.add.at(trials, batch.groups, (1.0 - post[:, EMPTY])[:, None] * reads)
        new_alpha, new_beta = _newton(rsum, trials, alpha, beta, params)
        new_eps = _error_update(batch, r, prior, params)
        moved = max(float(np.max(np.abs(new_alpha - alpha), initial=0.0)),
                    float(np.max(np.abs(new_beta - beta), initial=0.0)),
                    float(np.max(np.abs(new_eps - eps), initial=0.0)))
        alpha, beta, eps = new_alpha, new_beta, new_eps
        if moved < tol:
            return alpha, beta, eps, True, iterations
    return alpha, beta, eps, False, max_iter


def fit_anchored(
    cells: Mapping[str, Cell], readings: Sequence[Reading], *, models: Mapping[str, str],
    params: V3Params, tol: float = EM_TOL, max_iter: int = EM_MAX_ITER,
) -> AnchoredFit:
    """MAP by EM (§3.4), deterministic. EM runs from T3's median start and from each common
    α in STARTS (§3.4's multi-start), and the fixed point with the highest log posterior is
    kept (ties to the earlier start). models: model → family for the whole population; a
    model without readings keeps α = μ_α. Cells without readings are left out."""
    family = dict(models) | {r.model: r.family for r in readings}
    names = sorted(family)
    by_cell: dict[str, list[Reading]] = {}
    for r in sorted(readings, key=lambda x: (x.cell, x.run_key)):
        by_cell.setdefault(r.cell, []).append(r)
    used = [cells[c] for c in sorted(by_cell)]
    batch = _encode(used, by_cell, names, params)
    prior = _flat(batch)
    alpha = np.full(len(names), params.mu_alpha)
    beta, eps = np.zeros(len(set(batch.groups.tolist()))), prior
    converged, iterations, start = True, 0, "t3"
    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    if used:
        starts = [("t3", _t3_alpha(batch, params))] + [
            (f"alpha={a}", np.full(len(names), a)) for a in STARTS]
        best = -np.inf
        for label, alpha0 in starts:
            a, b, e, ok, n = _em(batch, prior, alpha0, params, tol, max_iter)
            scores[label] = _log_posterior(batch, a, b, e, prior, params)
            if scores[label] > best:
                best, start = scores[label], label
                alpha, beta, eps, converged, iterations = a, b, e, ok, n
    cell_beta = beta[batch.groups]
    post, r = _e_step(batch, alpha, cell_beta, eps)
    sums: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
    for c, keys in enumerate(batch.keys):
        for key, k, m in keys:
            sums.setdefault(key, []).append(float(r[c, k, m]))
    alphas = {name: float(alpha[i]) for i, name in enumerate(names)}
    mean = sum(alphas.values()) / len(alphas) if alphas else 0.0
    effect = {f: sum(alphas[m] for m in names if family[m] == f)
              / sum(1 for m in names if family[m] == f) - mean
              for f in sorted(set(family.values()))}
    return AnchoredFit(
        alphas, {m: float(1 / (1 + np.exp(-a))) for m, a in alphas.items()}, effect,
        {cell.id: _posterior(batch, c, post, float(cell_beta[c]), eps)
         for c, cell in enumerate(used)},
        {k: sum(v) / len(v) for k, v in sorted(sums.items())}, converged, iterations, params,
        start, scores)


def posterior_at(fit: AnchoredFit, cell: Cell, readings: Sequence[Reading]) -> CellPosterior:
    """P(A_c | readings) with α, β_{g(c)}, ε_c and V_c held at their fitted values: the
    ddmin oracle's test on re-run readings (§5.3)."""
    fitted = fit.cells[cell.id]
    return cell_posterior(cell, fitted.values, readings, alpha=fit.alpha, beta=fitted.beta,
                          error=fitted.error, params=fit.params)
```

### Task 6: The simulation generator (spec v3.1 §8.1)

**Files:**
- Create: `specdx/sim/__init__.py`, `specdx/sim/generator.py`
- Create: `tests/sim/__init__.py`, `tests/sim/test_generator.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3's `Cell`, `Reading`, `SATISFIED`, `VIOLATED`.
- Produces (in `specdx/sim/generator.py`):
  - `ARMS = ("base", "a3", "a5")`
  - `SimConfig(alphas: tuple[float, ...] = (2.0, 1.0), act: tuple[float, ...] = (0.9, 0.9), columns: int = 40, pooled_share: float = 0.3, scenarios: int = 4, variants: int = 3, seeds: int = 3, items: int = 10, beta_sd: float = 0.5, beta_within: float = 0.0, anchored_share: float = 0.7, constraint_share: float = 0.3, values: tuple[float, ...] = (0.0, 0.3, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1, 0.1), defect_rate: float = 0.15, forms: tuple[float, float, float] = (0.4, 0.3, 0.3), unanchored: tuple[float, float, float] = (0.5, 0.2, 0.3), error_conc: float = 1.0, kappa_true: float = 30.0, arm: str = "base", seed: int = 0)` (frozen). `values[i]` is P(|V| = i + 2). Each column draws one difficulty β_g ~ N(0, `beta_sd`²); a cell's β is β_g + `beta_within`·N(0, 1) (0: truly per group; 0.5: §8.1's within-group arm).
  - `Truth(form: str, admitted: tuple[str, ...], hidden: bool, ambiguity: bool, weak: bool)`
  - `SimSpec(config, cells: dict[str, Cell], readings: list[Reading], truth: dict[str, Truth], models: dict[str, str], alphas: dict[str, float], betas: dict[str, float])` with `.theta`
  - `generate(config: SimConfig) -> SimSpec` (models `m0`, `m1`, … in families `f0`, `f1`, …; cell ids `colNNN|sK` or `colNNN|pooled`; each cell's `group` is its column; its one step is its item `iK`; run keys `m|sS|vV|kK`). A column has one form, one anchored status and one β, its cells differing only by scenario: the single-form limitation that resolution 14 records (ruling F24).

```python
"""The simulation generator (spec v3 §8.1)."""

import math
from collections import Counter

import numpy as np

from specdx.sim.generator import SimConfig, generate

BIG = SimConfig(alphas=(2.0, 0.0), act=(1.0, 1.0), columns=200, defect_rate=0.3, seed=1)


def test_generation_is_deterministic_in_its_seed() -> None:
    assert generate(SimConfig(seed=4)).readings == generate(SimConfig(seed=4)).readings
    assert generate(SimConfig(seed=4)).readings != generate(SimConfig(seed=5)).readings


def test_each_behaviour_reads_a_cell_at_most_once_and_pooled_cells_span_every_scenario() -> None:
    spec = generate(SimConfig(act=(1.0, 1.0), seed=2))
    per = Counter((r.cell, r.run_key) for r in spec.readings)
    assert max(per.values()) == 1
    reads = Counter((r.cell, r.model) for r in spec.readings)
    for cell in spec.cells:
        expected = 9 * (4 if cell.endswith("|pooled") else 1)
        assert all(reads[(cell, m)] == expected for m in spec.models)


def test_act_rates_thin_the_readings() -> None:
    spec = generate(SimConfig(act=(1.0, 0.3), columns=100, seed=3))
    count = Counter(r.model for r in spec.readings)
    assert 0.2 < count["m1"] / count["m0"] < 0.4


def test_planted_truths_follow_the_definitions() -> None:
    spec = generate(BIG)
    for cid, t in spec.truth.items():
        cell = spec.cells[cid]
        assert t.hidden == (cell.anchor is not None and t.admitted != (cell.anchor,))
        assert t.ambiguity == (len(t.admitted) == 2)
        assert t.weak == (cell.anchor is None and len(t.admitted) == 1)
    forms = Counter(t.form for cid, t in spec.truth.items() if spec.cells[cid].anchor)
    assert forms["mis"] and forms["amb"] and forms["empty"] and forms["none"]
    columns = {}
    for cid, t in spec.truth.items():
        columns.setdefault(spec.cells[cid].group, set()).add(t)
    assert all(len(ts) == 1 for ts in columns.values())  # a form belongs to a column


def test_readings_follow_the_accuracy_model_at_non_defective_cells() -> None:
    spec = generate(BIG)
    for m, a in spec.alphas.items():
        hits, expected = [], []
        for cid, t in spec.truth.items():
            if t.form == "none" and spec.cells[cid].anchor is not None:
                mine = [r.value for r in spec.readings if r.cell == cid and r.model == m]
                hits += [v == spec.cells[cid].anchor for v in mine]
                expected += [1 / (1 + math.exp(-(a - spec.betas[cid])))] * len(mine)
        assert abs(np.mean(hits) - np.mean(expected)) < 0.03, m


def test_a_behaviour_is_clean_when_every_anchored_cell_of_its_item_reads_its_anchor() -> None:
    spec = generate(SimConfig(seed=6))
    by_item: dict[tuple[str, str], list] = {}
    for r in spec.readings:
        by_item.setdefault((r.run_key, spec.cells[r.cell].steps[0]), []).append(r)
    for rs in by_item.values():
        clean = all(r.value == spec.cells[r.cell].anchor for r in rs
                    if spec.cells[r.cell].anchor is not None)
        assert all(r.clean == clean for r in rs)


def test_the_a3_arm_gives_each_model_its_own_errors() -> None:
    base = generate(SimConfig(alphas=(0.0, 0.0), columns=60, seed=8))
    a3 = generate(SimConfig(alphas=(0.0, 0.0), columns=60, seed=8, arm="a3"))

    def disagreement(spec) -> float:
        out = []
        for cid, t in spec.truth.items():
            if t.form == "none" and spec.cells[cid].kind == "value":
                tops = []
                for m in spec.models:
                    errs = Counter(r.value for r in spec.readings
                                   if r.cell == cid and r.model == m and r.value not in t.admitted)
                    tops.append(errs.most_common(1)[0][0] if errs else None)
                out.append(tops[0] != tops[1])
        return float(np.mean(out))

    assert disagreement(a3) > disagreement(base)


def test_the_a5_arm_lets_the_strongest_model_guess_where_t_is_silent() -> None:
    config = SimConfig(alphas=(3.0, -1.0), act=(1.0, 1.0), columns=200, defect_rate=0.5,
                       forms=(0.0, 0.0, 1.0), seed=9)

    def top_share(spec, model: str) -> float:
        shares = []
        for cid, t in spec.truth.items():
            if t.form == "empty":
                mine = Counter(r.value for r in spec.readings if r.cell == cid and r.model == model)
                shares.append(mine.most_common(1)[0][1] / sum(mine.values()))
        return float(np.mean(shares))

    base, a5 = generate(config), generate(SimConfig(**{**config.__dict__, "arm": "a5"}))
    assert top_share(a5, "m0") > top_share(base, "m0") + 0.2
    assert abs(top_share(a5, "m1") - top_share(base, "m1")) < 0.1


def test_difficulty_is_one_per_column_unless_the_within_group_arm_spreads_it() -> None:
    def spread(beta_within: float) -> float:
        spec = generate(SimConfig(columns=60, beta_sd=1.0, beta_within=beta_within, seed=12))
        by_column: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
        for cid, beta in spec.betas.items():
            by_column.setdefault(spec.cells[cid].group, []).append(beta)
        return float(np.mean([np.std(b) for b in by_column.values() if len(b) > 1]))

    assert spread(0.0) == 0.0
    assert 0.3 < spread(0.5) < 0.7  # the within-group arm of spec v3.1 §8.1
```

<!-- apply: create specdx/sim/generator.py -->
```python
"""Synthetic specifications for the simulation study (spec v3.1 §8.1).

A synthetic specification is a set of columns of cells, value and constraint, anchored and
unanchored. A column is read either per scenario (one cell per scenario, as most golden
values are) or as one cell pooled over every scenario (as lifted and cross-cutting clauses
are), and all its cells share one planted form, because a defect is a property of a rule,
not of a scenario, and one difficulty β_g, as difficulty is grouped by column (v3.1 §3.2).
Columns are spread over items; a behaviour acts at an item with its model's act rate and
then reads every cell of the item in its scenario. Readings follow the §3.2 model with
known parameters, except in the two misspecification arms and the within-group arm.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np

from specdx.infer.cells import SATISFIED, VIOLATED, Cell, Reading

ARMS: tuple[str, ...] = ("base", "a3", "a5")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SimConfig:
    """alphas, act: per model, the true ability α_m and the act rate. values: P(|V| = k)
    for k = 2, 3, … on a value column (the truth and its error values). forms: the shares
    of mis, amb and empty among anchored defects. unanchored: the shares of a determined
    value (a weak-check truth), a pair and silence on unanchored columns. error_conc: the
    Dirichlet concentration of a column's error tendency (small: concentrated errors);
    kappa_true: a cell's ε_c ~ Dirichlet(kappa_true · tendency). arm: base, a3 (each model
    errs by its own distribution) or a5 (at silent cells each model guesses one value with
    its accuracy). beta_sd: τ_true, the spread of the group difficulties β_g; beta_within:
    each cell's difficulty spreads around its group's by this (0: difficulty truly per
    group; 0.5: the within-group arm of §8.1)."""

    alphas: tuple[float, ...] = (2.0, 1.0)
    act: tuple[float, ...] = (0.9, 0.9)
    columns: int = 40
    pooled_share: float = 0.3
    scenarios: int = 4
    variants: int = 3
    seeds: int = 3
    items: int = 10
    beta_sd: float = 0.5
    beta_within: float = 0.0
    anchored_share: float = 0.7
    constraint_share: float = 0.3
    values: tuple[float, ...] = (0.0, 0.3, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1, 0.1)
    defect_rate: float = 0.15
    forms: tuple[float, float, float] = (0.4, 0.3, 0.3)
    unanchored: tuple[float, float, float] = (0.5, 0.2, 0.3)
    error_conc: float = 1.0
    kappa_true: float = 30.0
    arm: str = "base"
    seed: int = 0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Truth:
    """A cell's planted truth: its form (none, mis, amb, empty) and admitted set, and the
    defect types it is a truth of (spec v1 §2.4 as §8.1 scores them)."""

    form: str
    admitted: tuple[str, ...]
    hidden: bool
    ambiguity: bool
    weak: bool


@dataclass
class SimSpec:
    config: SimConfig
    cells: dict[str, Cell]
    readings: list[Reading]
    truth: dict[str, Truth]
    models: dict[str, str]
    alphas: dict[str, float]
    betas: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)

    @property
    def theta(self) -> dict[str, float]:
        return {m: 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-a)) for m, a in self.alphas.items()}


def _vocabulary(rng: np.random.Generator, config: SimConfig, constraint: bool,
                anchored: bool) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    if constraint:
        return (SATISFIED, VIOLATED)
    sizes = np.array(config.values, dtype=float)
    k = int(rng.choice(len(sizes), p=sizes / sizes.sum())) + 2
    first = ["d"] if anchored else []
    return tuple(first + [f"v{i}" for i in range(k - len(first))])


def _plant(rng: np.random.Generator, config: SimConfig, vocab: tuple[str, ...],
           anchored: bool) -> tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]]:
    """A column's form and admitted set. Anchored: {d} unless a defect is planted; mis is
    {a}, amb is {d, a} or {a, a'} (ambiguous-pick, both kinds), empty is ∅. Unanchored: one
    value, a pair, or ∅."""
    d, a, a2 = vocab[0], vocab[1], vocab[2] if len(vocab) > 2 else vocab[1]
    if anchored:
        if rng.random() >= config.defect_rate:
            return "none", (d,)
        form = ("mis", "amb", "empty")[int(rng.choice(3, p=np.array(config.forms)))]
        if form == "mis":
            return form, (a,)
        if form == "amb":
            return form, (d, a) if rng.random() < 0.5 or a2 == a else (a, a2)
        return form, ()
    form = ("none", "amb", "empty")[int(rng.choice(3, p=np.array(config.unanchored)))]
    if form == "none":
        return form, (d,)
    return (form, (d, a)) if form == "amb" else (form, ())


def generate(config: SimConfig) -> SimSpec:
    """One synthetic specification, deterministic in config (seed included)."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(config.seed)
    models = {f"m{i}": f"f{i}" for i in range(len(config.alphas))}
    alphas = {f"m{i}": float(a) for i, a in enumerate(config.alphas)}
    strongest = max(alphas, key=lambda m: alphas[m])
    cells: dict[str, Cell] = {}
    truth: dict[str, Truth] = {}
    betas: dict[str, float] = {}
    errors: dict[str, dict[str, np.ndarray]] = {}
    guess: dict[str, str] = {}
    vocabs: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
    layout: dict[tuple[int, int], list[str]] = {}  # (item, scenario) → cells read there
    for col in range(config.columns):
        constraint = rng.random() < config.constraint_share
        anchored = constraint or rng.random() < config.anchored_share
        pooled = rng.random() < config.pooled_share
        vocab = _vocabulary(rng, config, constraint, anchored)
        form, admitted = _plant(rng, config, vocab, anchored)
        tendency = rng.dirichlet(np.full(len(vocab), config.error_conc))
        group_beta = float(rng.normal(0.0, config.beta_sd))
        item = col % config.items
        group = f"col{col:03d}"
        for s in ([-1] if pooled else range(config.scenarios)):
            cid = f"{group}|{'pooled' if pooled else f's{s}'}"
            cells[cid] = Cell(cid, "constraint" if constraint else "value",
                              vocab[0] if anchored else None,
                              f"clause:{cid}" if anchored else None, group, group, (f"i{item}",))
            truth[cid] = Truth(form, admitted, anchored and admitted != (vocab[0],),
                               len(admitted) == 2, not anchored and len(admitted) == 1)
            betas[cid] = group_beta + config.beta_within * float(rng.normal())
            # Floored: numpy's Dirichlet returns NaN for vanishing parameters.
            base = rng.dirichlet(np.maximum(config.kappa_true * tendency, 0.01))
            errors[cid] = {m: (rng.dirichlet(np.full(len(vocab), 0.3)) if config.arm == "a3"
                               else base) for m in models}
            guess[cid] = vocab[int(rng.integers(len(vocab)))]
            vocabs[cid] = vocab
            for scenario in (range(config.scenarios) if pooled else [s]):
                layout.setdefault((item, scenario), []).append(cid)
    readings: list[Reading] = []
    for m, a in alphas.items():
        act = config.act[int(m[1:])]
        for scenario in range(config.scenarios):
            for v in range(config.variants):
                for k in range(config.seeds):
                    key = f"{m}|s{scenario}|v{v}|k{k}"
                    for item in range(config.items):
                        if rng.random() >= act:
                            continue
                        mine: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
                        for cid in layout.get((item, scenario), []):
                            p = 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-(a - betas[cid])))
                            mine.append((cid, _read(rng, truth[cid].admitted, vocabs[cid],
                                                    errors[cid][m], p, guess[cid],
                                                    config.arm == "a5", m == strongest)))
                        clean = all(value == cells[cid].anchor for cid, value in mine
                                    if cells[cid].anchor is not None)
                        readings += [Reading(cid, key, m, models[m], f"s{scenario}", value,
                                             clean, v) for cid, value in mine]
    readings.sort(key=lambda r: (r.cell, r.run_key))
    return SimSpec(config, cells, readings, truth, models, alphas, betas)


def _read(rng: np.random.Generator, admitted: tuple[str, ...], vocab: tuple[str, ...],
          eps: np.ndarray, p: float, guess: str, a5: bool, strongest: bool) -> str:
    """One reading under §3.2. In the A5 arm, at a silent cell the strongest model reads
    one guessed value with its accuracy (domain knowledge where T says nothing)."""
    if admitted and rng.random() < p:
        return admitted[int(rng.integers(len(admitted)))]
    if not admitted and a5 and strongest and rng.random() < p:
        return guess
    outside = np.array([0.0 if x in admitted else e for x, e in zip(vocab, eps, strict=True)])
    if outside.sum() <= 0:
        # A pair that is the whole vocabulary (a constraint cell): no value is an error.
        return admitted[int(rng.integers(len(admitted)))]
    return vocab[int(rng.choice(len(vocab), p=outside / outside.sum()))]
```

### Task 7: The cell-level typing core (spec v3.1 §5.1, §5.3, §5.4)

**Files:**
- Create: `specdx/infer/typing.py` (part 1)
- Create: `tests/infer/test_typing.py` (part 1)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5's `AnchoredFit` (`alpha`, `cells`), Task 4's `CellPosterior`, `V3Params` (`tau_h`, `tau_a`, `tau_w`), Task 3's `Cell`, `Reading` (`clean`).
- Produces (in `specdx/infer/typing.py`), shared by the simulation (Task 8) and the real diagnosis (Task 9):
  - `MIS_KEY`, `AMBIGUOUS_PICK`, `UNSTATED`, `SUBTYPES`
  - `CellFinding(cell: str, type: str, score: float, subtype: str = "", values: tuple[str, ...] = (), witnesses: tuple[str, ...] = ())` (frozen; `type` ∈ hidden, ambiguity, weak)
  - `subtype(post) -> str` (the most probable defect form, for any number of models: T2′)
  - `hidden_finding(cell, post, readings, tau_h) -> CellFinding | None`
  - `ambiguity_finding(cell, post, readings, tau_a) -> CellFinding | None` (P(|A_c| = 2) ≥ τ_A and a pair exists; no rank-correlation rule and no accuracy-contrast gate, spec v3.1 §5.3)
  - `weak_finding(cell, post, readings, tau_w) -> CellFinding | None`
  - `co_occur(cell, post, readings, hidden, ambiguity) -> tuple[CellFinding | None, CellFinding | None]`: on an anchored cell an ambiguity and its hidden requirement are reported together (spec §3.1, §5.1, §5.3; ruling F1, resolution 11). A reported ambiguity brings its hidden candidate (subtype ambiguous-pick, score max(P(A_c ≠ {d_c}), P(|A_c| = 2))); a hidden candidate whose most probable defect form is a pair brings its ambiguity. An unanchored cell keeps the ambiguity alone.
  - `type_cells(cells: Mapping[str, Cell], fit: AnchoredFit, by_cell: Mapping[str, Sequence[Reading]], params: V3Params) -> list[CellFinding]` (cell id order; `co_occur` applied; a cell reporting an ambiguity reports no weak check)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

The posteriors are built by hand, so the tests pin the rules, not the fit. One-model cells are typed (resolution 11). The weak-suppression test uses a cell whose weak check passes τ_W on its own, so deleting the rule fails it (ruling F4); the co-occurrence test covers both directions of `co_occur` and the unanchored case (ruling F1).

<!-- apply: create tests/infer/test_typing.py -->
```python
"""The cell-level typing core (spec v3.1 §5.1, §5.3, §5.4), on hand-built posteriors."""

import pytest

from specdx.infer.anchored import AnchoredFit, CellPosterior, V3Params
from specdx.infer.cells import Cell, Reading
from specdx.infer.typing import (
    AMBIGUOUS_PICK,
    MIS_KEY,
    UNSTATED,
    ambiguity_finding,
    hidden_finding,
    type_cells,
    weak_finding,
)

P = V3Params(pi=0.1, s_mis=0.5, s_amb=0.25, s_empty=0.25, beta_sd=1.0,
             mu_alpha=0.0, sigma_alpha=1.5, tau_h=0.5, tau_a=0.5, tau_w=0.8)
ANCHORED = Cell("c", "value", "d", "golden:t:K:c", "t.c", "t.c", ("step",))
FREE = Cell("u", "value", None, None, "t.u", "t.u", ("step",))
RULE = Cell("inv:x", "constraint", "satisfied", "inv:x", "inv:x", "inv:x", ("step",))
F = frozenset


def _post(cell: Cell, sets: dict) -> CellPosterior:
    values = tuple(sorted({v for s in sets for v in s}))
    return CellPosterior(cell.id, cell.anchor, values, sets, 0.0, {})


def _fit(alpha: dict[str, float], *posts: CellPosterior) -> AnchoredFit:
    return AnchoredFit(alpha, {}, {}, {p.cell: p for p in posts}, {}, True, 1, P)


def _r(cell: str, model: str, value: str, i: int, clean: bool = False) -> Reading:
    return Reading(cell, f"{model}|{i}", model, model, "s", value, clean)


def test_a_hidden_candidate_needs_an_anchor_and_tau_h() -> None:
    post = _post(ANCHORED, {F({"d"}): 0.3, F({"a"}): 0.6, F(): 0.1})
    readings = [_r("c", "m1", "a", 0), _r("c", "m2", "a", 1), _r("c", "m2", "d", 2)]
    found = hidden_finding(ANCHORED, post, readings, 0.5)
    assert found is not None and found.score == pytest.approx(0.7)
    assert found.subtype == MIS_KEY and found.witnesses == ("m1|0", "m2|1")
    assert hidden_finding(ANCHORED, post, readings, 0.75) is None
    free = _post(FREE, {F({"a"}): 1.0})
    assert hidden_finding(FREE, free, readings, 0.5) is None


def test_the_subtype_follows_the_most_probable_defect_form_even_for_one_model() -> None:
    # Theorem T2′: with the flat error pool one model separates the forms, so the unstated
    # and ambiguous-pick subtypes need no second model.
    one = [_r("c", "m1", "a", 0), _r("c", "m1", "b", 1)]
    silent = _post(ANCHORED, {F({"d"}): 0.1, F({"a"}): 0.2, F(): 0.7})
    assert hidden_finding(ANCHORED, silent, one, 0.5).subtype == UNSTATED
    split = _post(ANCHORED, {F({"d"}): 0.1, F({"d", "a"}): 0.6, F(): 0.3})
    assert hidden_finding(ANCHORED, split, one, 0.5).subtype == AMBIGUOUS_PICK


def test_an_ambiguity_needs_tau_a_and_a_pair_but_no_accuracy_contrast() -> None:
    post = _post(ANCHORED, {F({"d"}): 0.2, F({"d", "a"}): 0.8})
    readings = [_r("c", "m1", "d", 0), _r("c", "m1", "a", 1), _r("c", "m2", "x", 2)]
    found = ambiguity_finding(ANCHORED, post, readings, 0.5)
    assert found is not None and found.values == ("a", "d") and found.score == pytest.approx(0.8)
    assert found.witnesses == ("m1|0", "m1|1")
    alone = [r for r in readings if r.model == "m1"]  # one model: still typed (T2′)
    assert ambiguity_finding(ANCHORED, post, alone, 0.5) is not None
    assert ambiguity_finding(ANCHORED, post, readings, 0.9) is None
    single = _post(ANCHORED, {F({"d"}): 0.9, F(): 0.1})  # one value: no pair to report
    assert ambiguity_finding(ANCHORED, single, readings, 0.0) is None


def test_a_weak_check_is_a_clean_deviant_from_a_confident_single_value() -> None:
    post = _post(FREE, {F({"x"}): 0.9, F(): 0.1})
    readings = [_r("u", "m1", "x", 0, True), _r("u", "m1", "y", 1, True),
                _r("u", "m2", "z", 2, False)]
    found = weak_finding(FREE, post, readings, 0.8)
    assert found is not None and found.values == ("x", "y") and found.witnesses == ("m1|1",)
    assert weak_finding(FREE, post, readings[::2], 0.8) is None  # the deviant is not clean
    assert weak_finding(FREE, post, readings, 0.95) is None
    pair = _post(FREE, {F({"x", "y"}): 0.9, F({"x"}): 0.1})
    assert weak_finding(FREE, pair, readings, 0.5) is None
    rule = _post(RULE, {F({"satisfied"}): 0.95, F(): 0.05})
    assert weak_finding(RULE, rule, [_r("inv:x", "m1", "violated", 0, True)], 0.8) is None


def _params(tau_h: float, tau_a: float, tau_w: float) -> V3Params:
    return V3Params(pi=0.1, s_mis=0.5, s_amb=0.25, s_empty=0.25, beta_sd=1.0,
                    mu_alpha=0.0, sigma_alpha=1.5, tau_h=tau_h, tau_a=tau_a, tau_w=tau_w)


def test_a_cell_reporting_an_ambiguity_reports_no_weak_check() -> None:
    # {x} at 0.55 passes τ_W = 0.5 and the pair at 0.45 passes τ_A = 0.4, with a clean
    # deviant reading y: only the ambiguity rule keeps the weak check out.
    amb = _post(FREE, {F({"x"}): 0.55, F({"x", "y"}): 0.45})
    hidden = _post(ANCHORED, {F({"a"}): 0.95, F({"d"}): 0.05})
    fit = _fit({"m1": 2.0, "m2": 0.0}, amb, hidden)
    by_cell = {"u": [_r("u", "m1", "x", 0, True), _r("u", "m2", "y", 1, True)],
               "c": [_r("c", "m1", "a", 2), _r("c", "m2", "a", 3)]}
    assert weak_finding(FREE, amb, by_cell["u"], 0.5) is not None
    found = type_cells({"u": FREE, "c": ANCHORED}, fit, by_cell, _params(0.5, 0.4, 0.5))
    assert [(f.cell, f.type) for f in found] == [("c", "hidden"), ("u", "ambiguity")]


def test_on_an_anchored_cell_an_ambiguity_and_its_hidden_requirement_co_occur() -> None:
    # §3.1, §5.1, §5.3 (ruling F1). "c": the pair passes τ_A but P(A ≠ {d}) misses τ_H.
    # "e": P(A ≠ {d}) passes τ_H with a pair as the most probable form; the pair misses τ_A.
    # "u": unanchored, so its ambiguity comes alone.
    other = Cell("e", "value", "d", "golden:t:K:e", "t.e", "t.e", ("step",))
    by_pair = _post(ANCHORED, {F({"d"}): 0.45, F({"d", "a"}): 0.4, F({"a"}): 0.15})
    by_form = _post(other, {F({"d"}): 0.05, F({"d", "a"}): 0.34, F({"a"}): 0.31, F(): 0.3})
    free = _post(FREE, {F({"x", "y"}): 0.9, F({"x"}): 0.1})
    by_cell = {"c": [_r("c", "m1", "a", 0), _r("c", "m1", "d", 1)],
               "e": [_r("e", "m1", "a", 2)], "u": [_r("u", "m1", "y", 3)]}
    found = type_cells({"c": ANCHORED, "e": other, "u": FREE},
                       _fit({"m1": 1.0}, by_pair, by_form, free), by_cell,
                       _params(0.9, 0.35, 0.9))
    assert [(f.cell, f.type) for f in found] == [
        ("c", "hidden"), ("c", "ambiguity"), ("e", "hidden"), ("e", "ambiguity"),
        ("u", "ambiguity")]
    hidden = {f.cell: f for f in found if f.type == "hidden"}
    assert hidden["c"].subtype == hidden["e"].subtype == AMBIGUOUS_PICK
    assert hidden["c"].score == pytest.approx(0.55) and hidden["c"].witnesses == ("m1|0",)
    assert hidden["e"].score == pytest.approx(0.95)
    assert type_cells({"c": ANCHORED}, _fit({"m1": 1.0}, by_pair), by_cell,
                      _params(0.9, 0.5, 0.9)) == []

```

<!-- apply: create specdx/infer/typing.py -->
```python
"""v3.1 typing (spec v3.1 §5).

The cell-level core turns a fit and its thresholds into cell findings: hidden candidates
(§5.1), ambiguities (§5.3) and weak checks (§5.4). The simulation scores these findings
directly; the real diagnosis refutes the hidden candidates and adds multi-clause
contradictions.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass

from specdx.infer.anchored import AnchoredFit, CellPosterior, V3Params
from specdx.infer.cells import Cell, Reading

MIS_KEY = "mis-key"
AMBIGUOUS_PICK = "ambiguous-pick"
UNSTATED = "unstated"
SUBTYPES = {"mis": MIS_KEY, "amb": AMBIGUOUS_PICK, "empty": UNSTATED}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CellFinding:
    """type: hidden, ambiguity or weak. score: P(A_c ≠ {d_c}), P(|A_c| = 2) or the MAP set's
    posterior. values: an ambiguity's pair, or a weak check's admitted value then the
    deviant values. witnesses: the run keys of the readings behind the finding."""

    cell: str
    type: str
    score: float
    subtype: str = ""
    values: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    witnesses: tuple[str, ...] = ()


def subtype(post: CellPosterior) -> str:
    """§5.1: the hidden finding's subtype follows the most probable defect form. With the
    flat error pool, one model separates the forms (Theorem T2′), so no accuracy contrast
    is needed."""
    forms = post.forms
    return SUBTYPES[max(("mis", "amb", "empty"), key=lambda f: forms[f])]


def hidden_finding(cell: Cell, post: CellPosterior, readings: Sequence[Reading],
                   tau_h: float) -> CellFinding | None:
    """§5.1's candidate: an anchored cell with P(A_c ≠ {d_c}) ≥ τ_H."""
    if cell.anchor is None or post.p_defect < tau_h:
        return None
    witnesses = tuple(sorted({r.run_key for r in readings if r.value != cell.anchor}))
    return CellFinding(cell.id, "hidden", post.p_defect, subtype(post), (), witnesses)


def ambiguity_finding(cell: Cell, post: CellPosterior, readings: Sequence[Reading],
                      tau_a: float) -> CellFinding | None:
    """§5.3: P(|A_c| = 2) ≥ τ_A. No accuracy contrast and no rank-correlation rule: with the
    flat error pool one model identifies a pair (Theorem T2′), and v3's Spearman rule passed
    at about 0% of true pairs (v3.1 §5.3). A cell with one candidate value has no pair."""
    pairs = [s for s in post.sets if len(s) == 2]
    if not pairs or post.p_pair < tau_a:
        return None
    pair = max(pairs, key=lambda s: (post.sets[s], sorted(s)))
    witnesses = tuple(sorted({r.run_key for r in readings if r.value in pair}))
    return CellFinding(cell.id, "ambiguity", post.p_pair, "", tuple(sorted(pair)), witnesses)


def weak_finding(cell: Cell, post: CellPosterior, readings: Sequence[Reading],
                 tau_w: float) -> CellFinding | None:
    """§5.4: a value or event cell whose MAP set is one value x with posterior ≥ τ_W, and
    an acted behaviour that read another value and violates no relevant clause."""
    if cell.kind == "constraint":
        return None
    best = post.map_set
    if len(best) != 1 or post.sets[best] < tau_w:
        return None
    x = next(iter(best))
    deviant = [r for r in readings if r.value != x and r.clean]
    if not deviant:
        return None
    values = (x, *sorted({r.value for r in deviant}))
    return CellFinding(cell.id, "weak", post.sets[best], "", values,
                       tuple(sorted({r.run_key for r in deviant})))


def co_occur(cell: Cell, post: CellPosterior, readings: Sequence[Reading],
             hidden: CellFinding | None,
             ambiguity: CellFinding | None) -> tuple[CellFinding | None, CellFinding | None]:
    """§3.1, §5.1, §5.3 (v1 §2.4): on an anchored cell an ambiguity and its hidden
    requirement are reported together (ruling F1, resolution 11). A reported ambiguity
    brings its hidden candidate, subtype ambiguous-pick, scored max(P(A_c ≠ {d_c}),
    P(|A_c| = 2)); a hidden candidate whose most probable defect form is a pair brings its
    ambiguity. An unanchored cell reports the ambiguity alone."""
    if cell.anchor is None:
        return hidden, ambiguity
    if ambiguity is None and hidden is not None and hidden.subtype == AMBIGUOUS_PICK:
        ambiguity = ambiguity_finding(cell, post, readings, 0.0)
    if ambiguity is not None:
        base = hidden or hidden_finding(cell, post, readings, 0.0)
        assert base is not None
        hidden = CellFinding(cell.id, "hidden", max(post.p_defect, post.p_pair),
                             AMBIGUOUS_PICK, (), base.witnesses)
    return hidden, ambiguity


def type_cells(cells: Mapping[str, Cell], fit: AnchoredFit,
               by_cell: Mapping[str, Sequence[Reading]], params: V3Params) -> list[CellFinding]:
    """Every cell finding of the fit, cell by cell in id order. An ambiguity and its hidden
    requirement co-occur on an anchored cell (`co_occur`). A cell reporting an ambiguity
    reports no weak check: its readings are not deviant."""
    out: list[CellFinding] = []
    for cid in sorted(fit.cells):
        cell, post, mine = cells[cid], fit.cells[cid], by_cell.get(cid, [])
        found, ambiguity = co_occur(cell, post, mine,
                                    hidden_finding(cell, post, mine, params.tau_h),
                                    ambiguity_finding(cell, post, mine, params.tau_a))
        if found is not None:
            out.append(found)
        if ambiguity is not None:
            out.append(ambiguity)
            continue
        weak = weak_finding(cell, post, mine, params.tau_w)
        if weak is not None:
            out.append(weak)
    return out

```

### Task 8: Simulation metrics, baselines, sweeps, the regime map and `specdx simulate` (spec v3.1 §8.1)

**Files:**
- Create: `specdx/sim/metrics.py`, `specdx/sim/baselines.py`, `specdx/sim/study.py`
- Modify: `specdx/cli.py` (the `simulate` subcommand)
- Create: `tests/sim/test_study.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6's `SimConfig`, `SimSpec`, `Truth`, `generate`; Task 5's `fit_anchored`, `AnchoredFit`; Task 7's `type_cells`; v2's `specdx.infer.latent.fit` and `Observation` (the stage-2 baseline).
- Produces:
  - `specdx/sim/metrics.py`: `TYPES = ("hidden", "ambiguity", "weak")`; `Counts(tp, fp, fn)` with `.add`, `.f1` (exact 2tp / (2tp + fp + fn)), `.as_dict()`; `typed_counts(predicted: Iterable[tuple[str, str]], truth) -> dict[str, Counts]`; `theta_error(estimated, true) -> float`; `calibration(pairs, bins=10) -> tuple[float, float]` (ECE, Brier)
  - `specdx/sim/baselines.py`: `by_cell(spec) -> dict[str, list[Reading]]` (every cell a key, readings in order; the one helper the baselines, the study and G0 share, ruling F7); `majority_vote(spec)`, `anchor_only(spec)`, `stage2_v2(spec, k_max=6, p_single=0.9)`, each `-> set[tuple[str, str]]` of (cell, type)
  - `specdx/sim/study.py`:
    - `METHODS = ("v3", "majority", "anchor_only", "v2_stage2")`, `BASELINES` (the last three), `F1_TARGET = 0.6`; `min_replicates(sweep) -> int` (§8.1's minimum: 10, or 5 on the regime map);
    - the regime map: `REGIME_ACCURACY = (0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 0.95)`, `REGIME_DELTA = (0.0, 0.1, 0.2)`, `REGIME_MODELS = (2, 4)`, `MARGIN = 0.05`; `regime_config(base, models, accuracy, delta) -> SimConfig`, `regime_points(base)`; `versus(f1, margin=MARGIN) -> dict[str, str]` (W, T or L per type against the best baseline);
    - `Result` (`counts`, `theta_errors`, `pairs`, `converged`, `reads`, `gaps`, `sizes`, `accuracy`) with `.summary() -> dict` (T4(a)'s bound; the mean accuracy, T2′'s chance line |A|/K for |A| = 1 and 2, and T4(c)'s bound, ruling F20); `gamma_min(k, p) -> float` (T4(c)'s γ_min under the flat pool); `merge(results) -> Result`; `v3_counts(spec, fit, params) -> dict[str, Counts]`; `score(spec, fit, params, baselines=True) -> Result`; `replicate(config, params, baselines=True) -> tuple[Result, AnchoredFit, SimSpec]`;
    - `SWEEPS` (delta, models, n, tau_true, defect_rate, error_conc, arms with the within-group arm, regime); `run_sweep(name, base, params, replicates) -> list[dict]` (each point carries `versus`); `config_dict(config) -> dict`; `load_config(path) -> SimConfig` (a bare SimConfig JSON or one under `"config"`); `load_params(path) -> V3Params`; `sweep_markdown(sweeps, params, replicates: Mapping[str, int]) -> str` (a sweep below its minimum is labelled exploratory)
  - CLI: `specdx simulate --sweep NAME|all [--replicates R] [--config PATH] [--params PATH] --out OUT` writes `OUT.json` and `OUT.md`; `--replicates` defaults to §8.1's minimum per sweep (10, or 5 for `regime`), and fewer is labelled exploratory (ruling F20); exit 2 for an unknown sweep. It opens no `Workbench`.

```python
"""The simulation's metrics, baselines, sweeps and the `specdx simulate` command (§8.1)."""

import dataclasses
import json
import math

import pytest

from specdx.cli import main
from specdx.infer.anchored import V3Params
from specdx.infer.cells import Cell, Reading
from specdx.sim.baselines import anchor_only, majority_vote, stage2_v2
from specdx.sim.generator import SimConfig, SimSpec, Truth
from specdx.sim.metrics import Counts, calibration, theta_error, typed_counts
from specdx.sim.study import (
    METHODS,
    SWEEPS,
    config_dict,
    gamma_min,
    load_config,
    min_replicates,
    regime_config,
    replicate,
    versus,
)

P = V3Params(pi=0.1, s_mis=0.5, s_amb=0.25, s_empty=0.25, beta_sd=1.0,
             mu_alpha=1.0, sigma_alpha=1.5, tau_h=0.5, tau_a=0.5, tau_w=0.8)
TINY = SimConfig(alphas=(2.0, 0.5), act=(1.0, 1.0), columns=12, items=4, scenarios=2,
                 seeds=1, seed=3)
NONE, HIDDEN = Truth("none", ("d",), False, False, False), Truth("mis", ("a",), True, False, False)
WEAK, AMB = Truth("none", ("x",), False, False, True), Truth("amb", ("x", "y"), False, True, False)


def _spec(rows: dict[str, tuple[str | None, Truth, dict[str, list[str]]]]) -> SimSpec:
    """cell → (anchor, truth, model → values). A reading is clean when it is unanchored or
    reads the anchor, as if each cell were alone at its item."""
    cells, readings, truth = {}, [], {}
    for cid, (anchor, t, by_model) in rows.items():
        cells[cid] = Cell(cid, "value", anchor, "clause" if anchor else None, cid, cid, ("i0",))
        truth[cid] = t
        for m, values in by_model.items():
            readings += [Reading(cid, f"{m}|{i}", m, m, "s0", v, anchor in (None, v))
                         for i, v in enumerate(values)]
    return SimSpec(TINY, cells, readings, truth, {"m0": "m0", "m1": "m1"}, {"m0": 2.0, "m1": 0.5})


def test_each_type_is_scored_over_cells_with_exact_f1() -> None:
    truth = {"a": HIDDEN, "b": NONE, "c": WEAK}
    counts = typed_counts([("a", "hidden"), ("b", "hidden"), ("b", "weak")], truth)
    assert counts["hidden"].as_dict() == {"tp": 1, "fp": 1, "fn": 0, "f1": pytest.approx(2 / 3)}
    assert (counts["weak"].tp, counts["weak"].fp, counts["weak"].fn) == (0, 1, 1)
    assert counts["ambiguity"].f1 == 0.0 and Counts(3, 5, 4).f1 == pytest.approx(0.4)


def test_accuracy_error_and_calibration() -> None:
    assert theta_error({"m": 0.9, "n": 0.4}, {"m": 0.8, "n": 0.5}) == pytest.approx(0.1)
    ece, brier = calibration([(0.0, False)] * 5 + [(1.0, True)] * 5)
    assert ece == 0.0 and brier == 0.0
    ece, brier = calibration([(0.8, False), (0.8, True)])
    assert ece == pytest.approx(0.3) and brier == pytest.approx((0.64 + 0.04) / 2)


def test_majority_vote_and_anchor_only() -> None:
    spec = _spec({
        "h": ("d", HIDDEN, {"m0": ["a", "a", "a"], "m1": ["a", "e", "a"]}),
        "n": ("d", NONE, {"m0": ["d", "d", "d"], "m1": ["d", "e", "d"]}),
        "w": (None, WEAK, {"m0": ["x", "x", "y"], "m1": ["x", "x", "x"]}),
        "z": (None, AMB, {"m0": ["x", "x", "y"], "m1": ["y", "y", "x"]}),
    })
    assert majority_vote(spec) == {("h", "hidden"), ("w", "weak"), ("z", "ambiguity")}
    assert anchor_only(spec) == {("h", "hidden")}


def test_v2_stage_2_on_joint_keys_flags_a_cell_every_model_reads_off_its_anchor() -> None:
    reads = {m: ["d"] * 6 for m in ("m0", "m1")}
    spec = _spec({"h": ("d", HIDDEN, {m: ["a"] * 6 for m in ("m0", "m1")}),
                  "n": ("d", NONE, reads)})
    assert ("h", "hidden") in stage2_v2(spec) and ("n", "hidden") not in stage2_v2(spec)


def test_a_replicate_scores_every_method_and_is_deterministic() -> None:
    result, fit, spec = replicate(TINY, P)
    again, _, _ = replicate(TINY, P)
    assert set(result.counts) == set(METHODS)
    assert result.summary() == again.summary()
    assert result.theta_errors[0] == pytest.approx(theta_error(fit.theta, spec.theta))
    summary = result.summary()
    assert {"theta_error", "ece", "brier", "converged", "n", "delta", "t4a", "accuracy",
            "chance", "t4c"} <= set(summary)
    assert 0.5 < summary["accuracy"] < 1 and 0 <= summary["t4c"] <= 1


def test_t2_prime_s_chance_line_and_t4c_s_distance() -> None:
    # At p = |A|/K a set's prediction is flat, like ∅'s: γ_min is 0 on the chance line.
    assert gamma_min(4, 0.25) == pytest.approx(0.0) and gamma_min(4, 0.5) == pytest.approx(0.0)
    assert gamma_min(4, 0.9) > 0.1
    assert (min_replicates("regime"), min_replicates("arms"), min_replicates("n")) == (5, 10, 10)


def test_each_sweep_varies_its_own_factor() -> None:
    base = SimConfig(alphas=(2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.0), act=(0.9, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4))
    assert [len(c.alphas) for _, c in SWEEPS["models"](base)] == [2, 3, 4]
    spreads = [c.alphas for _, c in SWEEPS["delta"](base)]
    assert spreads[0] == (0.875,) * 4 and spreads[2] == base.alphas
    arms = SWEEPS["arms"](base)
    assert [label for label, _ in arms] == ["base", "a3", "a5", "within"]
    assert [c.arm for _, c in arms] == ["base", "a3", "a5", "base"]
    assert arms[3][1].beta_within == 0.5
    assert [c.seeds for _, c in SWEEPS["n"](base)] == [1, 2, 3, 5, 8]
    assert {c.beta_sd for _, c in SWEEPS["tau_true"](base)} == {0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0}
    assert len(SWEEPS) == 8 and len(SWEEPS["regime"](base)) == 30


def test_the_regime_map_shifts_every_alpha_and_judges_against_the_best_baseline() -> None:
    base = SimConfig(alphas=(3.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.0), act=(0.9, 0.3, 0.8, 0.6))
    config = regime_config(base, 4, 0.9, 0.2)
    accuracy = [1 / (1 + math.exp(-a)) for a in config.alphas]
    assert accuracy == pytest.approx([0.995, 0.9 + 0.1 / 3, 0.9 - 0.1 / 3, 0.8])  # 1.0 clips
    assert config.act == base.act and regime_config(base, 2, 0.7, 0.0).act == (0.9, 0.3)
    f1 = {"v3": {"hidden": 0.8, "ambiguity": 0.5, "weak": 0.3},
          "majority": {"hidden": 0.7, "ambiguity": 0.54, "weak": 0.4},
          "anchor_only": {"hidden": 0.78, "ambiguity": 0.0, "weak": 0.0},
          "v2_stage2": {"hidden": 0.5, "ambiguity": 0.0, "weak": 0.1}}
    assert versus(f1) == {"hidden": "T", "ambiguity": "T", "weak": "L"}
    f1["v3"]["ambiguity"] = 0.6
    assert versus(f1)["ambiguity"] == "W"


def test_the_simulate_command_writes_json_and_markdown(tmp_path) -> None:
    config = tmp_path / "config.json"
    config.write_text(json.dumps(config_dict(TINY)), encoding="utf-8")
    params = tmp_path / "params.json"
    params.write_text(json.dumps(dataclasses.asdict(P)), encoding="utf-8")
    out = tmp_path / "sim"
    assert main(["simulate", "--sweep", "arms", "--replicates", "1", "--config", str(config),
                 "--params", str(params), "--out", str(out)]) == 0
    written = json.loads(out.with_suffix(".json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert [p["point"] for p in written["sweeps"]["arms"]] == ["base", "a3", "a5", "within"]
    assert set(written["sweeps"]["arms"][0]["versus"]) == {"hidden", "ambiguity", "weak"}
    assert written["params"] == dataclasses.asdict(P) and load_config(config) == TINY
    assert written["replicates"] == {"arms": 1}
    report = out.with_suffix(".md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "## Sweep: arms" in report and "Exploratory: below §8.1's minimum of 10" in report
    assert main(["simulate", "--sweep", "nope", "--out", str(out)]) == 2
```

<!-- apply: create specdx/sim/metrics.py -->
```python
"""The simulation's metrics (spec v3 §8.1): typed F1, accuracy recovery, calibration."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from fractions import Fraction

from specdx.sim.generator import Truth

TYPES: tuple[str, ...] = ("hidden", "ambiguity", "weak")  # contradiction is not simulated


@dataclass
class Counts:
    tp: int = 0
    fp: int = 0
    fn: int = 0

    def add(self, other: Counts) -> None:
        self.tp, self.fp, self.fn = self.tp + other.tp, self.fp + other.fp, self.fn + other.fn

    @property
    def f1(self) -> float:
        """2tp / (2tp + fp + fn), exactly (matching.exact_f1), as a float."""
        denominator = 2 * self.tp + self.fp + self.fn
        return float(Fraction(2 * self.tp, denominator)) if denominator else 0.0

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, float]:
        return {"tp": self.tp, "fp": self.fp, "fn": self.fn, "f1": self.f1}


def typed_counts(predicted: Iterable[tuple[str, str]],
                 truth: Mapping[str, Truth]) -> dict[str, Counts]:
    """predicted: (cell, type) pairs. Each type is scored over cells: a predicted cell that
    is a truth of that type is a TP, any other an FP, a missed truth an FN."""
    found = set(predicted)
    out: dict[str, Counts] = {}
    for t in TYPES:
        wanted = {c for c, x in truth.items() if getattr(x, t)}
        mine = {c for c, kind in found if kind == t}
        out[t] = Counts(len(mine & wanted), len(mine - wanted), len(wanted - mine))
    return out


def theta_error(estimated: Mapping[str, float], true: Mapping[str, float]) -> float:
    """Mean |θ̂_m − θ_m| over the models."""
    return sum(abs(estimated[m] - true[m]) for m in true) / len(true)


def calibration(pairs: Sequence[tuple[float, bool]], bins: int = 10) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """Expected calibration error over equal-width bins, and the Brier score, of posterior
    defect probabilities against the planted truth."""
    if not pairs:
        return 0.0, 0.0
    ece = 0.0
    for b in range(bins):
        lo, hi = b / bins, (b + 1) / bins
        mine = [(p, y) for p, y in pairs if lo <= p < hi or (b == bins - 1 and p == 1.0)]
        if mine:
            gap = sum(p for p, _ in mine) / len(mine) - sum(y for _, y in mine) / len(mine)
            ece += len(mine) / len(pairs) * abs(gap)
    brier = sum((p - y) ** 2 for p, y in pairs) / len(pairs)
    return ece, brier
```

<!-- apply: create specdx/sim/baselines.py -->
```python
"""The simulation's baselines (spec v3 §8.1): majority vote over models, "deviates from the
anchor ⇒ defect", and v2's stage 2 on joint class keys built from the simulated items."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
from collections.abc import Sequence

from specdx.infer.cells import Reading
from specdx.infer.latent import Observation, fit
from specdx.sim.generator import SimSpec

Predictions = set[tuple[str, str]]


def by_cell(spec: SimSpec) -> dict[str, list[Reading]]:
    """Each cell's readings in reading order, every cell of the specification a key (an
    unread cell has none). The baselines, the study and G0 share this one helper."""
    out: dict[str, list[Reading]] = {c: [] for c in spec.cells}
    for r in spec.readings:
        out[r.cell].append(r)
    return out


def _mode(values: Sequence[str]) -> str:
    counts = Counter(values)
    return min(counts, key=lambda v: (-counts[v], v))


def majority_vote(spec: SimSpec) -> Predictions:
    """Each model votes its modal reading. Hidden: the plurality of the votes differs from
    the anchor. Ambiguity: the models' votes differ. Weak: every model votes x on a value
    cell, and a clean behaviour read another value."""
    out: Predictions = set()
    for cid, rs in by_cell(spec).items():
        if not rs:
            continue
        cell = spec.cells[cid]
        votes = [_mode([r.value for r in rs if r.model == m])
                 for m in sorted({r.model for r in rs})]
        top = _mode(votes)
        if cell.anchor is not None and top != cell.anchor:
            out.add((cid, "hidden"))
        if len(set(votes)) >= 2:
            out.add((cid, "ambiguity"))
        elif cell.kind != "constraint" and any(r.value != top and r.clean for r in rs):
            out.add((cid, "weak"))
    return out


def anchor_only(spec: SimSpec) -> Predictions:
    """Hidden wherever most readings deviate from the anchor; no other type."""
    return {(cid, "hidden") for cid, rs in by_cell(spec).items()
            if rs and spec.cells[cid].anchor is not None
            and sum(r.value != spec.cells[cid].anchor for r in rs) * 2 > len(rs)}


def stage2_v2(spec: SimSpec, k_max: int = 6, p_single: float = 0.9) -> Predictions:
    """v2's stage 2 (latent.fit, Beta(7, 3)) on joint class keys: a behaviour's class at an
    item is the tuple of its readings there. Typing as v2's scores.py does it, per cell:
    Hidden(c) ≥ 0.5 where some admissible class violates c alone (reads off its anchor);
    an ambiguity at the cells the MAP set's classes differ on when P(|A| ≥ 2) ≥ 0.5; a weak
    check at the cells where a clean class outside the MAP set, read twice or more,
    differs from the MAP set's best class, when its outside mass is ≥ 0.5."""
    rows: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, str]] = {}
    who: dict[str, Reading] = {}
    for r in spec.readings:
        item = f"{spec.cells[r.cell].steps[0]}|{r.scenario}"
        rows.setdefault((item, r.run_key), {})[r.cell] = r.value
        who[r.run_key] = r
    classes = {key: repr(sorted(cells.items())) for key, cells in rows.items()}
    obs = [Observation(item, run, who[run].model, who[run].family, classes[(item, run)])
           for item, run in sorted(rows)]
    post = fit(obs, models=dict(spec.models), k_max=k_max, p_single=p_single)
    content = {classes[key]: rows[key] for key in rows}
    out: Predictions = set()
    for item, ip in post.items.items():
        counts = Counter(classes[key] for key in rows if key[0] == item)

        def off(k: str) -> frozenset[str]:
            return frozenset(c for c, v in content[k].items()
                             if spec.cells[c].anchor is not None and v != spec.cells[c].anchor)

        for cid in {c for k in ip.classes for c in content[k]}:
            if spec.cells[cid].anchor is None:
                continue
            hits = {k for k in ip.classes if off(k) == {cid}}
            if sum(p for s, p in ip.set_posterior.items() if s & hits) >= 0.5:
                out.add((cid, "hidden"))
        best = ip.map_set
        if ip.p_ambiguous >= 0.5 and len(best) >= 2:
            members = sorted(best)
            for cid in content[members[0]]:
                if len({content[k].get(cid) for k in members}) >= 2:
                    out.add((cid, "ambiguity"))
            continue
        reference = max(sorted(best), key=lambda k: ip.p_member.get(k, 0.0))
        for k in sorted(counts):
            if k in best or counts[k] < 2 or off(k):
                continue
            outside = (1.0 if k not in ip.classes
                       else sum(p for s, p in ip.set_posterior.items() if k not in s))
            if outside >= 0.5:
                for cid, v in content[k].items():
                    if spec.cells[cid].kind != "constraint" and content[reference].get(cid) != v:
                        out.add((cid, "weak"))
    return out
```

<!-- apply: create specdx/sim/study.py -->
```python
"""The simulation study (spec v3.1 §8.1): replicates, sweeps, the regime map, the report.

A replicate generates one synthetic specification, fits the anchored model, types its
cells with the same core the real diagnosis uses (infer.typing), and scores v3 and the
three baselines against the planted truth.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
import math
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from itertools import combinations
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from specdx.infer.anchored import AnchoredFit, V3Params, fit_anchored
from specdx.infer.typing import type_cells
from specdx.sim.baselines import anchor_only, by_cell, majority_vote, stage2_v2
from specdx.sim.generator import SimConfig, SimSpec, generate
from specdx.sim.metrics import TYPES, Counts, calibration, theta_error, typed_counts

METHODS: tuple[str, ...] = ("v3", "majority", "anchor_only", "v2_stage2")
BASELINES: tuple[str, ...] = METHODS[1:]
F1_TARGET = 0.6  # "runs needed to reach a given F1": the G0 bar for hidden
# The regime map of spec v3.1 §8.1: mean cell accuracy × accuracy spread × models, judged
# against the best baseline per type with a margin.
REGIME_ACCURACY: tuple[float, ...] = (0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 0.95)
REGIME_DELTA: tuple[float, ...] = (0.0, 0.1, 0.2)
REGIME_MODELS: tuple[int, ...] = (2, 4)
MARGIN = 0.05


def min_replicates(sweep: str) -> int:
    """§8.1's minimum replicates per point: 5 on the regime map, 10 elsewhere (at least 10
    per arm at the calibrated point). Fewer is reported as exploratory."""
    return 5 if sweep == "regime" else 10


@dataclass
class Result:
    """One replicate, or several merged. counts: method → type → counts. pairs: P(A_c ≠
    {d_c}) and the hidden truth for every anchored cell (calibration)."""

    counts: dict[str, dict[str, Counts]]
    theta_errors: list[float]
    pairs: list[tuple[float, bool]]
    converged: list[bool]
    reads: list[float]
    gaps: list[float]
    sizes: list[float]
    accuracy: list[float] = dataclasses.field(default_factory=list)

    def summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """n: mean readings per model and cell; delta: the accuracy gap of the two most
        accurate models; t4a: Theorem T4(a)'s bound. accuracy: the mean true accuracy at a
        cell of median difficulty; chance: T2′'s chance line |A|/K for |A| = 1 and 2, with
        K the mean number of candidate values; t4c: T4(c)'s bound at that accuracy."""
        ece, brier = calibration(self.pairs)
        n, gap, size = _mean(self.reads), _mean(self.gaps), _mean(self.sizes)
        p, k = _mean(self.accuracy), max(2, round(size))
        return {"methods": {m: {t: c.as_dict() for t, c in by.items()}
                            for m, by in self.counts.items()},
                "theta_error": _mean(self.theta_errors), "ece": ece, "brier": brier,
                "converged": _mean([float(c) for c in self.converged]), "n": n, "delta": gap,
                "t4a": min(1.0, 4 * size * math.exp(-n * gap ** 2 / 32)),
                "accuracy": p, "chance": [1 / k, 2 / k],
                "t4c": min(1.0, 2 * k * math.exp(-n * gamma_min(k, p) ** 2 / 2))}


def gamma_min(k: int, p: float) -> float:
    """T2′/T4(c): the smallest sup-norm distance between the reading distributions that
    two candidate sets (one value, a pair or ∅, over k values) predict at accuracy p under
    the flat error pool. It is 0 where p equals some |A|/k: T2′'s chance line."""

    def predicted(a: tuple[int, ...]) -> list[float]:
        if not a:
            return [1 / k] * k
        return [p / len(a) if y in a else (1 - p) / (k - len(a)) for y in range(k)]

    sets = [(), *((i,) for i in range(k)), *combinations(range(k), 2)]
    shapes = [predicted(a) for a in sets]
    return min(max(abs(x - y) for x, y in zip(u, v, strict=True))
               for u, v in combinations(shapes, 2))


def _mean(xs: Sequence[float]) -> float:
    return sum(xs) / len(xs) if xs else 0.0


def merge(results: Sequence[Result]) -> Result:
    counts = {m: {t: Counts() for t in TYPES} for m in METHODS}
    for r in results:
        for m, by in r.counts.items():
            for t, c in by.items():
                counts[m][t].add(c)
    return Result(counts, [x for r in results for x in r.theta_errors],
                  [x for r in results for x in r.pairs], [x for r in results for x in r.converged],
                  [x for r in results for x in r.reads], [x for r in results for x in r.gaps],
                  [x for r in results for x in r.sizes], [x for r in results for x in r.accuracy])


def v3_counts(spec: SimSpec, fit: AnchoredFit, params: V3Params) -> dict[str, Counts]:
    """v3's typed counts at the given thresholds (no refit: G0 varies τ_H, τ_A, τ_W)."""
    found = type_cells(spec.cells, fit, by_cell(spec), params)
    return typed_counts(((f.cell, f.type) for f in found), spec.truth)


def score(spec: SimSpec, fit: AnchoredFit, params: V3Params, baselines: bool = True) -> Result:
    counts = {"v3": v3_counts(spec, fit, params)}
    if baselines:
        counts |= {"majority": typed_counts(majority_vote(spec), spec.truth),
                   "anchor_only": typed_counts(anchor_only(spec), spec.truth),
                   "v2_stage2": typed_counts(stage2_v2(spec), spec.truth)}
    pairs = [(fit.cells[c].p_defect, spec.truth[c].hidden) for c in fit.cells
             if spec.cells[c].anchor is not None]
    true = spec.theta
    ranked = sorted(true.values(), reverse=True)
    reads = [len(rs) / len({r.model for r in rs}) for rs in by_cell(spec).values() if rs]
    return Result(counts, [theta_error(fit.theta, true)], pairs, [fit.converged],
                  [_mean(reads)], [ranked[0] - ranked[1] if len(ranked) > 1 else 0.0],
                  [_mean([len(p.values) + 1 for p in fit.cells.values()])], [_mean(ranked)])


def replicate(config: SimConfig, params: V3Params,
              baselines: bool = True) -> tuple[Result, AnchoredFit, SimSpec]:
    spec = generate(config)
    fit = fit_anchored(spec.cells, spec.readings, models=spec.models, params=params)
    return score(spec, fit, params, baselines), fit, spec


def _spread(alphas: tuple[float, ...], scale: float) -> tuple[float, ...]:
    mean = sum(alphas) / len(alphas)
    return tuple(mean + scale * (a - mean) for a in alphas)


def _logit(p: float) -> float:
    p = min(max(p, 0.51), 0.995)
    return math.log(p / (1 - p))


def regime_config(base: SimConfig, models: int, accuracy: float, delta: float) -> SimConfig:
    """The base configuration with `models` models whose accuracies at a cell of median
    difficulty are evenly spread by `delta` around `accuracy` (all α shifted); act rates are
    the base's, in its order."""
    acc = [accuracy + delta * (0.5 - i / (models - 1)) for i in range(models)]
    act = list(base.act) + [base.act[-1]] * max(0, models - len(base.act))
    return dataclasses.replace(base, alphas=tuple(_logit(p) for p in acc),
                               act=tuple(act[:models]))


def regime_points(base: SimConfig) -> list[tuple[str, SimConfig]]:
    return [(f"M={m} acc={a} Δ={d}", regime_config(base, m, a, d))
            for m in REGIME_MODELS for a in REGIME_ACCURACY for d in REGIME_DELTA]


def versus(f1: dict[str, dict[str, float]], margin: float = MARGIN) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Per type: W when v3's F1 beats the best baseline's by more than the margin, L when
    it is below it by more than the margin, else T."""
    out = {}
    for t in TYPES:
        gap = f1["v3"][t] - max(f1[m][t] for m in BASELINES)
        out[t] = "W" if gap > margin else ("L" if gap < -margin else "T")
    return out


def _models(base: SimConfig, m: int) -> SimConfig:
    alphas, act = list(base.alphas), list(base.act)
    while len(alphas) < m:
        alphas.append(min(alphas) - 0.5)
        act.append(act[-1])
    return dataclasses.replace(base, alphas=tuple(alphas[:m]), act=tuple(act[:m]))


Sweep = Callable[[SimConfig], list[tuple[str, SimConfig]]]
# §8.1's sweeps, each varying one factor from the base (calibrated) configuration.
SWEEPS: dict[str, Sweep] = {
    "delta": lambda b: [(f"spread x{s}", dataclasses.replace(b, alphas=_spread(b.alphas, s)))
                        for s in (0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0)],
    "models": lambda b: [(f"M={m}", _models(b, m)) for m in (2, 3, 4)],
    "n": lambda b: [(f"seeds={k}", dataclasses.replace(b, seeds=k)) for k in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8)],
    "tau_true": lambda b: [(f"tau={t}", dataclasses.replace(b, beta_sd=t))
                           for t in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0)],
    "defect_rate": lambda b: [(f"rate={r}", dataclasses.replace(b, defect_rate=r))
                              for r in (0.05, 0.15, 0.3)],
    "error_conc": lambda b: [(f"conc={c}", dataclasses.replace(b, error_conc=c))
                             for c in (0.2, 1.0, 5.0)],
    "arms": lambda b: [(arm, dataclasses.replace(b, arm=arm)) for arm in ("base", "a3", "a5")]
    + [("within", dataclasses.replace(b, beta_within=0.5))],
    "regime": regime_points,
}


def run_sweep(name: str, base: SimConfig, params: V3Params,
              replicates: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Each point of one sweep, merged over `replicates` seeds (base.seed, base.seed + 1, …)."""
    out = []
    for label, config in SWEEPS[name](base):
        runs = [replicate(dataclasses.replace(config, seed=base.seed + i), params)[0]
                for i in range(replicates)]
        summary = merge(runs).summary()
        f1 = {m: {t: summary["methods"][m][t]["f1"] for t in TYPES} for m in METHODS}
        out.append({"point": label, "config": config_dict(config)} | summary
                   | {"versus": versus(f1)})
    return out


def config_dict(config: SimConfig) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {k: list(v) if isinstance(v, tuple) else v
            for k, v in dataclasses.asdict(config).items()}


def load_config(path: Path) -> SimConfig:
    raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    raw = raw.get("config", raw)
    return SimConfig(**{k: tuple(v) if isinstance(v, list) else v for k, v in raw.items()})


def load_params(path: Path) -> V3Params:
    raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return V3Params(**raw.get("params", raw))


def _f1s(point: dict[str, Any], method: str) -> str:
    return " / ".join(f"{point['methods'][method][t]['f1']:.2f}" for t in TYPES)


def sweep_markdown(sweeps: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]], params: V3Params,
                   replicates: Mapping[str, int]) -> str:
    lines = ["<!-- generated by specdx simulate -->", "", "# SpecDx v3.1 simulation study", "",
             f"Parameters: {dataclasses.asdict(params)}.",
             "F1 cells read hidden / ambiguity / weak. T4(a) is Theorem T4's bound "
             "4|V|·exp(−nΔ²/32) on the two-model contrast rule, with n the mean readings per "
             "model and cell and Δ the accuracy gap of the two most accurate models. p̄ is the "
             "mean accuracy at a cell of median difficulty; T2′ identifies a set A from one "
             "model unless p equals its chance line |A|/K (shown for |A| = 1 and 2, K the mean "
             "number of candidate values), and T4(c) is the minimum-distance rule's bound "
             "2K·exp(−nγ_min²/2) at p̄. The last column compares v3.1 with the best baseline "
             f"per type (W, T or L, margin {MARGIN}).", ""]
    for name, points in sweeps.items():
        n, least = replicates[name], min_replicates(name)
        label = (f" Exploratory: below §8.1's minimum of {least} replicates per point."
                 if n < least else "")
        lines += [f"## Sweep: {name}", "", f"Replicates per point: {n}.{label}", "",
                  "| point | n | Δ | T4(a) | p̄ | T2′ chance | T4(c) | v3 | majority | "
                  "anchor-only | v2 stage 2 | mean abs θ error | ECE | converged | "
                  "v3 vs best (H A W) |",
                  "|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|---:|---|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---|"]
        for p in points:
            chance = " / ".join(f"{c:.2f}" for c in p["chance"])
            lines.append(f"| {p['point']} | {p['n']:.1f} | {p['delta']:.3f} | {p['t4a']:.3f} | "
                         f"{p['accuracy']:.3f} | {chance} | {p['t4c']:.3f} | "
                         f"{_f1s(p, 'v3')} | {_f1s(p, 'majority')} | {_f1s(p, 'anchor_only')} | "
                         f"{_f1s(p, 'v2_stage2')} | {p['theta_error']:.3f} | {p['ece']:.3f} | "
                         f"{p['converged']:.2f} | {' '.join(p['versus'][t] for t in TYPES)} |")
        if name == "n":
            for t in TYPES:
                reached = [p["n"] for p in points if p["methods"]["v3"][t]["f1"] >= F1_TARGET]
                where = f"{min(reached):.1f}" if reached else "not reached"
                lines.append(f"\nReadings per model and cell for v3 {t} F1 ≥ {F1_TARGET}: "
                             f"{where}.")
        lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
```

```python
        print(cache_summary(RunCache(WORK_DIR / "runs.jsonl")))
```

```python
    if args.command == "simulate":
        import json

        from specdx.infer.anchored import V3Params
        from specdx.sim.generator import SimConfig
        from specdx.sim.study import (
            SWEEPS,
            config_dict,
            load_config,
            load_params,
            min_replicates,
            run_sweep,
            sweep_markdown,
        )

        names = list(SWEEPS) if args.sweep == "all" else [args.sweep]
        if any(n not in SWEEPS for n in names):
            print(f"error: unknown sweep {args.sweep!r}; choose from {', '.join(SWEEPS)} or all",
                  file=sys.stderr)
            return 2
        base = load_config(args.config) if args.config else SimConfig()
        params = load_params(args.params) if args.params else V3Params()
        replicates = {n: args.replicates or min_replicates(n) for n in names}
        sweeps = {n: run_sweep(n, base, params, replicates[n]) for n in names}
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.out.with_suffix(".json").write_text(json.dumps(
            {"base": config_dict(base), "params": dataclasses.asdict(params),
             "replicates": replicates, "sweeps": sweeps}, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        text = sweep_markdown(sweeps, params, replicates)
        args.out.with_suffix(".md").write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        print(text)
        return 0
```
