### Task 9: The real typing, `diagnose_v3` (spec v3.1 §5.1–§5.5)

**Files:**
- Modify: `specdx/types.py` (`Defect.subtype`)
- Modify: `specdx/infer/scores.py` (`merge_defects` keeps the subtype; v2's multi-clause candidate path moves into shared helpers that `diagnose` and `diagnose_v3` both call)
- Modify: `specdx/infer/typing.py` (imports; part 2 appended)
- Modify: `tests/infer/test_typing.py` (imports; part 2 appended)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7's core; Task 3's `CellData`; v2's `scores.UNATTEMPTED`, `UNOBSERVABLE`, `Diagnosis`, `ambiguity_key`, `identity`, `merge_defects`; `active.refute.SYMBOLIC_WITNESS`; `types.Refuter`.
- Produces:
  - In `specdx/infer/scores.py`, v2's multi-clause candidate path as shared helpers (spec v3.1 §11: hitting sets and refutation are reused unchanged; ruling F6): `scenario_groups(behaviours) -> dict[str, list[Behaviour]]`, `hitting_candidates(groups, violated, weights, tau) -> list[tuple[float, str, frozenset[str]]]`, `refutation_order(known)` (the candidates' sort key) and `observable_clauses(hitting, scenario_id, observable, relevant) -> bool`. v2's `diagnose` calls them with unchanged behaviour: `tests/infer/test_scores.py` and `tests/test_replay.py` pass unmodified.
  - `Defect(type, location, scenarios, witnesses, score, note="", subtype="")`: `subtype` is informational and never scored.
  - `CellDiagnosis(Diagnosis)`: adds `findings: list[CellFinding]` and `ambiguity_cells: dict[tuple[str, str, tuple[str, ...]], str]` (ambiguity identity → its cell).
  - `refutation_scenario(cell: Cell, readings: Sequence[Reading]) -> str`
  - `diagnose_v3(behaviours, violated: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]], data: CellData, fit: AnchoredFit, refuter: Refuter, *, observable: frozenset[str], relevant: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]], tau: float = 0.05, known: frozenset[tuple[str, ...]] = frozenset()) -> CellDiagnosis`. Thresholds come from `fit.params`. §5.2's V(b) is v2 §3.4's, over every clause of C (resolution 18). Witnesses are sorted, and the refuter sees each scenario's behaviours in run-key order, so the result does not depend on the input order (ruling F19).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

A recording refuter scripts each refutation's result and records the behaviours it was given. The location test runs `specdx.eval.matching.location_matches` against W2- and H1-shaped truths (resolution 9). The determinism test reverses the behaviours of a multi-clause contradiction too (ruling F19).

```python
# Part 2 (spec v3 §5.1–§5.5): diagnose_v3 on hand-built cells and fits.

S1, S2 = Scenario("s1", 15, 12, 0), Scenario("s2", 15, 15, 0)
CLAUSE = "golden:po:PO-0015:trang_thai"
VALUE = Cell("s1|po:PO-0015:trang_thai", "value", "d", CLAUSE, "po.trang_thai",
             "po.trang_thai", ("step",))


class Recorder:
    """Returns the scripted result for each target set; records every call in order."""

    def __init__(self, results: dict[frozenset[str], RefuteResult] | None = None) -> None:
        self.results, self.calls, self.groups = results or {}, [], []

    def refute(self, targets, scenario, behaviours):
        self.calls.append((tuple(sorted(targets)), scenario.id))
        self.groups.append(tuple(b.run_key for b in behaviours))
        return self.results.get(targets, RefuteResult(True, SYMBOLIC_WITNESS, 1))


def _b(key: str, model: str, scenario: Scenario) -> Behaviour:
    return Behaviour(key, InterpreterId(model, model, 0, 1), scenario, State.empty())


def _case(cells, posts, readings, weights=None):
    fit = AnchoredFit({"m1": 2.0, "m2": 0.0}, {}, {}, {p.cell: p for p in posts},
                      weights or {r.run_key: 0.5 for r in readings}, True, 1, P)
    return CellData({c.id: c for c in cells}, readings), fit


def _hidden_case():
    readings = [Reading(VALUE.id, "b1", "m1", "m1", "s1", "a"),
                Reading(VALUE.id, "b2", "m2", "m2", "s1", "a")]
    post = _post(VALUE, {F({"a"}): 0.9, F({"d"}): 0.1})
    data, fit = _case([VALUE], [post], readings)
    runs = [_b("b1", "m1", S1), _b("b2", "m2", S1)]
    return data, fit, runs, {"b1": F({CLAUSE}), "b2": F({CLAUSE})}


EVERY = F({"s1|step", "s2|step"})
NOTHING: frozenset[tuple[str, ...]] = frozenset()


def _diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, refuter, observable=EVERY, known=NOTHING):
    relevant = {"step": F({CLAUSE, "inv:x", "inv:y"})}
    return diagnose_v3(runs, violated, data, fit, refuter, observable=observable,
                       relevant=relevant, known=known)


def test_refutation_separates_a_hidden_requirement_from_a_contradiction_of_one() -> None:
    data, fit, runs, violated = _hidden_case()
    refuter = Recorder({F({CLAUSE}): RefuteResult(True, "s1|m1|v0|s100", 2)})
    found = _diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, refuter)
    [hidden] = found.defects
    assert (hidden.type, hidden.location, hidden.note, hidden.subtype) == (
        "hidden", (CLAUSE,), "refuted by s1|m1|v0|s100", MIS_KEY)
    assert refuter.calls == [((CLAUSE,), "s1")]  # the singleton hitting set defers to the cell
    failed = Recorder({F({CLAUSE}): RefuteResult(False, None, 7)})
    [contradiction] = _diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, failed).defects
    assert (contradiction.type, contradiction.note) == ("contradiction",
                                                        "unrefuted after 7 attempts")
    capped = Recorder({F({CLAUSE}): RefuteResult(False, None, 0, attempted=False)})
    [default] = _diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, capped).defects
    assert (default.type, default.note) == ("hidden", UNATTEMPTED)


def test_a_pooled_candidate_is_refuted_where_most_readings_leave_the_anchor() -> None:
    pooled = Cell("golden:po:PO-0015:sl_dat", "value", "{ordered}", "golden:po:PO-0015:sl_dat",
                  "po.sl_dat", "po.sl_dat", ("step",))
    readings = [Reading(pooled.id, "b1", "m1", "m1", "s1", "1"),
                Reading(pooled.id, "b2", "m2", "m2", "s2", "35"),
                Reading(pooled.id, "b3", "m1", "m1", "s2", "{ordered}"),
                Reading(pooled.id, "b4", "m2", "m2", "s1", "{ordered}")]
    data, fit = _case([pooled], [_post(pooled, {F({"1"}): 0.8, F({"{ordered}"}): 0.2})],
                      readings)
    runs = [_b("b1", "m1", S1), _b("b2", "m2", S2), _b("b3", "m1", S2), _b("b4", "m2", S1)]
    refuter = Recorder()
    [d] = _diagnose(data, fit, runs, dict.fromkeys(("b1", "b2", "b3", "b4"), F()),
                    refuter).defects
    assert refuter.calls == [((pooled.id,), "s1")] and d.scenarios == ("s1", "s2")  # a tie
    assert refutation_scenario(pooled, readings[1:]) == "s2"


def test_multi_clause_contradictions_come_from_weighted_hitting_sets() -> None:
    runs = [_b(f"b{i}", "m1", S1) for i in range(4)]
    violated = {"b0": F({"inv:x"}), "b1": F({"inv:x"}), "b2": F({"inv:y"}), "b3": F({"inv:y"})}
    data, fit = _case([], [], [], weights={f"b{i}": 0.5 for i in range(4)})
    pair = F({"inv:x", "inv:y"})
    failed = _diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, Recorder({pair: RefuteResult(False, None, 4)}))
    assert [(d.type, d.location) for d in failed.defects] == [
        ("contradiction", ("inv:x", "inv:y"))]
    probed = _diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, Recorder({pair: RefuteResult(True, "s1|p", 3)}))
    assert probed.defects == [] and probed.spent == {("inv:x", "inv:y")}
    repaired = _diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, Recorder())
    assert repaired.defects == [] and repaired.spent == frozenset()


def test_candidates_go_by_score_and_the_base_reports_locations_last() -> None:
    data, fit, runs, violated = _hidden_case()
    runs += [_b("b3", "m1", S1), _b("b4", "m1", S1)]
    violated |= {"b3": F({"inv:x"}), "b4": F({"inv:y"})}
    fit.weights.update({"b3": 0.5, "b4": 0.5})
    triple = (CLAUSE, "inv:x", "inv:y")  # hits every violation set: support 1.0 > 0.9
    order = Recorder()
    _diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, order)
    assert [c[0] for c in order.calls] == [triple, (CLAUSE,)]
    later = Recorder()
    _diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, later, known=F({triple}))
    assert [c[0] for c in later.calls] == [(CLAUSE,), triple]


def test_an_unobservable_candidate_is_listed_and_never_refuted() -> None:
    data, fit, runs, violated = _hidden_case()
    refuter = Recorder()
    found = _diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, refuter, observable=F())
    assert refuter.calls == [] and found.defects == []
    assert [(d.type, d.location) for d in found.unobservable] == [("unobservable", (CLAUSE,))]


def test_ambiguities_and_weak_checks_use_v2s_location_formats_and_merge_by_step() -> None:
    cells = [Cell(f"{s}|po:PO-0015:trang_thai", "value", None, None, "po.trang_thai",
                  "po.trang_thai", ("step",)) for s in ("s1", "s2")]
    event = Cell("s1|evt:DRAFTED:SO-0031:DE-SM-02", "event", None, None, "evt:DRAFTED:SO-0031",
                 "evt", ("step",))
    readings = [Reading(c.id, f"{c.id}|{m}|{i}", m, m, c.id.split("|")[0], v, True)
                for c in cells for m, vals in (("m1", "xy"), ("m2", "yx")) for i, v in
                enumerate(vals)]
    readings += [Reading(event.id, f"e{i}", "m1", "m1", "s1", "present" if i else "absent", True)
                 for i in range(3)]
    posts = [_post(c, {F({"x", "y"}): 0.9 if c.id.startswith("s1") else 0.7, F({"x"}): 0.1})
             for c in cells] + [_post(event, {F({"present"}): 0.95, F(): 0.05})]
    data, fit = _case([*cells, event], posts, readings)
    runs = [_b(r.run_key, r.model, S1 if r.scenario == "s1" else S2) for r in readings]
    found = _diagnose(data, fit, runs, {r.run_key: F() for r in readings}, Recorder())
    by_type = {d.type: d for d in found.defects}
    amb, weak = by_type["ambiguity"], by_type["weak"]
    assert (amb.location, amb.scenarios, amb.note) == (("po.trang_thai",), ("s1", "s2"),
                                                        "s1|step")
    assert found.ambiguity_cells == {("ambiguity", "step", ("po.trang_thai",)): cells[0].id}
    assert (weak.location, weak.witnesses) == (("evt:DRAFTED:SO-0031",), ("e0",))
    w2 = GroundTruth("W2-00", "weak", F({"evt:DRAFTED:SO-0031"}), "field")
    h1 = GroundTruth("H1-00", "hidden", F({CLAUSE}), "clause")
    assert location_matches(weak, w2)
    data, fit, runs, violated = _hidden_case()
    [hidden] = _diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, Recorder()).defects
    assert location_matches(hidden, h1)


def test_the_diagnosis_is_deterministic() -> None:
    data, fit, runs, violated = _hidden_case()
    assert (_diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, Recorder())
            == _diagnose(data, fit, runs[::-1], violated, Recorder()))
    # A multi-clause contradiction: its witnesses and the behaviours the refuter sees do not
    # follow the input order (ruling F19).
    runs = [_b(f"b{i}", "m1", S1) for i in range(4)]
    violated = {"b0": F({"inv:x"}), "b1": F({"inv:x"}), "b2": F({"inv:y"}), "b3": F({"inv:y"})}
    data, fit = _case([], [], [], weights={f"b{i}": 0.5 for i in range(4)})
    pair = F({"inv:x", "inv:y"})
    forward, backward = (Recorder({pair: RefuteResult(False, None, 4)}) for _ in range(2))
    found = _diagnose(data, fit, runs, violated, forward)
    assert found == _diagnose(data, fit, runs[::-1], violated, backward)
    assert found.defects[0].witnesses == ("b0", "b1", "b2", "b3")
    assert forward.groups == backward.groups == [("b0", "b1", "b2", "b3")]
```

```python
# The multi-clause candidate path (§3.4), shared by v2's diagnose and v3's diagnose_v3
# (spec v3.1 §11: hitting sets and refutation are reused unchanged).

Candidate = tuple[float, str, frozenset[str]]  # (support, scenario id, clauses)


def scenario_groups(behaviours: Sequence[Behaviour]) -> dict[str, list[Behaviour]]:
    """The behaviours that ran, by scenario id, each in input order."""
    groups: dict[str, list[Behaviour]] = defaultdict(list)
    for b in behaviours:
        if b.status == "ok":
            groups[b.scenario.id].append(b)
    return dict(groups)


def hitting_candidates(
    groups: Mapping[str, Sequence[Behaviour]], violated: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]],
    weights: Mapping[str, float], tau: float,
) -> list[Candidate]:
    """Every scenario's soft hitting sets over its runs' weighted V(b), in scenario order."""
    candidates: list[Candidate] = []
    for sid, group in groups.items():
        sets = [(violated[b.run_key], weights.get(b.run_key, 0.0)) for b in group]
        candidates += [(support, sid, hitting)
                       for hitting, support in soft_hitting_sets(sets, tau=tau)]
    return candidates


def refutation_order(known: frozenset[tuple[str, ...]]) -> Callable[[tuple], tuple]:
    """The sort key of candidates (support, scenario, clauses, ...): the base report's
    locations last, then descending support, scenario, size and clauses (§11)."""
    return lambda c: (tuple(sorted(c[2])) in known, -c[0], c[1], len(c[2]), sorted(c[2]))


def observable_clauses(
    hitting: Iterable[str], scenario_id: str, observable: frozenset[str],
    relevant: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]],
) -> bool:
    """Every clause of the set is read by some observable item of the scenario (§3.9)."""
    return all(_observable(c, scenario_id, observable, relevant) for c in hitting)
```

```python
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from specdx.active.refute import SYMBOLIC_WITNESS
from specdx.infer.anchored import AnchoredFit, CellPosterior, V3Params
from specdx.infer.cells import Cell, CellData, Reading
from specdx.infer.scores import (
    UNATTEMPTED,
    UNOBSERVABLE,
    Diagnosis,
    ambiguity_key,
    hitting_candidates,
    identity,
    merge_defects,
```

<!-- apply: append specdx/infer/typing.py -->
```python
# The real diagnosis (§5.1–§5.5): refutation, multi-clause contradictions, observability.

@dataclass
class CellDiagnosis(Diagnosis):
    """findings: every cell finding before refutation. ambiguity_cells: each reported
    ambiguity's identity before localisation (scores.ambiguity_key) → the cell behind it,
    the best scored when several merged: the ddmin oracle re-reads that cell (§5.3).
    spent: the multi-clause candidates refuted by a probe run, or never attempted, that
    found nothing (resolution 8); a cell candidate always yields a finding, so no singleton
    location is spent in v3.1."""

    findings: list[CellFinding] = field(default_factory=list)
    ambiguity_cells: dict[tuple[str, str, tuple[str, ...]], str] = field(default_factory=dict)


def _observable_cell(cell: Cell, readings: Sequence[Reading], observable: frozenset[str]) -> bool:
    """Stage 1 (v2 §3.5): a cell carries evidence when an observable item reads it."""
    return any(f"{r.scenario}|{step}" in observable for r in readings for step in cell.steps)


def _scenarios(readings: Sequence[Reading]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    return tuple(sorted({r.scenario for r in readings}))


def refutation_scenario(cell: Cell, readings: Sequence[Reading]) -> str:
    """Where a candidate is refuted: its own scenario, or for a pooled cell the scenario
    with the most acted readings outside d_c, ties to the smaller scenario id."""
    off = Counter(r.scenario for r in readings if r.value != cell.anchor)
    every = _scenarios(readings)
    return min(every, key=lambda s: (-off[s], s))


def _note(cell: Cell, readings: Sequence[Reading]) -> str:
    """An ambiguity's or weak check's item, scenario|step: the scenario with the most
    readings (ties to the smaller id) and the cell's first in-scope step."""
    counts = Counter(r.scenario for r in readings)
    return f"{min(counts, key=lambda s: (-counts[s], s))}|{cell.steps[0]}"


def diagnose_v3(
    behaviours: Sequence[Behaviour],
    violated: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]],
    data: CellData,
    fit: AnchoredFit,
    refuter: Refuter,
    *,
    observable: frozenset[str],
    relevant: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]],
    tau: float = 0.05,
    known: frozenset[tuple[str, ...]] = frozenset(),
) -> CellDiagnosis:
    """violated: each run's V(b), restricted to the items where it acted (v2 §3.4).
    observable: the observable items (stage 1). relevant: step id → the clauses relevant to
    it. known: locations the base report holds, refuted last on a mutant (v1 §11 item 13).
    Thresholds come from fit.params. The multi-clause path is v2's (scores.py's shared
    helpers); witnesses are sorted and the refuter sees each group in run-key order, so
    the diagnosis does not depend on the order of the behaviours."""
    params = fit.params
    by_cell = data.by_cell()
    findings = type_cells(data.cells, fit, by_cell, params)
    groups = {sid: sorted(group, key=lambda b: b.run_key)
              for sid, group in scenario_groups(behaviours).items()}

    def witnesses(group: Sequence[Behaviour], targets: frozenset[str]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        return tuple(sorted(b.run_key for b in group if violated[b.run_key] & targets))

    defects: list[Defect] = []
    unobservable: list[Defect] = []
    spent: set[tuple[str, ...]] = set()
    # (score, scenario, targets, the cell finding or None for a multi-clause candidate)
    candidates: list[tuple[float, str, frozenset[str], CellFinding | None]] = []
    for f in findings:
        if f.type != "hidden":
            continue
        cell, mine = data.cells[f.cell], by_cell[f.cell]
        assert cell.clause is not None
        if not _observable_cell(cell, mine, observable):
            unobservable.append(Defect(UNOBSERVABLE, (cell.clause,), _scenarios(mine),
                                       f.witnesses, f.score, "no observable item reads the cell"))
            continue
        candidates.append((f.score, refutation_scenario(cell, mine), frozenset({cell.clause}), f))
    for support, sid, hitting in hitting_candidates(groups, violated, fit.weights, tau):
        if len(hitting) < 2:
            continue  # a single clause is the cell path's: hidden or a contradiction of 1
        if not observable_clauses(hitting, sid, observable, relevant):
            unobservable.append(Defect(UNOBSERVABLE, tuple(sorted(hitting)), (sid,),
                                       witnesses(groups[sid], hitting), support,
                                       "no observable item reads every clause"))
            continue
        candidates.append((support, sid, hitting, None))
    candidates.sort(key=refutation_order(known))
    for score, sid, targets, finding in candidates:
        group = groups[sid]
        location = tuple(sorted(targets))
        result = refuter.refute(targets, group[0].scenario, group)
        failed = not result.refuted and result.attempted
        if finding is not None:
            mine = by_cell[finding.cell]
            if failed:
                defects.append(Defect("contradiction", location, _scenarios(mine),
                                      finding.witnesses, score,
                                      f"unrefuted after {result.attempts} attempts"))
            else:
                note = f"refuted by {result.witness}" if result.refuted else UNATTEMPTED
                defects.append(Defect("hidden", location, _scenarios(mine), finding.witnesses,
                                      score, note, finding.subtype))
        elif failed:
            defects.append(Defect("contradiction", location, (sid,), witnesses(group, targets),
                                  score, f"unrefuted after {result.attempts} attempts"))
        elif not result.refuted or result.witness != SYMBOLIC_WITNESS:
            spent.add(location)  # it cost a probe, or would have, and found nothing
    cells_of: dict[tuple[str, str, tuple[str, ...]], tuple[float, str]] = {}
    for f in findings:
        if f.type == "hidden":
            continue
        cell, mine = data.cells[f.cell], by_cell[f.cell]
        if not _observable_cell(cell, mine, observable):
            continue
        d = Defect(f.type, (cell.location,), _scenarios(mine), f.witnesses, f.score,
                   _note(cell, mine))
        defects.append(d)
        if f.type == "ambiguity":
            key = ambiguity_key(d)
            if key not in cells_of or f.score > cells_of[key][0]:
                cells_of[key] = (f.score, cell.id)
    return CellDiagnosis(merge_defects(defects, key=identity), merge_defects(unobservable), {},
                         frozenset(spent), findings, {k: v for k, (_, v) in cells_of.items()})
```

### Task 10: The loop, the gate and the screen on v3.1 (spec v3.1 §5.3, §6, §7, §11)

**Files:**
- Modify: `specdx/active/loop.py` (shown whole)
- Modify: `specdx/workbench.py`, `specdx/eval/pilot.py`, `specdx/eval/screen.py`, `specdx/cli.py`, `specdx/baselines/b2_single.py`, `experiments/p1b_replay.py`
- Modify (tests): `tests/active/test_loop.py` (rewritten), `tests/eval/test_pilot.py`, `tests/eval/test_screen.py`, `tests/test_workbench.py`, `tests/test_preregistration.py`, `tests/test_end_to_end.py`, `tests/test_parallel.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3's `CellLens`, `CellData`; Task 5's `fit_anchored`, `posterior_at`, `AnchoredFit`, `V3Params`; Task 9's `diagnose_v3`, `CellDiagnosis` (`ambiguity_cells`); v2's escalation, stage 1, refutation, ddmin and differential, unchanged.
- Produces:
  - `SpecDxConfig.params: V3Params = V3Params()`; `ambig_threshold`, `weak_threshold` and `p_single` leave `SpecDxConfig` (S1/S2 and v2's stage 2 are out of the loop; `sensitivity.py` and `latent.py` stay in the tree as simulation baselines).
  - `CellsLike` (Protocol: `extract(behaviours) -> CellData`); `LoopContext.cells: CellsLike | None = None` (the loop raises `ValueError` without one).
  - `Persists = Callable[[Sequence[Behaviour]], bool]`; `Localizer = Callable[[SpecVariant, Defect, Sequence[InterpreterId], RunBudget, Persists], tuple[str, ...] | None]`.
  - `Report` gains `alpha`, `cells`, `converged`, `iterations`, `fit_start`, all in `to_json()`; `items` holds stage 1 only. `Report.spent` holds the multi-clause candidate locations that cost a probe run, or would have, and found nothing (resolution 8; ruling F9).
  - `item_table(rasch) -> dict`; `cell_table(data, fit) -> dict` (per cell: anchor, readings per model, β, `sets`: the posterior over A_c as at most 22 `[sorted set, p]` pairs, most probable first (spec §3.5, §5.5; ruling F16), forms, MAP set and its posterior).
  - B2 and the refuter's interpreter order use θ_m; w_b weights only the hitting sets (resolution 17).
  - `oracle_interpreters(models_by_family, theta, variants, acted) -> list[InterpreterId]` (no θ threshold).
  - `GateConfig.params: V3Params = V3Params()` (never read from JSON: the gate test loads a configuration that carries a `"params"` object and gets the code's `V3Params()`, ruling F8); the gate's lines carry `alpha`, `converged`, `iterations` and `cells`.
  - `screen.ACT_RATE_MIN = 0.3`; `screen.MIN_MODELS = 4`; `Screen.stop` is true when fewer than 2 families or fewer than 4 acting models were admitted (spec v3.1 §7, G2); `screen.rescreen(path: Path) -> Screen`; `specdx screen --from PATH --out OUT` (no run, no database, no model; exit 3 on the stop rule).

- [ ] **Step 1: Rewrite the loop's tests around cells**

The fakes keep v2's lens (stage 1, escalation, violation sets) and add a cell lens. q1 and l1 are accurate at every calibration item and q2 at three of five, so the fit has an accuracy contrast. In `_split_plan` the accurate models read the grn12 step two ways and q2 both ways and wrong once: a pair T admits.

<!-- apply: create tests/active/test_loop.py -->
```python
"""The v3 loop on scripted interpreters: fakes for the runner, the v2 lens (stage 1,
escalation, violation sets) and the cell lens (the evidence)."""

import dataclasses
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from specdx.active.loop import LoopContext, SpecDxConfig, oracle_interpreters, run_specdx
from specdx.infer.act import Act
from specdx.infer.anchored import V3Params
from specdx.infer.cells import Cell, CellData, Reading, normalise
from specdx.infer.items import ItemView
from specdx.infer.scores import UNATTEMPTED
from specdx.scenarios.tguided import TLiteral
from specdx.spec.tell import TellCorpus
from specdx.types import Behaviour, Defect, RefuteResult, Scenario, State
from specdx.variant import SpecVariant

CANON = {"discount8": Scenario("discount8", 15, 15, 8), "grn12": Scenario("grn12", 15, 12, 0),
         "happy": Scenario("happy", 15, 15, 0)}
MODELS = {"qwen": ("q1", "q2"), "llama": ("l1",)}
GOOD = ("q1", "l1")  # accurate at every calibration item; q2 at three of five
VARIANT = SpecVariant("toy", Path("."), TellCorpus([]))
P = V3Params(pi=0.1, s_mis=0.5, s_amb=0.25, s_empty=0.25, beta_sd=1.0,
             mu_alpha=1.0, sigma_alpha=1.5, tau_h=0.5, tau_a=0.5, tau_w=0.8)


def _config(**kwargs) -> SpecDxConfig:
    return SpecDxConfig(MODELS, **({"params": P, "synthesize": False} | kwargs))


class FakeRunner:
    def __init__(self, plan) -> None:
        self.plan, self.executed, self.calls = plan, 0, []

    def run(self, variant, scenario, interp, suffix="", *, kind="population"):
        self.executed += 1
        self.calls.append(scenario.id)
        klass, bad = self.plan(scenario, interp)
        return Behaviour(f"{scenario.id}|{interp.key}|{variant.name}", interp, scenario,
                         State({}, {"k": klass, "v": ",".join(sorted(bad))}, {}))

    def run_batch(self, requests):
        return [self.run(r.variant, r.scenario, r.interp, r.suffix, kind=r.kind) for r in requests]


CLAUSES = frozenset({"c1", "n", "noise", "n1", "n2", "n3"})


def _calibration(model: str, j: int, run_key: str) -> str:
    return "OK" if model in GOOD or j < 3 else f"E{j}-{run_key}"


class FakeLens:
    """One item under test plus five calibration items, as a real run has eleven steps.
    Every behaviour acts at every item, and every clause is read at the steps under test."""

    relevant = {"step": CLAUSES, "step2": CLAUSES} | {f"cal{j}": frozenset() for j in range(5)}

    def violations(self, b):
        return frozenset(x for x in b.state.derived["v"].split(",") if x)

    def views(self, b, violated):
        k = b.state.derived["k"]
        model, family = b.interpreter.model, b.interpreter.family
        out = [ItemView(f"{b.scenario.id}|step", b.run_key, model, family, k, violated,
                        (("x", ((k, ()),)),))]
        for j in range(5):
            cal = _calibration(model, j, b.run_key)
            out.append(ItemView(f"{b.scenario.id}|cal{j}", b.run_key, model, family, cal,
                                frozenset(), (("x", ((cal, ()),)),)))
        return out

    def acts(self, b):
        return [Act(v.item, v.run_key, v.model, v.acted) for v in self.views(b, frozenset())]

    def diff(self, a, b):
        return frozenset({"x.k"}) if a != b else frozenset()


class FakeCells:
    """A value cell per acted view: at the steps under test the class read there (anchored
    to OK with clause c1 at the scenarios in `anchored`), at the calibration items OK."""

    def __init__(self, lens: FakeLens, anchored: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"grn12"})) -> None:
        self.lens, self.anchored = lens, anchored

    def extract(self, behaviours):
        cells: dict[str, Cell] = {}
        readings: list[Reading] = []
        for b in behaviours:
            for v in self.lens.views(b, self.lens.violations(b)):
                if not v.acted:
                    continue
                sid, step = v.item.split("|")
                if step.startswith("cal"):
                    cell = Cell(v.item, "value", "ok", f"golden:{step}", f"cal.{step}",
                                f"cal.{step}", (step,))
                elif sid in self.anchored:
                    cell = Cell(v.item, "value", "ok", "c1", "x.k", "x.k", (step,))
                else:
                    cell = Cell(v.item, "value", None, None, "x.k", "x.k", (step,))
                cells[cell.id] = cell
                readings.append(Reading(cell.id, b.run_key, v.model, v.family, sid,
                                        normalise(v.klass), not v.violated, v.variant))
        return CellData(cells, sorted(readings, key=lambda r: (r.cell, r.run_key)))


class AlwaysRefute:
    def refute(self, targets, scenario, behaviours):
        return RefuteResult(True, "w", 1)


def _hidden_plan(scenario, interp):
    good = interp.model in GOOD
    if scenario.id == "grn12":
        return ("K", {"c1"}) if good else (f"E{interp.seed}", {f"n{interp.seed}"})
    return ("OK", set()) if good else (f"E{interp.seed}-{scenario.id}", {"noise"})


class NeverRefute:
    """Every refutation that the probe budget allows fails; past the cap none is attempted."""

    def __init__(self, budget) -> None:
        self.budget = budget

    def refute(self, targets, scenario, behaviours):
        if self.budget.take("probe"):
            return RefuteResult(False, None, 1)
        return RefuteResult(False, None, 0, attempted=False)


def _ctx(runner, localizer=None, lens=None, refuter=None, anchored=frozenset({"grn12"})):
    lens = lens or FakeLens()
    return LoopContext(runner, lens, CANON, list(CANON.values()), 5,
                       refuter or (lambda interps, budget: AlwaysRefute()), localizer,
                       cells=FakeCells(lens, anchored))


def test_a_planted_hidden_requirement_is_reported_first() -> None:
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)), _config(budget=100))
    top = report.defects[0]
    assert (top.type, top.location) == ("hidden", ("c1",)) and top.scenarios == ("grn12",)
    assert top.note == "refuted by w" and top.subtype
    assert report.theta["q1"] > report.theta["q2"]


def test_an_agreeing_pilot_on_known_ground_does_not_escalate() -> None:
    runner = FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)
    run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(runner), _config(budget=100))
    assert runner.calls.count("discount8") == 9 and runner.calls.count("grn12") == 9
    assert runner.calls.count("happy") == 2


def test_the_budget_is_respected() -> None:
    runner = FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(runner), _config(budget=5))
    assert report.runs <= 5 and runner.executed <= 5


def test_the_loop_refuses_a_context_without_cells() -> None:
    ctx = dataclasses.replace(_ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)), cells=None)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="cell lens"):
        run_specdx(VARIANT, ctx, _config(budget=10))


def _split_plan(scenario, interp):
    """grn12: the accurate models read the step two ways, q1 and l1 opposite to each other
    and q2 both ways and wrong once, so the pilot already disagrees."""
    if scenario.id == "grn12":
        if interp.model == "q2":
            return ("X", "E", "Y")[interp.seed - 1], set()
        first = "X" if interp.model == "q1" else "Y"
        return (first if interp.seed != 2 else ("Y" if first == "X" else "X")), set()
    good = interp.model in GOOD
    return ("OK", set()) if good else (f"E{interp.seed}-{scenario.id}", {"n"})


def _split_ctx(runner, localizer=None, lens=None, refuter=None):
    return _ctx(runner, localizer, lens, refuter, anchored=frozenset())


def test_ambiguity_locations_are_replaced_by_the_localiser() -> None:
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan),
                                            lambda v, d, i, b, p: ("A:01",)),
                        _config(budget=200))
    assert any(d.type == "ambiguity" and d.location == ("A:01",) for d in report.defects)


def test_an_unlocalised_ambiguity_keeps_its_type_with_an_empty_location() -> None:
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan),
                                            lambda v, d, i, b, p: None),
                        _config(budget=200))
    ambiguities = [d for d in report.defects if d.type == "ambiguity"]
    assert ambiguities and all(d.location == () for d in ambiguities)


def test_the_base_reports_locations_are_refuted_last_on_a_mutant(monkeypatch) -> None:
    config = _config(budget=100)
    base = run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)), config)
    assert ("c1",) in {d.location for d in base.defects}
    assert isinstance(base.spent, frozenset) and ("c1",) not in base.spent
    seen = []
    real = run_specdx.__globals__["diagnose_v3"]

    def recording(*args, known=frozenset(), **kwargs):
        seen.append(known)
        return real(*args, known=known, **kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setitem(run_specdx.__globals__, "diagnose_v3", recording)
    run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)), config, base=base)
    assert seen and all(("c1",) in known for known in seen)
    # Symbolic repair spent no probe on the base, so a mutant needing one probes it early;
    # ambiguity and weak locations are no refutation candidates at all.
    seen.clear()
    noted = dataclasses.replace(base, defects=[
        Defect("contradiction", ("a",), ("s",), (), 1.0),
        Defect("hidden", ("b",), ("s",), (), 1.0, UNATTEMPTED),
        Defect("hidden", ("c",), ("s",), (), 1.0, "refuted by symbolic-repair"),
        Defect("hidden", ("d",), ("s",), (), 1.0, "refuted by grn12|q1|v0|s1"),
        Defect("ambiguity", ("e",), ("s",), (), 1.0),
        Defect("weak", ("f",), ("s",), (), 1.0),
    ], spent=frozenset({("g", "h")}))  # a multi-clause candidate that cost a probe
    run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)), config, base=noted)
    assert seen and all(known == {("a",), ("b",), ("d",), ("g", "h")} for known in seen)
    assert json.loads(noted.to_json())["spent"] == [["g", "h"]]  # a reloaded base keeps it


def test_a_base_noise_ambiguity_is_removed_before_localisation() -> None:
    config = _config(budget=200)
    base = run_specdx(VARIANT, _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan)),
                      dataclasses.replace(config, localize=False))
    assert [d for d in base.defects if d.type == "ambiguity"]
    assert base.ambiguity_keys == {("ambiguity", "step", ("x.k",))}
    calls = []

    def localiser(v, d, i, b, p):
        calls.append(d.note)
        return (f"A:{len(calls):02d}",)  # another run, other sentence ids

    mutant = run_specdx(VARIANT, _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan), localiser), config,
                        base=base)
    assert not [d for d in mutant.defects if d.type == "ambiguity"] and calls == []
    alone = run_specdx(VARIANT, _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan), localiser), config)
    assert [d.location for d in alone.defects if d.type == "ambiguity"] == [("A:01",)]


class TwoStepLens(FakeLens):
    """The step under test twice: the same split at two steps of one scenario."""

    def views(self, b, violated):
        out = super().views(b, violated)
        return [*out, dataclasses.replace(out[0], item=f"{b.scenario.id}|step2")]


def test_localised_ambiguities_that_share_a_location_are_merged() -> None:
    report = run_specdx(
        VARIANT,
        _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan), lambda v, d, i, b, p: ("A:01",), lens=TwoStepLens()),
        _config(budget=200),
    )
    assert len(report.ambiguity_keys) == 2
    assert [d.location for d in report.defects if d.type == "ambiguity"] == [("A:01",)]


def test_unlocalised_ambiguities_at_different_steps_stay_apart() -> None:
    report = run_specdx(
        VARIANT,
        _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan), lambda v, d, i, b, p: None, lens=TwoStepLens()),
        _config(budget=200),
    )
    unlocalised = [d for d in report.defects if d.type == "ambiguity"]
    assert [d.location for d in unlocalised] == [(), ()]
    assert sorted(d.note.split("|")[1] for d in unlocalised) == ["step", "step2"]


def test_the_report_records_the_ambiguities_the_differential_dropped() -> None:
    config = _config(budget=200)
    base = run_specdx(VARIANT, _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan)),
                      dataclasses.replace(config, localize=False))
    mutant = run_specdx(VARIANT, _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan), lens=TwoStepLens()),
                        config, base=base)
    assert mutant.ambiguity_keys == {("ambiguity", "step", ("x.k",)),
                                     ("ambiguity", "step2", ("x.k",))}
    assert mutant.ambiguity_dropped == {("ambiguity", "step", ("x.k",))}


def test_an_unattempted_singleton_is_hidden_and_an_attempted_one_a_contradiction() -> None:
    def run(probe_budget):
        return run_specdx(
            VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan), refuter=lambda i, b: NeverRefute(b)),
            _config(budget=100, probe_budget=probe_budget),
        )

    capped = run(0)
    top = capped.defects[0]
    assert (top.type, top.location) == ("hidden", ("c1",)) and "budget" in top.note
    assert capped.probe_runs == 0
    attempted = run(10)
    assert (attempted.defects[0].type, attempted.defects[0].location) == (
        "contradiction", ("c1",))
    # one candidate, refuted once per specification however many iterations see it
    assert attempted.probe_runs == 1


def _ddmin_localiser(calls, runs=3):
    """Spends `runs` ddmin runs per ambiguity, like the real one; unlocalised if cut short."""
    def localiser(v, d, i, budget, persists):
        calls.append(d.note)
        for _ in range(runs):
            if not budget.take("ddmin"):
                return None
        return ("A:01",)
    return localiser


def _split_and_shared_plan(scenario, interp):
    """grn12: the split, and seed 2 violates n where the other seeds violate c1, so the
    scenario has an ambiguity to localise and a two-clause candidate to refute (no single
    clause hits every violation set)."""
    klass, bad = _split_plan(scenario, interp)
    if scenario.id == "grn12":
        return klass, {"n"} if interp.seed == 2 else {"c1"}
    return klass, bad


def test_the_report_counts_probe_and_ddmin_runs() -> None:
    report = run_specdx(
        VARIANT,
        _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_and_shared_plan), _ddmin_localiser([]),
                   refuter=lambda i, b: NeverRefute(b)),
        _config(budget=200, probe_budget=4, localize_budget=5),
    )
    assert (report.probe_runs, report.ddmin_runs) == (1, 3)
    out = json.loads(report.to_json())
    assert (out["runs"], out["probe_runs"], out["ddmin_runs"]) == (
        report.runs, report.probe_runs, report.ddmin_runs)


def test_refutation_spending_its_whole_budget_leaves_localisation_its_own() -> None:
    calls: list[str] = []
    report = run_specdx(
        VARIANT,
        _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_and_shared_plan), _ddmin_localiser(calls),
                   refuter=lambda i, b: NeverRefute(b)),
        _config(budget=200, probe_budget=1, localize_budget=80),
    )
    assert report.probe_runs == 1  # the probe budget is spent
    assert calls and report.ddmin_runs == 3
    assert [d.location for d in report.defects if d.type == "ambiguity"] == [("A:01",)]


def test_the_localisation_budget_caps_ddmin_in_descending_score_order() -> None:
    def run(localize_budget):
        calls: list[str] = []
        report = run_specdx(
            VARIANT,
            _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan), _ddmin_localiser(calls), lens=TwoStepLens()),
            _config(budget=200, localize_budget=localize_budget),
        )
        found = sorted(d.location for d in report.defects if d.type == "ambiguity")
        return report, calls, found

    full, calls, found = run(6)
    assert full.ddmin_runs == 6 and len(calls) == 2 and found == [("A:01",)]
    cut, calls, found = run(4)  # the second localisation runs out after one run
    assert cut.ddmin_runs == 4 and len(calls) == 2 and found == [(), ("A:01",)]
    spent, calls, found = run(3)  # nothing left for the second: it is not even tried
    assert spent.ddmin_runs == 3 and len(calls) == 1 and found == [(), ("A:01",)]
    score = {d.location: d.score for d in spent.defects if d.type == "ambiguity"}
    assert score[("A:01",)] >= score[()]  # the better supported one went first


def test_t_boundary_scenarios_run_after_the_canon_ones_and_before_the_bandit() -> None:
    runner = FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)
    t1, t2 = Scenario("o15-r12-d7-g1", 15, 12, 7), Scenario("o15-r12-d9-g1", 15, 12, 9)
    extra = Scenario("o16-r14-d4-g2", 16, 14, 4, 2)  # a cell no queued scenario tried
    ctx = dataclasses.replace(_ctx(runner), candidates=[*CANON.values(), t1, extra],
                              tboundary=[t1, t2])
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, ctx, _config(budget=200, synthesize=True))
    assert report.scenarios[:6] == ["discount8", "grn12", "happy", t1.id, t2.id, extra.id]


def test_a_queued_scenario_whose_signature_was_visited_is_skipped() -> None:
    runner = FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)
    twin = Scenario("twin", 15, 12, 0)  # grn12's parameters under another id
    ctx = dataclasses.replace(_ctx(runner), tboundary=[twin])
    config = _config(budget=100)
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, ctx, config)
    assert report.scenarios == ["discount8", "grn12", "happy"] and "twin" not in runner.calls
    plain = run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)), config)
    assert report.runs == plain.runs  # the skipped twin spent no population budget


def test_the_report_carries_stage_one_the_cells_and_the_literals() -> None:
    literal = TLiteral("discount_pct", 5, "%", "tối đa", "KB", "KB:KB-0003", "KB:KB-0003:03", 9)
    ctx = dataclasses.replace(_ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)), literals=[literal])
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, ctx, _config(budget=100))
    assert set(report.kappa) == set(report.theta) == set(report.alpha) == {"q1", "q2", "l1"}
    item = report.items["grn12|step"]
    assert item["observable"] and set(item) == {"delta", "observability", "acted", "observable"}
    cell = report.cells["grn12|step"]
    assert cell["anchor"] == "ok" and cell["readings"] == {"l1": 3, "q1": 3, "q2": 3}
    assert set(cell["forms"]) == {"none", "mis", "amb", "empty"} and cell["map"] == ["k"]
    # §3.5: the posterior over A_c, most probable first; it sums to 1
    assert cell["sets"][0] == [["k"], cell["map_posterior"]] and len(cell["sets"]) <= 22
    assert sum(p for _, p in cell["sets"]) == pytest.approx(1.0)
    out = json.loads(report.to_json())
    assert out["literals"] == [literal.as_dict()] and out["unobservable"] == []
    assert set(out["items"]) == set(report.items) and out["kappa"] == report.kappa
    assert out["cells"] == report.cells and out["alpha"] == report.alpha
    assert out["converged"] is True and out["iterations"] >= 1 and out["fit_start"]
    # the family effect the pre-registration promises: one value per family, in the report
    assert out["family_effect"] == report.family_effect
    assert set(report.family_effect) == {"qwen", "llama"}


def test_non_convergence_surfaces_in_the_report_json(monkeypatch) -> None:
    real = run_specdx.__globals__["fit_anchored"]

    def one_step(*args, **kwargs):
        return real(*args, **kwargs, max_iter=1)

    monkeypatch.setitem(run_specdx.__globals__, "fit_anchored", one_step)
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)), _config(budget=100))
    out = json.loads(report.to_json())
    assert out["converged"] is False and out["iterations"] == 1


def test_identical_input_gives_a_byte_identical_report() -> None:
    one = run_specdx(VARIANT, _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan), lambda v, d, i, b, p: None),
                     _config(budget=200))
    two = run_specdx(VARIANT, _split_ctx(FakeRunner(_split_plan), lambda v, d, i, b, p: None),
                     _config(budget=200))
    assert one.to_json() == two.to_json()


class IdleLens(FakeLens):
    """No one acts at the step under test: it is never observable."""

    def views(self, b, violated):
        out = super().views(b, violated)
        return [dataclasses.replace(v, acted=not v.item.endswith("|step")) for v in out]


def test_an_item_nobody_acts_at_is_unobservable_and_types_nothing() -> None:
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan), lens=IdleLens()),
                        _config(budget=100))
    assert not [d for d in report.defects if "c1" in d.location]
    assert report.items["grn12|step"]["observable"] is False
    assert "grn12|step" not in report.cells


def test_the_diagnosis_unobservable_list_reaches_the_report_and_its_json(monkeypatch) -> None:
    real = run_specdx.__globals__["diagnose_v3"]
    entry = Defect("unobservable", ("c1",), ("grn12",), (), 1.0, "no observable item reads it")

    def wrapped(*args, **kwargs):
        return dataclasses.replace(real(*args, **kwargs), unobservable=[entry])

    monkeypatch.setitem(run_specdx.__globals__, "diagnose_v3", wrapped)
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan)), _config(budget=100))
    assert report.unobservable == [entry]
    [row] = json.loads(report.to_json())["unobservable"]
    assert (row["type"], row["location"], row["scenarios"]) == ("unobservable", ["c1"], ["grn12"])


def test_the_ddmin_oracle_reruns_every_acting_model_and_tests_the_cell() -> None:
    seen = []

    def localiser(v, d, interps, budget, persists):
        seen.append((list(interps), persists))
        return ("A:01",)

    runner = FakeRunner(_split_plan)
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, _split_ctx(runner, localiser),
                        _config(variants=(0, 1), budget=400))
    assert [d.location for d in report.defects if d.type == "ambiguity"] == [("A:01",)]
    interps, persists = seen[0]
    assert {(i.model, i.variant, i.seed) for i in interps} == {
        (m, v, 1) for m in ("q1", "q2", "l1") for v in (0, 1)}
    thetas = [report.theta[i.model] for i in interps]
    assert thetas == sorted(thetas, reverse=True)  # highest θ_m first
    # the test re-reads the ambiguity's cell at the fitted parameters
    rerun = [runner.run(VARIANT, CANON["grn12"], i, "ddmin") for i in interps]
    assert persists(rerun) is True
    agree = [dataclasses.replace(b, state=State({}, {"k": "X", "v": ""}, {})) for b in rerun]
    assert persists(agree) is False


def test_oracle_interpreters_are_the_acting_models_at_seed_one() -> None:
    interps = oracle_interpreters({"a": ("m1", "m2"), "b": ("m3",)},
                                  {"m1": 0.6, "m2": 0.4, "m3": 0.9}, (0, 2),
                                  acted={"m1", "m2", "m3"})
    assert [(i.model, i.family, i.variant, i.seed) for i in interps] == [
        ("m3", "b", 0, 1), ("m3", "b", 2, 1), ("m1", "a", 0, 1), ("m1", "a", 2, 1),
        ("m2", "a", 0, 1), ("m2", "a", 2, 1)]


def test_a_model_that_never_acted_is_left_out_of_the_oracle_whatever_its_theta() -> None:
    interps = oracle_interpreters({"a": ("idle", "acting", "weak")},
                                  {"idle": 0.95, "acting": 0.6, "weak": 0.1}, (0,),
                                  acted={"acting", "weak"})
    assert [i.model for i in interps] == ["acting", "weak"]


class SilentLens(FakeLens):
    """A population that acts nowhere: every step fails or writes nothing."""

    def views(self, b, violated):
        return [dataclasses.replace(v, acted=False) for v in super().views(b, violated)]


def test_a_population_that_never_acts_types_nothing_and_still_reports() -> None:
    report = run_specdx(VARIANT, _ctx(FakeRunner(_hidden_plan), lens=SilentLens()),
                        _config(budget=100))
    assert report.defects == [] and report.runs > 0 and report.cells == {}
    prior = 1 / (1 + 2.718281828459045 ** -P.mu_alpha)
    assert report.theta == pytest.approx({"q1": prior, "q2": prior, "l1": prior})
    assert not any(row["observable"] for row in report.items.values())
    assert json.loads(report.to_json())["defects"] == []
```

```python
class _Cells:
    """The constraint cell c1, and three anchored cells every behaviour reads at its
    anchor: they are what tells the fit that the models are accurate."""

    def extract(self, behaviours):
        ok = [b for b in behaviours if b.status == "ok"]
        cells = {"c1": Cell("c1", "constraint", SATISFIED, "c1", "c1", "invariant", ("s",))}
        cells |= {f"k{j}": Cell(f"k{j}", "value", "ok", f"k{j}", f"t.k{j}", f"t.k{j}", ("s",))
                  for j in range(3)}
        readings = [Reading("c1", b.run_key, b.interpreter.model, b.interpreter.family,
                            b.scenario.id, VIOLATED if b.state.derived.get("v") else SATISFIED)
                    for b in ok]
        readings += [Reading(f"k{j}", b.run_key, b.interpreter.model, b.interpreter.family,
                             b.scenario.id, "ok") for b in ok for j in range(3)]
        return CellData(cells if ok else {}, readings)


class _Refute:
    def refute(self, targets, scenario, behaviours):
```

```python
def _split_oracle(tmp_path, monkeypatch, interps):
    """The workbench's ddmin oracle (split_persists) on a fake runner and lens: each model
    answers with the views scripted in `scripted`. Returns (persists, budget, scripted)."""
    from specdx.active.budget import RunBudget
    from specdx.infer.items import ItemView
    from specdx.types import Defect

    scripted: dict[str, list[ItemView]] = {}
    note = "grn12|DE-OP-02:record_goods_receipt"

    class Runner:
        executed = 0

        def run(self, probe, scenario, interp, suffix="", *, kind="population"):
            return interp.model

        def run_batch(self, requests):
            return [self.run(r.variant, r.scenario, r.interp, r.suffix, kind=r.kind)
                    for r in requests]

    class Lens:
        def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs) -> None: ...

        def violations(self, b):
            return frozenset()

        def views(self, b, violated):
            return scripted[b]

        def diff(self, a, b):
            return frozenset(f for f in dict(a) if dict(a)[f] != dict(b).get(f))

    captured = []
    monkeypatch.setattr("specdx.workbench.SpecLens", Lens)
    monkeypatch.setattr("specdx.workbench.localize_ambiguity",
                        lambda corpus, pool, test, max_tests=None: captured.append(test))
```

with:

```python
def _split_oracle(tmp_path, monkeypatch, interps):
    """The workbench's ddmin oracle (split_persists) on a fake runner: it charges and
    dispatches one re-run per interpreter, as one batch, and asks the loop's test. Returns
    (the test, the budget, the batches dispatched, the runs the loop's test saw)."""
    from specdx.active.budget import RunBudget
    from specdx.types import Defect

    batches: list[list] = []
    seen: list[list] = []

    class Runner:
        executed = 0

        def run_batch(self, requests):
            batches.append([(r.interp.model, r.kind, r.scenario.id, r.variant.name)
                            for r in requests])
            return [r.interp.model for r in requests]

    captured = []
    monkeypatch.setattr("specdx.workbench.localize_ambiguity",
                        lambda corpus, pool, test, max_tests=None: captured.append(test))
```

```python
    return (lambda corpus: test(corpus)), budget, scripted, view


def test_the_ddmin_oracle_counts_only_acted_splits_on_the_ambiguitys_fields(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    interps = [InterpreterId("m1", "f", 0, 1), InterpreterId("m2", "f", 0, 1)]
    persists, budget, scripted, view = _split_oracle(tmp_path, monkeypatch, interps)
    corpus = None
    scripted["m1"], scripted["m2"] = [view("m1", "A")], [view("m2", "B")]
    assert persists(corpus) is True and budget.ddmin == 2
    # a difference on another field is not this ambiguity's split
    scripted["m1"], scripted["m2"] = [view("m1", "A", field="y.z")], [view("m2", "B", field="y.z")]
    assert persists(corpus) is False
    # a behaviour that did not act yields no class
    scripted["m1"], scripted["m2"] = [view("m1", "A")], [view("m2", "B", acted=False)]
    assert persists(corpus) is False
    # another item's views are not this ambiguity's either
    scripted["m1"], scripted["m2"] = [view("m1", "A")], [view("m2", "B", item="other|step")]
    assert persists(corpus) is False


def test_an_empty_oracle_leaves_the_ambiguity_unlocalised_and_spends_nothing(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    persists, budget, _, _ = _split_oracle(tmp_path, monkeypatch, [])
    assert persists(None) is False and budget.ddmin == 0
```

```python
    ctx = bench.loop_context(bench.base, SpecDxConfig({"ref": ("reference",)}))
    budget = RunBudget(4, 10)
    defect = Defect("ambiguity", ("po.trang_thai",), ("grn12",), (), 1.0,
                    "grn12|DE-FN-04:draft_purchase_order")

    def persists(runs):
        seen.append(list(runs))
        return len(set(runs)) >= 2

    assert ctx.localizer(bench.base, defect, interps, budget, persists) is None

def test_the_ddmin_oracle_reruns_each_interpreter_and_asks_the_loops_test(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    interps = [InterpreterId("m1", "f", 0, 1), InterpreterId("m2", "f", 0, 1)]
    test, budget, batches, seen = _split_oracle(tmp_path, monkeypatch, interps)
    assert test(None) is True and budget.ddmin == 2
    assert batches == [[("m1", "ddmin", "grn12", "base+ddmin"),
                        ("m2", "ddmin", "grn12", "base+ddmin")]]
    assert seen == [["m1", "m2"]]


def test_an_empty_oracle_leaves_the_ambiguity_unlocalised_and_spends_nothing(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    test, budget, batches, seen = _split_oracle(tmp_path, monkeypatch, [])
    assert test(None) is False and budget.ddmin == 0 and batches == [] and seen == []
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Run them to verify they fail**

Run: `.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/active/test_loop.py tests/eval tests/test_workbench.py tests/test_preregistration.py`
Expected: errors and failures, among them `TypeError: LoopContext.__init__() got an unexpected keyword argument 'cells'`, `TypeError: GateConfig.__init__() got an unexpected keyword argument 'params'`, `ImportError: cannot import name 'rescreen'`, `assert 0.6 == 0.3`, and the four-model stop test (a 3-model population does not stop yet).

- [ ] **Step 4: The loop**

The whole file, since most of `run_specdx` and its report change: cells and the anchored fit replace stage 2, `diagnose_v3` replaces `diagnose`, and the oracle gets the loop's test.

<!-- apply: create specdx/active/loop.py -->
```python
"""The SpecDx main loop (spec §3.8; v2 §3.8; v3 §5–§6).

Per iteration: pilot, escalation, stage 1 (act), then the anchored cell model on the
cells read so far, then the v3 diagnosis. Escalation still compares the acted views' class
keys (v2 §3.8); the evidence about the specification comes from cells only.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
import random
from collections.abc import Callable, Collection, Hashable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Protocol

from specdx.active.budget import RunBudget
from specdx.active.refute import SYMBOLIC_WITNESS
from specdx.active.search import CellBandit, information_gain, should_escalate
from specdx.infer.act import Act, Rasch, fit_rasch
from specdx.infer.anchored import AnchoredFit, V3Params, fit_anchored, posterior_at
from specdx.infer.cells import CellData
from specdx.infer.items import ItemView, acted_violations
from specdx.infer.scores import ambiguity_key, identity, merge_defects
from specdx.infer.typing import CellDiagnosis, diagnose_v3
from specdx.interpreters.population import committee, pilot
from specdx.observe.run import RunRequest
from specdx.scenarios.tguided import TLiteral
from specdx.spec.columns import Projection
from specdx.types import Behaviour, Defect, InterpreterId, Refuter, RefuteResult, Scenario
from specdx.variant import SpecVariant


class RunnerLike(Protocol):
    executed: int

    def run(
        self, variant: SpecVariant, scenario: Scenario, interp: InterpreterId, suffix: str = "",
        *, kind: str = "population",
    ) -> Behaviour: ...

    def run_batch(self, requests: Sequence[RunRequest]) -> list[Behaviour]: ...


class LensLike(Protocol):
    relevant: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]]

    def violations(self, behaviour: Behaviour) -> frozenset[str]: ...

    def views(self, behaviour: Behaviour, violated: frozenset[str]) -> list[ItemView]: ...

    def acts(self, behaviour: Behaviour) -> list[Act]: ...

    def diff(self, a: Projection, b: Projection) -> frozenset[str]: ...


class CellsLike(Protocol):
    def extract(self, behaviours: Sequence[Behaviour]) -> CellData: ...


# The ddmin oracle's statistical test on one test's re-runs (spec v3 §5.3).
Persists = Callable[[Sequence[Behaviour]], bool]
# The localised sentence ids, or None when the ambiguity stays unlocalised.
Localizer = Callable[
    [SpecVariant, Defect, Sequence[InterpreterId], RunBudget, Persists], tuple[str, ...] | None
]
AmbiguityKey = tuple[str, str, tuple[str, ...]]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SpecDxConfig:
    models_by_family: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]
    variants: tuple[int, ...] = (0,)
    seeds: tuple[int, ...] = (1, 2, 3)
    budget: int = 300
    tau: float = 0.05
    eps: float = 0.01
    k_max: int = 6
    rng_seed: int = 20261006
    synthesize: bool = True
    localize: bool = True
    localize_tests: int = 40
    # Runs requested per specification, cached or not (§11): directed probes for
    # refutation, and ddmin runs for localisation, each under its own cap.
    probe_budget: int = 100
    localize_budget: int = 80
    # The anchored model's priors and thresholds (spec v3 §9).
    params: V3Params = V3Params()


@dataclass
class LoopContext:
    """tboundary: the T-boundary scenarios, queued after the canon ones; literals: the T
    literals they come from, reported per specification (spec v2 §3.7). cells: the cell
    lens the v3 diagnosis reads (infer.cells.CellLens); the loop refuses to run without."""

    runner: RunnerLike
    lens: LensLike
    canon: dict[str, Scenario]
    candidates: list[Scenario]
    cap_pct: int
    refuter_factory: Callable[[Sequence[InterpreterId], RunBudget], Refuter]
    localizer: Localizer | None = None
    tboundary: list[Scenario] = field(default_factory=list)
    literals: list[TLiteral] = field(default_factory=list)
    cells: CellsLike | None = None


@dataclass
class Report:
    """runs counts population runs; probe_runs and ddmin_runs the requested runs charged to
    the probe and localisation budgets. ambiguity_keys are the ambiguities found, before
    the differential and localisation; ambiguity_dropped those the differential removed.
    The v2 additions (spec v2 §3.8): κ per model; per item δ, O_s and whether it is
    observable; the Unobservable list; the T literals. The v3 additions (spec v3 §3.5,
    §5.5): θ_m (theta) and α_m (alpha) of the anchored model, the family effect on the
    logit scale, the per-cell posterior summaries, and whether EM converged (converged,
    iterations, the winning start) — non-convergence is reported, never silent."""

    variant: str
    defects: list[Defect]
    runs: int
    executed: int
    scenarios: list[str]
    theta: dict[str, float]
    behaviours: list[Behaviour] = field(default_factory=list, repr=False)
    probe_runs: int = 0
    ddmin_runs: int = 0
    ambiguity_keys: frozenset[AmbiguityKey] = frozenset()
    ambiguity_dropped: frozenset[AmbiguityKey] = frozenset()  # base noise, removed
    # Multi-clause candidate locations that cost a probe run, or would have, and found
    # nothing (resolution 8; CellDiagnosis.spent).
    spent: frozenset[tuple[str, ...]] = frozenset()
    kappa: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    family_effect: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    items: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    unobservable: list[Defect] = field(default_factory=list)
    literals: list[TLiteral] = field(default_factory=list)
    alpha: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    cells: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    converged: bool = True
    iterations: int = 0
    fit_start: str = ""

    def to_json(self) -> str:
        return json.dumps(
            {"variant": self.variant, "defects": [asdict(d) for d in self.defects],
             "unobservable": [asdict(d) for d in self.unobservable],
             "runs": self.runs, "probe_runs": self.probe_runs, "ddmin_runs": self.ddmin_runs,
             "executed": self.executed, "scenarios": self.scenarios, "theta": self.theta,
             "alpha": self.alpha, "kappa": self.kappa, "family_effect": self.family_effect,
             "converged": self.converged, "iterations": self.iterations,
             "fit_start": self.fit_start, "items": self.items, "cells": self.cells,
             "literals": [x.as_dict() for x in self.literals],
             "spent": [list(loc) for loc in sorted(self.spent)]},
            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2,
        )


def item_table(rasch: Rasch) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Per item, stage 1: δ, O_s, acted behaviours, observable."""
    return {item: {"delta": rasch.delta[item], "observability": rasch.observability[item],
                   "acted": rasch.acted[item], "observable": rasch.observable(item)}
            for item in sorted(rasch.delta)}


def cell_table(data: CellData, fit: AnchoredFit) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Per cell (spec v3.1 §3.5, §5.5): its anchor, the readings per model, β_{g(c)}, the
    posterior over A_c (sets: at most 22 [sorted set, p] pairs, most probable first), the
    form masses, the MAP set and its posterior."""
    counts: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
    for r in data.readings:
        mine = counts.setdefault(r.cell, {})
        mine[r.model] = mine.get(r.model, 0) + 1
    out: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for cid, post in sorted(fit.cells.items()):
        best = post.map_set
        sets = sorted(([sorted(s), p] for s, p in post.sets.items()),
                      key=lambda x: (-x[1], x[0]))
        out[cid] = {"anchor": post.anchor, "readings": dict(sorted(counts[cid].items())),
                    "beta": post.beta, "sets": sets, "forms": post.forms, "map": sorted(best),
                    "map_posterior": post.sets[best]}
    return out


class _Memoised:
    """One refutation per scenario and target set for the whole specification: the loop
    builds a fresh refuter every iteration, and repeated requests would drain the budget."""

    def __init__(self, refuter: Refuter, memo: dict) -> None:
        self.refuter, self.memo = refuter, memo

    def refute(
        self, targets: frozenset[str], scenario: Scenario, behaviours: Sequence[Behaviour]
    ) -> RefuteResult:
        key = (scenario.signature(), targets)
        if key not in self.memo:
            self.memo[key] = self.refuter.refute(targets, scenario, behaviours)
        result: RefuteResult = self.memo[key]
        return result


def ranked_interpreters(
    models_by_family: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]],
    theta: dict[str, float],
    variants: tuple[int, ...],
) -> list[InterpreterId]:
    """Every model once, most competent first: the order the refuter tries them in."""
    models = [(m, f) for f, ms in models_by_family.items() for m in ms]
    models.sort(key=lambda mf: -theta.get(mf[0], 0.0))
    return [InterpreterId(m, f, variants[0], 1) for m, f in models]


def oracle_interpreters(
    models_by_family: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]],
    theta: dict[str, float],
    variants: tuple[int, ...],
    acted: Collection[str],
) -> list[InterpreterId]:
    """The ddmin oracle's re-runs (spec v3 §5.3): one per model with at least one acted
    behaviour in the specification and per Tell variant, at seed 1, highest θ_m first. No θ
    threshold: a model that never acted cannot yield a reading, whatever its θ."""
    models = [(m, f) for f, ms in models_by_family.items() for m in ms if m in acted]
    models.sort(key=lambda mf: -theta.get(mf[0], 0.0))
    return [InterpreterId(m, f, v, 1) for m, f in models for v in variants]


def run_specdx(
    variant: SpecVariant, ctx: LoopContext, config: SpecDxConfig, *, base: Report | None = None
) -> Report:
    """base: the base specification's report. Ambiguities it already holds (same step and
    fields) are dropped before localisation, so only the mutant's own are localised, and
    candidates at locations it already holds are refuted last (§11)."""
    if ctx.cells is None:
        raise ValueError("the v3 loop reads cells: the loop context has no cell lens")
    cells = ctx.cells
    rng = random.Random(config.rng_seed)
    budget = RunBudget(config.probe_budget, config.localize_budget)
    refutations: dict = {}
    bandit = CellBandit(ctx.cap_pct)
    # Canon scenarios, then T-boundary scenarios, then the bandit (spec v2 §3.8).
    queue = [ctx.canon[name] for name in sorted(ctx.canon)] + list(ctx.tboundary)
    visited: set[tuple[int, ...]] = set()
    behaviours: list[Behaviour] = []
    violated: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {}
    views: list[ItemView] = []
    acts: list[Act] = []
    seen_sets: set[frozenset[str]] = set()
    population = {m: f for f, ms in config.models_by_family.items() for m in ms}
    data = CellData({}, [])
    model = fit_anchored({}, [], models=population, params=config.params)
    rasch = fit_rasch([])
    diagnosis = CellDiagnosis([])
    defects: list[Defect] = []
    spent: frozenset[tuple[str, ...]] = frozenset()
    previous: dict[str, float] = {}
    order: list[str] = []
    runs = 0
    executed_before = ctx.runner.executed
    members = committee(config.models_by_family, config.variants, config.seeds)
    # Base locations whose candidates spent probes, or would: contradictions, and hidden
    # findings refuted by a probe run or never attempted. A base finding that symbolic
    # repair refuted cost no probe; if it needs one on a mutant, the mutant changed it.
    known = (frozenset(d.location for d in base.defects
                       if d.type == "contradiction"
                       or (d.type == "hidden" and d.note != f"refuted by {SYMBOLIC_WITNESS}"))
             | base.spent if base is not None else frozenset())

    while runs < config.budget:
        if queue:
            scenario = queue.pop(0)
            if scenario.signature() in visited:
                continue
        elif config.synthesize and not bandit.exhausted(ctx.candidates, visited, config.eps):
            chosen = bandit.choose(ctx.candidates, visited, rng)
            if chosen is None:
                break
            scenario = chosen
        else:
            break
        visited.add(scenario.signature())
        order.append(scenario.id)

        # Each batch is charged to the budget before it is dispatched (spec v2 §5.1).
        pilot_ids = pilot(config.models_by_family, model.theta if model.cells else None)
        batch = pilot_ids[:config.budget - runs]
        runs += len(batch)
        fresh = ctx.runner.run_batch([RunRequest(variant, scenario, i) for i in batch])
        seen = {b.run_key: ctx.lens.views(b, ctx.lens.violations(b)) for b in fresh}
        pilot_views = [v for b in fresh for v in seen[b.run_key]]
        ok_sets = [acted_violations(seen[b.run_key]) for b in fresh if b.status == "ok"]
        if should_escalate(pilot_views, ok_sets, seen_sets):
            batch = [i for i in members if i not in pilot_ids][:config.budget - runs]
            runs += len(batch)
            fresh += ctx.runner.run_batch([RunRequest(variant, scenario, i) for i in batch])

        for b in fresh:
            mine = ctx.lens.views(b, ctx.lens.violations(b))
            violated[b.run_key] = acted_violations(mine)
            if b.status == "ok":
                seen_sets.add(violated[b.run_key])
            behaviours.append(b)
            views.extend(mine)
            acts.extend(ctx.lens.acts(b))
        rasch = fit_rasch(acts)
        data = cells.extract(behaviours)
        model = fit_anchored(data.cells, data.readings, models=population,
                             params=config.params)
        refuter = _Memoised(ctx.refuter_factory(
            ranked_interpreters(config.models_by_family, model.theta, config.variants),
            budget,
        ), refutations)
        diagnosis = diagnose_v3(
            behaviours, violated, data, model, refuter,
            observable=rasch.observable_items(), relevant=ctx.lens.relevant,
            tau=config.tau, known=known,
        )
        defects, spent = diagnosis.defects, diagnosis.spent
        scores = {repr(identity(d)): d.score for d in defects}
        bandit.update(scenario, information_gain(previous, scores))
        previous = scores

    found = frozenset(ambiguity_key(d) for d in defects if d.type == "ambiguity")
    dropped = found & base.ambiguity_keys if base is not None else frozenset()
    defects = [d for d in defects if d.type != "ambiguity" or ambiguity_key(d) not in dropped]
    if config.localize and ctx.localizer is not None:
        interps = oracle_interpreters(config.models_by_family, model.theta, config.variants,
                                      {v.model for v in views if v.acted})
        # Best supported first, until the localisation budget runs out; from then on every
        # ambiguity is unlocalised: an empty location, which matches no ground truth.
        located: dict[int, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
        for i in sorted((i for i, d in enumerate(defects) if d.type == "ambiguity"),
                        key=lambda i: -defects[i].score):
            persists = _persists(cells, data, model,
                                 diagnosis.ambiguity_cells[ambiguity_key(defects[i])])
            sentences = (None if budget.exhausted("ddmin")
                         else ctx.localizer(variant, defects[i], interps, budget, persists))
            located[i] = tuple(sentences or ())
        # Localised defects that now share type and location merge; unlocalised ones keep
        # their identity from before localisation (step and fields), so they stay apart.
        merge_key: dict[int, Hashable] = {}
        out: list[Defect] = []
        for i, d in enumerate(defects):
            new = dataclasses.replace(d, location=located[i]) if i in located else d
            merge_key[id(new)] = ambiguity_key(d) if i in located and not located[i] else new.key
            out.append(new)
        defects = merge_defects(out, key=lambda d: merge_key[id(d)])

    return Report(variant.name, defects, runs, ctx.runner.executed - executed_before, order,
                  dict(model.theta), behaviours, probe_runs=budget.probe,
                  ddmin_runs=budget.ddmin, ambiguity_keys=found, ambiguity_dropped=dropped,
                  spent=spent, kappa=dict(rasch.kappa),
                  family_effect=dict(model.family_effect), items=item_table(rasch),
                  unobservable=diagnosis.unobservable, literals=list(ctx.literals),
                  alpha=dict(model.alpha), cells=cell_table(data, model),
                  converged=model.converged, iterations=model.iterations,
                  fit_start=model.start)


def _persists(cells: CellsLike, data: CellData, model: AnchoredFit, cell: str) -> Persists:
    """Spec v3 §5.3: a ddmin test passes while P(|A_c| = 2), computed on the re-run
    readings of the ambiguity's cell with α, β and ε held at their fitted values, stays at
    or above τ_A."""
    def persists(runs: Sequence[Behaviour]) -> bool:
        readings = [r for r in cells.extract(runs).readings if r.cell == cell]
        post = posterior_at(model, data.cells[cell], readings)
        return post.p_pair >= model.params.tau_a

    return persists
```

```python
            chunks = v.corpus.search(query, k=5)
            pool = v.corpus.sentence_ids([c.id for c in chunks])
            fields = frozenset(defect.location)

            def split_persists(corpus: TellCorpus) -> bool:
                """Spec v2 §3.6: the re-run (one behaviour per competent model and variant,
                seed 1) still yields two classes that differ on the ambiguity's fields. The
                test's runs are charged, then dispatched as one batch."""
                probe = SpecVariant(f"{v.name}+ddmin", v.pack, corpus)
                for _ in interps:
                    if not budget.take("ddmin"):
                        raise OutOfBudget
                runs = self.runner.run_batch(
                    [RunRequest(probe, scenario, i, kind="ddmin") for i in interps])
                seen = [view.projection for b in runs for view in lens.views(b, lens.violations(b))
                        if view.item == defect.note and view.acted]
                return any(lens.diff(a, b) & fields for a, b in combinations(seen, 2))

            found = localize_ambiguity(v.corpus, pool, split_persists,
                                       max_tests=config.localize_tests)
            return None if found is None else tuple(found)

        return LoopContext(self.runner, lens, canon, candidates, discount_cap_pct(pack),
                           refuter_factory, localizer, tboundary=tboundary, literals=literals)
```

with:

```python
            chunks = v.corpus.search(query, k=5)
            pool = v.corpus.sentence_ids([c.id for c in chunks])

            def split_persists(corpus: TellCorpus) -> bool:
                """Spec v3 §5.3: re-run each model that acted, under each Tell variant at
                seed 1, and ask the loop's test (persists) whether the cell still splits.
                The test's runs are charged, then dispatched as one batch."""
                probe = SpecVariant(f"{v.name}+ddmin", v.pack, corpus)
                for _ in interps:
                    if not budget.take("ddmin"):
                        raise OutOfBudget
                if not interps:
                    return False
                runs = self.runner.run_batch(
                    [RunRequest(probe, scenario, i, kind="ddmin") for i in interps])
                return persists(runs)

            found = localize_ambiguity(v.corpus, pool, split_persists,
                                       max_tests=config.localize_tests)
            return None if found is None else tuple(found)

        return LoopContext(self.runner, lens, canon, candidates, discount_cap_pct(pack),
                           refuter_factory, localizer, tboundary=tboundary, literals=literals,
                           cells=cells)
```

```python
def rescreen(path: Path) -> Screen:
    """The admission rules as they now stand, applied to a screen's recorded measurements
    (spec v3 §7: the 0.3 act rate on the cached P1b screen). No run is made."""
    raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    results = [ScreenResult(r["model"], r["family"], int(r["runs"]), float(r["act_rate"]),
                            float(r["valid_rate"]), str(r.get("note", "")))
               for r in raw["results"]]
    return Screen(results, choose_population(results))
```
