### Task 11: Calibration from base runs and the G0 runner (spec v3.1 §8.1–§8.2, §9)

**Files:**
- Create: `specdx/sim/calibrate.py`, `specdx/sim/g0.py`
- Create: `tests/sim/test_calibrate.py`, `tests/sim/test_g0.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3's `CellLens`, `Cell`; Task 6's `SimConfig`, `generate`; Task 7's `hidden_finding`, `ambiguity_finding`, `weak_finding`, `CellFinding`, `AMBIGUOUS_PICK`; Task 8's `score`, `baselines.by_cell`, `load_config`, `typed_counts`, `Counts`, `theta_error`, `METHODS`, `BASELINES`, `MARGIN`, `REGIME_ACCURACY`, `REGIME_DELTA`, `regime_config`; Task 10's `rescreen`; `interpreters.cache.run_key`, `interpreters.population.committee`, `observe.run.context_digest`, `workbench.tell_payload`.
- Produces:
  - `specdx/sim/calibrate.py`:
    - `BUDGET = 200`, `DEFECT_RATE = 0.15`, `CONCENTRATIONS`; `CalibrationError` (a broken mapping, or a statistic without data: no silent fallback, ruling F13);
    - `Calibration` (fields `population`, `act`, `accuracy`, `dispersion`, `top_error`, `error_conc`, `reads`, `pooled_share`, `anchored_share`, `constraint_share`, `values`, `columns`, `items`, `runs`, `simulated_reads`) with `.config(seed=0) -> SimConfig` and `.to_json()` (it holds `"config"` for `load_config`). Accuracy, its plurality and the dispersion use the population's readings only; the cell mix and |V| are shares of columns, as the generator draws them; `reads` (base runs) is reported beside `simulated_reads` (the same statistic on a specification generated from `config()`), since readings per cell follow from the planned budget (resolution 15; ruling F12);
    - the mapping shared with G1 (ruling F7): `SMOKE_RUNS = 60`, `P1_SCHEMA = 2`, `P1_SETTINGS`, `NoRefutation`, `gate_members(path)`, `smoke_runs(base, cache, digests) -> list[Behaviour]`;
    - `load_cache(path)`, `model_digests(cache)`, `Base` (`pack`, `variant`, `lens`, `cells`, `scenarios`, `steps_digest`, `context`), `open_base(pdf_cache: Path) -> Base`, `mapped(base, cache, digests, scenario, interp) -> Behaviour | None`, `screen_results(path)`, `base_runs(base, cache) -> list[Behaviour]`, `column_of(cell_id) -> str`, `calibrate(base, runs) -> Calibration`; `python -m specdx.sim.calibrate --out PATH` (exit 2 on a broken mapping or a missing statistic).
  - `specdx/sim/g0.py`:
    - the grid `PI`, `SHARES`, `BETA_SD = (1.0, 2.0)`, `ALPHA_PRIOR`, `TAU_H`, `TAU_A`, `TAU_W` (36 prior settings × 27 threshold settings; no κ); `ARMS`, `REPLICATES = 10`;
    - the criteria `THETA_ERROR_MAX = 0.05`, `F1_MIN = {"hidden": 0.6, "ambiguity": 0.4, "weak": 0.4}`, `AMBIGUITY_WIN = 0.05`; `PASS`, `FAIL` (no fallback);
    - `PARAMS_FILE` (`experiments/p2-g0-params.json`); `prior_settings(grid=None)`, `thresholds(grid=None)`, `conservative(params)`, `Candidate(params, objective, arms, nonconverged)` (non-converged fits per arm of its prior setting, ruling F2), `potential(spec, fit)`, `counts_at(spec, found, tau_h, tau_a, tau_w)` (typing's rules, `co_occur` included), `search(base, replicates, grid=None, progress=None)`, `choose(candidates)`;
    - `population_point(base) -> tuple[int, float, float]` (resolution 16), `evaluations(base) -> dict[str, SimConfig]` (calibrated, a3, a5, the population's own point), `holds(f1) -> dict[str, bool]`, `check(base, params, replicates, offset=0) -> dict` (keys `theta_error`, `f1`, `i`, `ii`, `iii`, `ambiguity_win`, `nonconverged`; seeds base.seed + offset + i), `verdict(criteria) -> str`;
    - `render(result)`, `run(calibration, out, *, replicates=REPLICATES, grid=None, params_out=PARAMS_FILE) -> str`: the criteria on the selection replicates decide the verdict; `p2-g0.json` and `.md` add `nonconverged` (search, chosen setting, criteria points) and `out_of_sample`, the criteria on the next `replicates` seeds, for information only (ruling F22); `frozen_block(params)`, `freeze(path, target=ANCHORED)`, `NAMES`, `params_table(params) -> str`; `python -m specdx.sim.g0 run|freeze|table` (run exits 0 PASS, 1 FAIL, 2 error).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

The calibration tests use the real base pack with behaviours standing in for cached runs; they never read `work/`. The concentration test asserts the monotone order of `_matching_concentration` (a higher top-error share gives a smaller concentration): ruling F8 asks for that line only if the function is monotone, and the plan's pre-check confirmed it is non-increasing over shares 0.30–1.00 in steps of 0.01. Two G0 tests are added: the threshold filter keeps an ambiguity and its hidden requirement together (ruling F1), and a run reports non-convergence and the out-of-sample check (rulings F2, F22).

<!-- apply: create tests/sim/test_calibrate.py -->
```python
"""Calibration from base runs (spec v3 §8.1), on the real base pack and without the run
cache: synthetic behaviours stand in for cached runs."""

import json
import math

import pytest
from tonomyx.canon.pack import CanonPack

from specdx.config import BASE_PACK
from specdx.db import golden_tables
from specdx.infer.cells import CellData
from specdx.orchestrate.work_orders import load_work_orders
from specdx.sim.calibrate import (
    Calibration,
    CalibrationError,
    _matching_concentration,
    _top_error,
    base_runs,
    calibrate,
    column_of,
    open_base,
)
from specdx.sim.study import load_config
from specdx.types import Behaviour, InterpreterId, State

SCREENED = {"gpt-oss:20b": ("openai", 0.9135802469135802),
            "qwen3:30b-a3b": ("qwen", 0.8024691358024691),
            "qwen2.5:14b": ("qwen", 0.5802469135802469),
            "mistral-small3.2:24b": ("mistral", 0.345679012345679)}


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def base(tmp_path_factory):
    return open_base(tmp_path_factory.mktemp("pdf"))


def test_the_configuration_uses_the_planned_budget_and_orders_models_by_accuracy(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    c = Calibration(population={"q": ("a", "b"), "o": ("c",), "m": ("d",)},
                    act={"a": 0.8, "b": 0.6, "c": 0.9, "d": 0.4},
                    accuracy={"a": 0.9, "b": 0.95, "c": 0.97, "d": 0.8}, dispersion=1.0,
                    top_error=0.8, error_conc=0.2, reads=6.0, pooled_share=0.2,
                    anchored_share=0.3, constraint_share=0.1,
                    values=(0.5, 0.3, 0.1, 0.05, 0.05, 0.0), columns=100, items=9, runs=105)
    config = c.config()
    assert config.scenarios == 200 // (4 * 9) == 5
    assert config.alphas == pytest.approx(
        tuple(math.log(p / (1 - p)) for p in (0.97, 0.95, 0.9, 0.8)))
    assert config.act == (0.9, 0.6, 0.8, 0.4) and config.defect_rate == 0.15
    path = tmp_path / "calibration.json"
    path.write_text(c.to_json(), encoding="utf-8")
    assert load_config(path) == config
    assert json.loads(path.read_text())["models"] == ["c", "b", "a", "d"]


def test_the_error_concentration_is_the_nearest_match_of_the_top_error_share() -> None:
    assert _matching_concentration(0.95) == 0.1
    assert _matching_concentration(0.4) >= 1.0
    # A concentrated error (a high top share) means a small concentration: the match falls
    # as the share rises (checked over 0.30–1.00 in steps of 0.01 at the plan's pre-check).
    assert (_matching_concentration(0.95) <= _matching_concentration(0.7)
            <= _matching_concentration(0.4))


def test_a_statistic_without_data_raises_instead_of_falling_back() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(CalibrationError, match="error concentration"):
        _top_error(CellData({}, []))
    assert column_of("grn12|po:PO-0015:sl_dat") == "po:PO-0015:sl_dat"
    assert column_of("golden:po:PO-0015:sl_dat") == "golden:po:PO-0015:sl_dat"


def test_calibration_reads_base_runs_through_the_cell_lens(base) -> None:
    tables = golden_tables(CanonPack(BASE_PACK))
    steps = {s.id: "done" for s in load_work_orders()}
    def state(m: str, v: int) -> State:
        copy = {t: [dict(r) for r in rows] for t, rows in tables.items()}
        if m == "mistral-small3.2:24b" and v < 2:  # two different errors at one cell
            copy["po"][0]["trang_thai"] = f"lỗi {v}"
        return State(copy, {"fn_reconcile:PO-0015": "Cần kiểm"}, steps)

    runs = [Behaviour(f"{m}|v{v}|s{s}", InterpreterId(m, f, v, s), base.scenarios["happy"],
                      state(m, v) if s == 1 else state(m, 2))
            for m, (f, _) in SCREENED.items() for v in (0, 1, 2) for s in (1, 2, 3)]
    c = calibrate(base, runs)
    assert c.population == {"openai": ("gpt-oss:20b",),
                            "qwen": ("qwen3:30b-a3b", "qwen2.5:14b"),
                            "mistral": ("mistral-small3.2:24b",)}
    assert c.act == {m: a for m, (_, a) in sorted(SCREENED.items())}
    assert all(a > 0.9 for a in c.accuracy.values()) and c.runs == 36
    assert c.dispersion == 0.25 and c.items == 9 and c.columns > 0
    assert sum(c.values) == pytest.approx(1.0) and c.top_error == 0.5
    # one happy scenario: 9 readings per model of a per-scenario cell, and the simulated
    # specification's readings per cell reported beside them (ruling F12)
    assert c.reads == 9.0 and c.simulated_reads > 0


def test_the_smoke_runs_must_all_map(base) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(CalibrationError, match="60 runs"):
        base_runs(base, {})
```

```python
"""G0's grid, selection rule, criteria, outputs and freeze (spec v3 §8.2, §9)."""

import dataclasses
import itertools
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from specdx.infer.anchored import AnchoredFit, CellPosterior, V3Params, fit_anchored
from specdx.infer.cells import Cell, Reading
from specdx.sim import g0
from specdx.sim.generator import SimConfig, Truth, generate
from specdx.sim.study import config_dict, v3_counts

TINY = SimConfig(alphas=(2.5, 1.0), act=(1.0, 0.8), columns=14, items=4, scenarios=2,
                 seeds=2, seed=5)
P = V3Params(pi=0.1, s_mis=0.4, s_amb=0.3, s_empty=0.3, beta_sd=1.0,
             mu_alpha=0.0, sigma_alpha=1.5, tau_h=0.5, tau_a=0.3, tau_w=0.6)


def test_the_grid_and_the_criteria_are_the_pre_registered_ones() -> None:
    assert len(g0.prior_settings()) == 36 and len(g0.thresholds()) == 27
    assert g0.BETA_SD == (1.0, 2.0) and "kappa" not in {f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(P)}
    assert (g0.REPLICATES, g0.ARMS) == (10, ("base", "a3", "a5"))
    assert g0.THETA_ERROR_MAX == 0.05 and g0.AMBIGUITY_WIN == 0.05
    assert g0.F1_MIN == {"hidden": 0.6, "ambiguity": 0.4, "weak": 0.4}
    assert all(abs(p.s_mis + p.s_amb + p.s_empty - 1) < 1e-12 for p in g0.prior_settings())


def test_ties_go_to_the_more_conservative_setting_then_grid_order() -> None:
    def candidate(pi, s_mis, objective):
        return g0.Candidate(dataclasses.replace(P, pi=pi, s_mis=s_mis), objective, {})

    loose, firm, firmer = candidate(0.2, 0.4, 0.5), candidate(0.1, 0.4, 0.5), candidate(
        0.1, 0.6, 0.5)
    assert g0.choose([loose, firm, firmer]) is firmer  # higher P(|A| = 1)
    same = candidate(0.1, 0.6, 0.5)
    assert g0.choose([firmer, same]) is firmer  # then grid order
    assert g0.choose([firmer, candidate(0.2, 0.2, 0.6)]).objective == 0.6
    assert g0.conservative(firm.params) < g0.conservative(loose.params)


def test_thresholds_filter_the_zero_threshold_findings_as_the_typing_core_types() -> None:
    spec = generate(TINY)
    fit = fit_anchored(spec.cells, spec.readings, models=spec.models, params=P)
    found = g0.potential(spec, fit)
    # (0.9, 0.3, 0.6): τ_A below τ_H, where an ambiguity and its hidden requirement co-occur
    for tau_h, tau_a, tau_w in [(0.5, 0.3, 0.6), (0.9, 0.7, 0.9), (0.9, 0.3, 0.6),
                                (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)]:
        params = dataclasses.replace(P, tau_h=tau_h, tau_a=tau_a, tau_w=tau_w)
        expected = {t: c.as_dict() for t, c in v3_counts(spec, fit, params).items()}
        got = {t: c.as_dict() for t, c in g0.counts_at(spec, found, tau_h, tau_a, tau_w).items()}
        assert got == expected


def test_the_threshold_filter_keeps_an_ambiguity_and_its_hidden_requirement_together() -> None:
    # Ruling F1 in counts_at: the hand-built cells of Task 7's co-occurrence test, scored at
    # every threshold setting around them, agree with the typing core.
    f = frozenset
    cells = {"c": Cell("c", "value", "d", "golden:t:K:c", "t.c", "t.c", ("i0",)),
             "e": Cell("e", "value", "d", "golden:t:K:e", "t.e", "t.e", ("i0",)),
             "u": Cell("u", "value", None, None, "t.u", "t.u", ("i0",))}
    sets = {"c": {f({"d"}): 0.45, f({"d", "a"}): 0.4, f({"a"}): 0.15},
            "e": {f({"d"}): 0.05, f({"d", "a"}): 0.34, f({"a"}): 0.31, f(): 0.3},
            "u": {f({"x"}): 0.55, f({"x", "y"}): 0.45}}
    posts = {c: CellPosterior(c, cells[c].anchor, tuple(sorted({v for x in s for v in x})), s,
                              0.0, {}) for c, s in sets.items()}
    fit = AnchoredFit({"m": 1.0}, {}, {}, posts, {}, True, 1, P)
    readings = [Reading("c", "m|0", "m", "m", "s", "a"), Reading("e", "m|1", "m", "m", "s", "a"),
                Reading("u", "m|2", "m", "m", "s", "y", True)]
    truth = {"c": Truth("amb", ("a", "d"), True, True, False),
             "e": Truth("amb", ("a", "d"), True, True, False),
             "u": Truth("none", ("x",), False, False, True)}
    spec = SimpleNamespace(cells=cells, readings=readings, truth=truth)
    found = g0.potential(spec, fit)
    for tau_h, tau_a, tau_w in itertools.product((0.5, 0.9), (0.3, 0.35, 0.5), (0.5, 0.9)):
        params = dataclasses.replace(P, tau_h=tau_h, tau_a=tau_a, tau_w=tau_w)
        expected = {t: c.as_dict() for t, c in v3_counts(spec, fit, params).items()}
        got = {t: c.as_dict() for t, c in g0.counts_at(spec, found, tau_h, tau_a, tau_w).items()}
        assert got == expected, (tau_h, tau_a, tau_w)
    at = g0.counts_at(spec, found, 0.9, 0.35, 0.5)
    assert at["hidden"].as_dict()["tp"] == 2 and at["ambiguity"].as_dict()["tp"] == 2


def test_the_verdict_needs_every_criterion_and_has_no_fallback() -> None:
    good = {"i": True, "ii": {"hidden": True, "ambiguity": True, "weak": True},
            "iii": {"calibrated": {"hidden": True, "ambiguity": True, "weak": True},
                    "a3": {"hidden": True, "ambiguity": True, "weak": True}},
            "ambiguity_win": True}
    assert g0.verdict(good) == g0.PASS
    assert g0.verdict({**good, "ii": {**good["ii"], "ambiguity": False}}) == g0.FAIL
    assert g0.verdict({**good, "i": False}) == g0.FAIL
    assert g0.verdict({**good, "ambiguity_win": False}) == g0.FAIL
    lost = {**good["iii"], "a3": {"hidden": True, "ambiguity": False, "weak": True}}
    assert g0.verdict({**good, "iii": lost}) == g0.FAIL


def test_criterion_iii_is_judged_at_four_points_with_a_margin() -> None:
    base = SimConfig(alphas=(3.6, 3.0, 2.7, 2.2), act=(0.9, 0.35, 0.8, 0.58))
    assert g0.population_point(base) == (4, 0.95, 0.1)  # accuracies 0.97–0.90
    names = list(g0.evaluations(base))
    assert names == ["calibrated", "a3", "a5", "own M=4 acc=0.95 Δ=0.1"]
    f1 = {"v3": {"hidden": 0.74, "ambiguity": 0.8, "weak": 0.3},
          "majority": {"hidden": 0.7, "ambiguity": 0.6, "weak": 0.36},
          "anchor_only": {"hidden": 0.78, "ambiguity": 0.0, "weak": 0.0},
          "v2_stage2": {"hidden": 0.5, "ambiguity": 0.0, "weak": 0.1}}
    assert g0.holds(f1) == {"hidden": True, "ambiguity": True, "weak": False}


def test_a_g0_run_writes_its_report_and_the_chosen_parameters(tmp_path) -> None:
    calibration = tmp_path / "calibration.json"
    calibration.write_text(json.dumps({"config": config_dict(TINY)}), encoding="utf-8")
    grid = {"pi": (0.1,), "shares": ((0.4, 0.3, 0.3),), "beta_sd": (1.0,),
            "alpha_prior": ((0.0, 1.5),), "tau_h": (0.5,), "tau_a": (0.3, 0.5), "tau_w": (0.6,)}
    out, params = tmp_path / "results" / "p2-g0", tmp_path / "params.json"
    verdict = g0.run(calibration, out, replicates=1, grid=grid, params_out=params)
    assert verdict in (g0.PASS, g0.FAIL)
    written = json.loads(out.with_suffix(".json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert written["verdict"] == verdict and len(written["candidates"]) == 2
    assert set(written["criteria"]) == {"theta_error", "f1", "i", "ii", "iii", "ambiguity_win",
                                        "nonconverged"}
    assert len(written["criteria"]["iii"]) == 4
    # §3.4: non-convergence is counted in the search and at every criteria point
    nc = written["nonconverged"]
    assert nc["search_fits"] == 3 and set(nc["chosen"]) == set(g0.ARMS)
    assert 0 <= nc["search"] <= 3 and set(written["criteria"]["nonconverged"]) == set(
        written["criteria"]["iii"])
    assert all(set(x["nonconverged"]) == set(g0.ARMS) for x in written["candidates"])
    # ruling F22: an out-of-sample report on the next seeds, for information only
    assert set(written["out_of_sample"]) == set(written["criteria"])
    chosen = json.loads(params.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["params"]
    assert chosen == written["chosen"]["params"] and chosen["tau_a"] in (0.3, 0.5)
    report = out.with_suffix(".md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert f"# G0: {verdict}" in report and "Non-converged fits (§3.4)" in report
    assert "## Out of sample (information only, not a criterion)" in report


def test_freeze_rewrites_only_the_marked_block(tmp_path) -> None:
    source = (g0.ANCHORED).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    target = tmp_path / "anchored.py"
    target.write_text(source, encoding="utf-8")
    chosen = tmp_path / "params.json"
    chosen.write_text(json.dumps({"params": dataclasses.asdict(P)}), encoding="utf-8")
    g0.freeze(chosen, target)
    text = target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "PARAMS_FROZEN = True" in text and text != source
    before, after = source.split("# --- G0 frozen parameters: begin ---")[0], source.split(
        "# --- G0 frozen parameters: end ---")[1]
    assert text.startswith(before) and text.endswith(after)
    block = g0._BLOCK.search(text).group(0)
    namespace: dict = {"dataclass": dataclasses.dataclass}
    exec(compile(block, "block", "exec"), namespace)  # noqa: S102 - our own generated code
    assert dataclasses.asdict(namespace["V3Params"]()) == dataclasses.asdict(P)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="no G0 frozen parameters block"):
        g0.freeze(chosen, chosen)


def test_the_pre_registration_table_names_every_parameter() -> None:
    table = g0.params_table(P)
    assert table.splitlines()[:2] == ["| parameter | field | value |", "|---|---|---:|"]
    assert len(table.splitlines()) == 2 + len(dataclasses.fields(V3Params))
    assert "| π | `pi` | 0.1 |" in table and "| τ_W | `tau_w` | 0.6 |" in table
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the calibration**

<!-- apply: create specdx/sim/calibrate.py -->
```python
"""Calibration of the simulation from cached base runs (spec v3 §8.1), read-only.

It reads the run cache (the P1b smoke run's population runs and the capability screen's
runs on happy), maps runs to the base specification the way Runner.key keys them, and
computes summary statistics of base runs only, never of a mutant: per-model accuracy and
act rates, the dispersion of cell accuracy, error concentration, the cell mix and the
number of values per column (resolution 15). Readings per cell follow from the planned
budget; the base runs' readings per cell are reported beside the simulated ones. It never
opens the database, never calls a model, and writes nothing under the run cache's
directory: the Tell PDFs are extracted into a temporary directory. A statistic that cannot
be computed raises CalibrationError: there is no silent fallback (ruling F13).

The run mapping (smoke_runs, mapped, P1_SETTINGS, NoRefutation) is shared with G1's replay
(experiments/p2_replay.py). experiments/p1b_replay.py keeps its own copies: it is an
archived script whose results are pinned (ruling F7).

Usage: SPECDX_WORK=/path/to/work python -m specdx.sim.calibrate --out PATH
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import dataclasses
import hashlib
import json
import math
import sys
import tempfile
from collections import Counter
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
from tonomyx.canon.pack import CanonPack

from specdx.config import BASE_PACK, PDF_DIR, REPO_ROOT, WORK_DIR
from specdx.db import golden_tables, split_background
from specdx.eval.screen import ScreenResult, rescreen
from specdx.infer.act import drafting_steps
from specdx.infer.cells import Cell, CellData, CellLens
from specdx.infer.items import SpecLens
from specdx.interpreters.cache import run_key
from specdx.interpreters.executor import interpreter_settings
from specdx.interpreters.population import committee
from specdx.interpreters.prompt import load_variants
from specdx.observe.run import context_digest
from specdx.orchestrate.work_orders import load_work_orders
from specdx.scenarios.space import boundary_domain, canon_scenarios, enumerate_scenarios
from specdx.scenarios.tguided import extract_literals, tboundary_scenarios
from specdx.sim.baselines import by_cell as by_cell_of
from specdx.sim.generator import SimConfig, generate
from specdx.spec.clauses import ClauseSet
from specdx.spec.columns import ColumnModel
from specdx.spec.tell import TellCorpus, pdf_chunks
from specdx.types import Behaviour, InterpreterId, RefuteResult, Scenario
from specdx.variant import SpecVariant
from specdx.workbench import tell_payload

SCREEN = REPO_ROOT / "results" / "p1b-screen.json"
SMOKE = REPO_ROOT / "results" / "p1b-smoke-base.json"
SMOKE_GATE = REPO_ROOT / "experiments" / "p1b-gate.json"
SMOKE_RUNS = 60  # the P1b smoke run: 4 scenarios x its population x 3 variants x 3 seeds
# P1's context digest (RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION 2 and P1's executor settings), for P1's base runs.
P1_SCHEMA = 2
P1_SETTINGS: dict[str, float | int] = {
    "temperature": 0.7, "max_steps": 16, "timeout": 180.0, "num_ctx": 16384, "search_k": 5,
    "search_reply_limit": 12000,
}
# The planned budget (spec v3 §8.2, G0): population 200 runs per specification, which
# escalates floor(200 / (models x 3 variants x 3 seeds)) scenarios to the full committee.
BUDGET = 200
DEFECT_RATE = 0.15  # planted, not calibrated: the base's defect rate is unknown
CONCENTRATIONS: tuple[float, ...] = (0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0)


class CalibrationError(RuntimeError):
    """The cache does not map as expected, or a statistic has no data: no statistic is
    computed from a broken mapping, and none falls back to a default."""


class NoRefutation:
    """No run may execute in a replay: every candidate stays unattempted."""

    def refute(self, targets: frozenset[str], scenario: Scenario,
               behaviours: Sequence[Behaviour]) -> RefuteResult:
        return RefuteResult(False, None, 0, attempted=False)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Calibration:
    """population: the §7 population, the screen with act rate ≥ 0.3. accuracy: per model,
    the share of its readings equal to d_c at anchored cells whose plurality among the
    population's readings is d_c. dispersion: the standard deviation over those cells of
    their logit accuracy, from the population's readings. top_error: the mean share of a
    cell's most common error among its errors. reads: the median readings per model of a
    per-scenario cell in the base runs, reported beside simulated_reads, the same statistic
    on a specification generated from config() (readings per cell follow from the planned
    budget). The cell mix and values are shares of columns, as the generator draws them: a
    column is a pooled cell, or a per-scenario cell's row and column across scenarios."""

    population: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]
    act: dict[str, float]
    accuracy: dict[str, float]
    dispersion: float
    top_error: float
    error_conc: float
    reads: float
    pooled_share: float
    anchored_share: float
    constraint_share: float
    values: tuple[float, ...]
    columns: int
    items: int
    runs: int
    simulated_reads: float = 0.0

    def config(self, seed: int = 0) -> SimConfig:
        models = [m for ms in self.population.values() for m in ms]
        models.sort(key=lambda m: -self.accuracy[m])
        scenarios = max(1, BUDGET // (len(models) * 9))
        return SimConfig(
            alphas=tuple(_logit(self.accuracy[m]) for m in models),
            act=tuple(self.act[m] for m in models), columns=self.columns,
            pooled_share=self.pooled_share, scenarios=scenarios, items=self.items,
            beta_sd=self.dispersion, anchored_share=self.anchored_share,
            constraint_share=self.constraint_share, values=self.values,
            defect_rate=DEFECT_RATE, error_conc=self.error_conc, seed=seed)

    def to_json(self) -> str:
        raw = dataclasses.asdict(self)
        raw["population"] = {f: list(ms) for f, ms in self.population.items()}
        raw["values"] = list(self.values)
        config = self.config()
        raw["config"] = {k: list(v) if isinstance(v, tuple) else v
                         for k, v in dataclasses.asdict(config).items()}
        raw["models"] = sorted((m for ms in self.population.values() for m in ms),
                               key=lambda m: -self.accuracy[m])
        return json.dumps(raw, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)


def _logit(p: float) -> float:
    p = min(max(p, 1e-3), 1 - 1e-3)
    return math.log(p / (1 - p))


def load_cache(path: Path) -> dict[str, Behaviour]:
    out: dict[str, Behaviour] = {}
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for raw in handle:
            if raw.strip():
                b = Behaviour.from_json(raw.decode("utf-8"))
                out[b.run_key] = b
    return out


def model_digests(cache: Mapping[str, Behaviour]) -> dict[str, str]:
    seen: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    for b in cache.values():
        seen.setdefault(b.interpreter.model, set()).add(b.model_digest)
    if any(len(v) != 1 for v in seen.values()):
        raise CalibrationError(f"a model has several digests in the cache: {seen}")
    return {m: next(iter(v)) for m, v in seen.items()}


@dataclass
class Base:
    """The base specification as the smoke run saw it, built without a database."""

    pack: CanonPack
    variant: SpecVariant
    lens: SpecLens
    cells: CellLens
    scenarios: dict[str, Scenario]
    steps_digest: str
    context: str


def open_base(pdf_cache: Path) -> Base:
    pack = CanonPack(BASE_PACK)
    steps = load_work_orders()
    corpus = TellCorpus.build(pack, pdf_chunks(PDF_DIR, pdf_cache))
    variant = SpecVariant("base", BASE_PACK, corpus)
    clauses = ClauseSet.from_pack(pack, steps)
    background, _ = split_background(golden_tables(pack))
    lens = SpecLens(clauses, steps, ColumnModel.of(pack), drafting=drafting_steps(pack, steps),
                    initial=background)
    canon = canon_scenarios(pack)
    literals = extract_literals(corpus)
    every = [*canon.values(), *enumerate_scenarios(boundary_domain(pack, [x.bound for x in
                                                                          literals])),
             *tboundary_scenarios(canon, literals)]
    return Base(pack, variant, lens, CellLens(lens, pack, corpus),
                {s.id: s for s in every},
                hashlib.sha256(repr(steps).encode()).hexdigest(),
                context_digest(tell_payload(load_variants()), interpreter_settings()))


def mapped(base: Base, cache: Mapping[str, Behaviour], digests: Mapping[str, str],
           scenario: Scenario, interp: InterpreterId) -> Behaviour | None:
    """The cached base run of this interpreter on this scenario, under the current run key
    (schema 3), with the caller's scenario and interpreter as Runner.run returns it."""
    if interp.model not in digests:
        return None
    key = run_key(base.variant.digest(), base.steps_digest, base.context,
                  json.dumps(scenario.params(), sort_keys=True), interp.key, "",
                  digests[interp.model])
    b = cache.get(key)
    return None if b is None else dataclasses.replace(b, scenario=scenario, interpreter=interp)


def screen_results(path: Path = SCREEN) -> list[ScreenResult]:
    return rescreen(path).results


def gate_members(path: Path) -> dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]:
    """A gate configuration's population, family → models."""
    raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return {f: tuple(ms) for f, ms in raw["models_by_family"].items()}


def smoke_runs(base: Base, cache: Mapping[str, Behaviour],
               digests: Mapping[str, str]) -> list[Behaviour]:
    """The P1b smoke run: its population (experiments/p1b-gate.json) on the smoke report's
    scenarios, Tell variants 0–2 and seeds 1–3, in scenario then committee order."""
    scenarios = json.loads(SMOKE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["scenarios"]
    members = committee(gate_members(SMOKE_GATE), (0, 1, 2), (1, 2, 3))
    out = [b for sid in scenarios for i in members
           if (b := mapped(base, cache, digests, base.scenarios[sid], i)) is not None]
    if len(out) != SMOKE_RUNS:
        raise CalibrationError(f"smoke: {len(out)} runs mapped, expected {SMOKE_RUNS} runs")
    return out


def base_runs(base: Base, cache: Mapping[str, Behaviour]) -> list[Behaviour]:
    """The smoke population's runs on the smoke scenarios, and every screened model's runs
    on happy (Tell variants 0–2, seeds 1–3). 18 runs are both."""
    digests = model_digests(cache)
    out = {b.run_key: b for b in smoke_runs(base, cache, digests)}
    for r in screen_results():
        for v in (0, 1, 2):
            for s in (1, 2, 3):
                interp = InterpreterId(r.model, r.family, v, s)
                if (b := mapped(base, cache, digests, base.scenarios["happy"], interp)):
                    out[b.run_key] = b
    return [out[k] for k in sorted(out)]


def _top_error(data: CellData) -> float:
    shares = []
    for rs in data.by_cell().values():
        values = Counter(r.value for r in rs)
        mode = min(values, key=lambda v: (-values[v], v))
        errors = Counter({v: n for v, n in values.items() if v != mode})
        if sum(errors.values()) >= 2:
            shares.append(max(errors.values()) / sum(errors.values()))
    if not shares:
        raise CalibrationError("no cell has two errors: the error concentration has no data")
    return float(np.mean(shares))


def column_of(cell_id: str) -> str:
    """A cell's column, as the generator draws one: a pooled cell is its own column; a
    per-scenario cell's column is its row and column across scenarios."""
    return cell_id.split("|", 1)[1] if "|" in cell_id else cell_id


def _columns(data: CellData) -> dict[str, list[Cell]]:
    out: dict[str, list[Cell]] = {}
    for cid in sorted(data.cells):
        out.setdefault(column_of(cid), []).append(data.cells[cid])
    return out


def _median_reads(by_cell: Mapping[str, Sequence[Any]], per_scenario: Any) -> float:
    """The median readings per model of a per-scenario cell."""
    reads = [n for cid, rs in by_cell.items() if per_scenario(cid)
             for n in Counter(r.model for r in rs).values()]
    if not reads:
        raise CalibrationError("no per-scenario cell was read")
    return float(np.median(reads))


def _matching_concentration(top: float, seed: int = 0) -> float:
    """The Dirichlet concentration whose error tendencies give the observed top-error share,
    by simulation over three to six error values, the nearest of CONCENTRATIONS."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    def share(conc: float) -> float:
        out = []
        for _ in range(2000):
            k = int(rng.integers(3, 7))
            out.append(float(rng.dirichlet(np.full(k, conc)).max()))
        return float(np.mean(out))

    return min(CONCENTRATIONS, key=lambda c: (abs(share(c) - top), c))


def calibrate(base: Base, runs: Sequence[Behaviour]) -> Calibration:
    """Resolution 15's statistics. The plurality, the accuracy and the dispersion read the
    population's readings only; the cell mix and |V| are shares of columns (ruling F12)."""
    screened = rescreen(SCREEN)
    results, population = screened.results, screened.population
    members = {m for ms in population.values() for m in ms}
    data = base.cells.extract(runs)
    by_cell = data.by_cell()
    accuracy_hits: dict[str, list[int]] = {m: [0, 0] for m in members}
    cell_logits = []
    for cid, cell in data.cells.items():
        rs = [r for r in by_cell[cid] if r.model in members]
        if cell.anchor is None or not rs:
            continue
        values = Counter(r.value for r in rs)
        if min(values, key=lambda v: (-values[v], v)) != cell.anchor:
            continue
        for r in rs:
            accuracy_hits[r.model][0] += r.value == cell.anchor
            accuracy_hits[r.model][1] += 1
        hits = sum(r.value == cell.anchor for r in rs)
        cell_logits.append(_logit((hits + 0.5) / (len(rs) + 1)))
    unread = sorted(m for m, (_, n) in accuracy_hits.items() if n == 0)
    if unread:
        raise CalibrationError(f"no anchored reading to measure accuracy for {unread}")
    if not cell_logits:
        raise CalibrationError("no anchored cell to measure the dispersion of accuracy")
    accuracy = {m: (h + 0.5) / (n + 1) for m, (h, n) in accuracy_hits.items()}
    rates = {r.model: r.act_rate for r in results}
    missing = sorted(members - set(rates))
    if missing:
        raise CalibrationError(f"the screen has no act rate for {missing}")
    columns = _columns(data)
    value_columns = [cs for cs in columns.values() if cs[0].kind != "constraint"]
    sizes = Counter(
        sorted(min(len({r.value for r in by_cell[c.id]}) + 1, 7) for c in cs)[(len(cs) - 1) // 2]
        for cs in value_columns)
    top = _top_error(data)
    calibration = Calibration(
        population=population,
        act={m: rates[m] for m in sorted(members)},
        accuracy=dict(sorted(accuracy.items())),
        dispersion=float(min(max(np.std(cell_logits), 0.25), 3.0)),
        top_error=top, error_conc=_matching_concentration(top),
        reads=_median_reads(by_cell, lambda cid: "|" in cid),
        pooled_share=sum("|" not in cs[0].id for cs in columns.values()) / len(columns),
        anchored_share=sum(2 * sum(c.anchor is not None for c in cs) >= len(cs)
                           for cs in value_columns) / len(value_columns),
        constraint_share=(len(columns) - len(value_columns)) / len(columns),
        values=tuple(sizes[k] / len(value_columns) for k in range(2, 8)),
        columns=len(columns), items=len(base.lens.steps), runs=len(runs))
    simulated = generate(calibration.config())
    return dataclasses.replace(calibration, simulated_reads=_median_reads(
        by_cell_of(simulated), lambda cid: not cid.endswith("|pooled")))


def summary(c: Calibration) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"population": c.population, "accuracy": c.accuracy, "act": c.act,
            "dispersion": c.dispersion, "top_error": c.top_error, "error_conc": c.error_conc,
            "reads": c.reads, "simulated_reads": c.simulated_reads, "columns": c.columns,
            "runs": c.runs}


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True, help="the calibration JSON")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        cache = load_cache(WORK_DIR / "runs.jsonl")
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as pdf:
            base = open_base(Path(pdf))
            calibration = calibrate(base, base_runs(base, cache))
    except CalibrationError as exc:
        print(f"error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.out.write_text(calibration.to_json(), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(summary(calibration), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the G0 runner**

Each prior setting is fitted once per arm and replicate; its zero-threshold findings are then filtered per threshold setting, exactly as `type_cells` would type them, co-occurrence included (the tests check it), so the 27 threshold settings cost no refit. Criterion (iii) is judged at four points, each with its own replicates and baselines. The criteria are judged on the selection replicates: the seeds stay as they were when the preview ran (ruling F22).

<!-- apply: create specdx/sim/g0.py -->
```python
"""G0 (spec v3.1 §8.2, §9): choose the free parameters by simulation, then check G0.

The grid and the selection rule are fixed here, before G0 runs. For each setting of the
priors (π and the form shares, τ, μ_α and σ_α; κ is fixed at 10) the anchored model is
fitted on REPLICATES synthetic specifications per arm (the calibrated configuration, A3
violated, A5 violated); each threshold setting (τ_H, τ_A, τ_W) is then scored on the same
fits. The objective is the mean typed F1 over hidden requirement, ambiguity and weak
check, averaged over the three arms with equal weight. Ties go to the more conservative
setting: higher P(|A| = 1) on an anchored cell (1 − π + π·s_mis), then smaller π, then
grid order.

At the chosen setting G0 checks §8.2's criteria: (i) mean |θ̂ − θ| ≤ 0.05; (ii) typed F1
≥ 0.6 for hidden requirement, ≥ 0.4 for ambiguity and ≥ 0.4 for weak check; (iii) at the
calibrated point, under the A3 and A5 arms, and at the population's own M and Δ, v3.1 is
never below the best baseline by more than 0.05 on any type, and at the calibrated point
it beats the best baseline on ambiguity by more than 0.05. If G0 fails, the work stops.
The criteria are judged on the selection replicates; the next replicates' seeds give an
out-of-sample report for information only (ruling F22). Non-converged fits are counted in
the search and at every criteria point and reported (§3.4, ruling F2).

Usage:
  python -m specdx.sim.g0 run --calibration PATH --out results/p2-g0
  python -m specdx.sim.g0 freeze PATH        # rewrite V3Params from G0's chosen values
  python -m specdx.sim.g0 table              # V3Params as the pre-registration's table
Exit status of run: 0 PASS, 1 FAIL, 2 error.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import dataclasses
import itertools
import json
import math
import re
import sys
import time
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from specdx.infer.anchored import AnchoredFit, V3Params, fit_anchored
from specdx.infer.typing import (
    AMBIGUOUS_PICK,
    CellFinding,
    ambiguity_finding,
    hidden_finding,
    weak_finding,
)
from specdx.sim.baselines import by_cell
from specdx.sim.generator import SimConfig, SimSpec, generate
from specdx.sim.metrics import TYPES, Counts, theta_error, typed_counts
from specdx.sim.study import (
    BASELINES,
    MARGIN,
    METHODS,
    REGIME_ACCURACY,
    REGIME_DELTA,
    load_config,
    regime_config,
    score,
)

# The grid (§9: "written in the simulation module before G0 runs").
PI: tuple[float, ...] = (0.05, 0.1, 0.2)
SHARES: tuple[tuple[float, float, float], ...] = ((0.6, 0.2, 0.2), (0.4, 0.3, 0.3),
                                                  (0.2, 0.4, 0.4))
BETA_SD: tuple[float, ...] = (1.0, 2.0)
ALPHA_PRIOR: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...] = ((0.0, 1.5), (1.0, 1.0))
TAU_H: tuple[float, ...] = (0.5, 0.7, 0.9)
TAU_A: tuple[float, ...] = (0.3, 0.5, 0.7)
TAU_W: tuple[float, ...] = (0.6, 0.8, 0.9)
ARMS: tuple[str, ...] = ("base", "a3", "a5")
REPLICATES = 10  # §8.1: at least 10 per arm at the calibrated point
# G0's criteria (§8.2).
THETA_ERROR_MAX = 0.05
F1_MIN: dict[str, float] = {"hidden": 0.6, "ambiguity": 0.4, "weak": 0.4}
AMBIGUITY_WIN = 0.05  # (iii): at the calibrated point v3.1 beats the best baseline by more
PASS, FAIL = "PASS", "FAIL"
PARAMS_FILE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "experiments" / "p2-g0-params.json"
ANCHORED = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "infer" / "anchored.py"
_BLOCK = re.compile(
    r"# --- G0 frozen parameters: begin ---\n.*?# --- G0 frozen parameters: end ---", re.DOTALL)


def prior_settings(grid: dict[str, Sequence[Any]] | None = None) -> list[V3Params]:
    """Every setting of the priors, in grid order, with the first thresholds."""
    g = grid or {}
    out = []
    for pi, shares, beta_sd, (mu, sigma) in itertools.product(
            g.get("pi", PI), g.get("shares", SHARES), g.get("beta_sd", BETA_SD),
            g.get("alpha_prior", ALPHA_PRIOR)):
        out.append(V3Params(pi=pi, s_mis=shares[0], s_amb=shares[1], s_empty=shares[2],
                            beta_sd=beta_sd, mu_alpha=mu, sigma_alpha=sigma,
                            tau_h=TAU_H[0], tau_a=TAU_A[0], tau_w=TAU_W[0]))
    return out


def thresholds(grid: dict[str, Sequence[Any]] | None = None) -> list[tuple[float, float, float]]:
    g = grid or {}
    return list(itertools.product(g.get("tau_h", TAU_H), g.get("tau_a", TAU_A),
                                  g.get("tau_w", TAU_W)))


def conservative(p: V3Params) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """The tie-break: higher P(|A| = 1) on an anchored cell, then smaller π."""
    return (-(1 - p.pi + p.pi * p.s_mis), p.pi)


@dataclass
class Candidate:
    """nonconverged: per arm, the fits of this prior setting that did not converge (§3.4:
    reported, never silent); the threshold settings of one prior setting share them."""

    params: V3Params
    objective: float
    arms: dict[str, dict[str, float]]
    nonconverged: dict[str, int] = dataclasses.field(default_factory=dict)


def potential(spec: SimSpec, fit: AnchoredFit) -> list[CellFinding]:
    """Every finding the typing core makes at zero thresholds: each threshold setting then
    keeps a subset of them, without typing the cells again."""
    readings = by_cell(spec)
    out: list[CellFinding] = []
    for cid in sorted(fit.cells):
        cell, post, mine = spec.cells[cid], fit.cells[cid], readings[cid]
        for found in (hidden_finding(cell, post, mine, 0.0),
                      ambiguity_finding(cell, post, mine, 0.0),
                      weak_finding(cell, post, mine, 0.0)):
            if found is not None:
                out.append(found)
    return out


def counts_at(spec: SimSpec, found: Sequence[CellFinding], tau_h: float, tau_a: float,
              tau_w: float) -> dict[str, Counts]:
    """typing.type_cells at these thresholds, from the zero-threshold findings. On an
    anchored cell (one with a hidden finding at threshold 0) an ambiguity and its hidden
    requirement co-occur (typing.co_occur); a cell reporting an ambiguity reports no weak
    check."""
    hidden = {f.cell: f for f in found if f.type == "hidden"}
    ambiguous = {f.cell for f in found if f.type == "ambiguity" and (
        f.score >= tau_a or (f.cell in hidden and hidden[f.cell].score >= tau_h
                             and hidden[f.cell].subtype == AMBIGUOUS_PICK))}
    kept = [(c, "hidden") for c, f in hidden.items() if f.score >= tau_h or c in ambiguous]
    kept += [(c, "ambiguity") for c in sorted(ambiguous)]
    kept += [(f.cell, f.type) for f in found
             if f.type == "weak" and f.score >= tau_w and f.cell not in ambiguous]
    return typed_counts(kept, spec.truth)


def search(base: SimConfig, replicates: int = REPLICATES,
           grid: dict[str, Sequence[Any]] | None = None,
           progress: Any = None) -> tuple[Candidate, list[Candidate]]:
    """Fit every prior setting on every arm and replicate, score every threshold setting,
    and choose by the selection rule. Returns the chosen candidate and all of them."""
    specs = {arm: [generate(dataclasses.replace(base, arm=arm, seed=base.seed + i))
                   for i in range(replicates)] for arm in ARMS}
    candidates: list[Candidate] = []
    for n, prior in enumerate(prior_settings(grid)):
        found: dict[str, list[list[CellFinding]]] = {arm: [] for arm in ARMS}
        nonconverged = dict.fromkeys(ARMS, 0)
        for arm in ARMS:
            for s in specs[arm]:
                fit = fit_anchored(s.cells, s.readings, models=s.models, params=prior)
                nonconverged[arm] += not fit.converged
                found[arm].append(potential(s, fit))
        for tau_h, tau_a, tau_w in thresholds(grid):
            params = dataclasses.replace(prior, tau_h=tau_h, tau_a=tau_a, tau_w=tau_w)
            arms: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
            for arm in ARMS:
                total = {t: Counts() for t in TYPES}
                for spec, mine in zip(specs[arm], found[arm], strict=True):
                    for t, c in counts_at(spec, mine, tau_h, tau_a, tau_w).items():
                        total[t].add(c)
                arms[arm] = {t: total[t].f1 for t in TYPES}
            objective = sum(sum(a.values()) / len(TYPES) for a in arms.values()) / len(ARMS)
            candidates.append(Candidate(params, objective, arms, dict(nonconverged)))
        if progress is not None:
            progress(n + 1)
    return choose(candidates), candidates


def choose(candidates: Sequence[Candidate]) -> Candidate:
    """The selection rule: the highest objective (to 1e-9); ties to the more conservative
    setting, then to the earlier one in grid order."""
    ranked = sorted(enumerate(candidates), key=lambda x: (
        -round(x[1].objective, 9), *conservative(x[1].params), x[0]))
    return ranked[0][1]


def population_point(base: SimConfig) -> tuple[int, float, float]:
    """The regime-map point of the population itself (§8.2 (iii)): its number of models,
    and the grid accuracy and spread nearest its mean accuracy and its accuracy range."""
    accuracy = [1 / (1 + math.exp(-a)) for a in base.alphas]
    mean, spread = sum(accuracy) / len(accuracy), max(accuracy) - min(accuracy)
    return (len(accuracy), min(REGIME_ACCURACY, key=lambda a: (abs(a - mean), a)),
            min(REGIME_DELTA, key=lambda d: (abs(d - spread), d)))


def evaluations(base: SimConfig) -> dict[str, SimConfig]:
    """Where criterion (iii) is judged: the calibrated point, its A3 and A5 arms, and the
    population's own point of the regime map."""
    m, accuracy, delta = population_point(base)
    return {"calibrated": dataclasses.replace(base, arm="base"),
            "a3": dataclasses.replace(base, arm="a3"),
            "a5": dataclasses.replace(base, arm="a5"),
            f"own M={m} acc={accuracy} Δ={delta}": regime_config(base, m, accuracy, delta)}


def holds(f1: dict[str, dict[str, float]]) -> dict[str, bool]:
    """Per type: v3.1 is not below the best baseline by more than MARGIN."""
    return {t: f1["v3"][t] - max(f1[b][t] for b in BASELINES) >= -MARGIN for t in TYPES}


def check(base: SimConfig, params: V3Params, replicates: int = REPLICATES,
          offset: int = 0) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """G0's criteria (§8.2) at the chosen parameters, on seeds base.seed + offset + i. G0
    judges them on the selection replicates (offset 0, ruling F22); `run` adds the next
    `replicates` seeds as an out-of-sample report, for information only.
    nonconverged: per evaluation point, the fits that did not converge (§3.4)."""
    where: dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, float]]] = {}
    errors: list[float] = []
    nonconverged: dict[str, int] = {}
    for name, config in evaluations(base).items():
        totals = {m: {t: Counts() for t in TYPES} for m in METHODS}
        nonconverged[name] = 0
        for i in range(replicates):
            spec = generate(dataclasses.replace(config, seed=base.seed + offset + i))
            fit = fit_anchored(spec.cells, spec.readings, models=spec.models, params=params)
            nonconverged[name] += not fit.converged
            for m, by in score(spec, fit, params).counts.items():
                for t, c in by.items():
                    totals[m][t].add(c)
            if name == "calibrated":
                errors.append(theta_error(fit.theta, spec.theta))
        where[name] = {m: {t: totals[m][t].f1 for t in TYPES} for m in METHODS}
    calibrated = where["calibrated"]
    error = sum(errors) / len(errors)
    best_ambiguity = max(calibrated[b]["ambiguity"] for b in BASELINES)
    return {"theta_error": error, "f1": where,
            "i": error <= THETA_ERROR_MAX,
            "ii": {t: calibrated["v3"][t] >= F1_MIN[t] for t in TYPES},
            "iii": {name: holds(f1) for name, f1 in where.items()},
            "ambiguity_win": calibrated["v3"]["ambiguity"] - best_ambiguity > AMBIGUITY_WIN,
            "nonconverged": nonconverged}


def verdict(criteria: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """PASS when (i), every (ii) and every part of (iii) hold."""
    ok = (criteria["i"] and all(criteria["ii"].values()) and criteria["ambiguity_win"]
          and all(all(by.values()) for by in criteria["iii"].values()))
    return PASS if ok else FAIL


def params_dict(p: V3Params) -> dict[str, float]:
    return dataclasses.asdict(p)


def _yes(ok: bool) -> str:
    return "yes" if ok else "no"


def render(result: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    chosen = result["chosen"]
    c = result["criteria"]
    lines = ["<!-- generated by python -m specdx.sim.g0 -->", "",
             f"# G0: {result['verdict']}", "",
             f"Calibration: `{result['calibration']}`. Replicates per arm: "
             f"{result['replicates']}. Grid: {result['settings']} prior settings x "
             f"{result['thresholds']} threshold settings. Runtime: {result['seconds']:.0f} s.",
             "", "## Chosen parameters (selection rule of spec v3.1 §9)", "",
             "| parameter | value |", "|---|---:|"]
    lines += [f"| {k} | {v} |" for k, v in chosen["params"].items()]
    lines += ["", f"Objective (mean typed F1, arms equally weighted): {chosen['objective']:.4f}.",
              "", "| arm | hidden | ambiguity | weak |", "|---|---:|---:|---:|"]
    lines += [f"| {arm} | {f['hidden']:.3f} | {f['ambiguity']:.3f} | {f['weak']:.3f} |"
              for arm, f in chosen["arms"].items()]
    nc = result["nonconverged"]
    lines += ["", f"Non-converged fits (§3.4): {nc['search']} of {nc['search_fits']} in the "
              f"search; at the chosen setting {nc['chosen']}; at the criteria points "
              f"{c['nonconverged']}; out of sample {result['out_of_sample']['nonconverged']}."]
    lines += ["", "## Criteria (spec v3.1 §8.2, on the selection replicates)", "",
              *_criteria_lines(c)]
    o = result["out_of_sample"]
    lines += [f"## Out of sample (information only, not a criterion): seeds base + "
              f"{result['replicates']}…{2 * result['replicates'] - 1}", "",
              f"The same criteria would give {verdict(o)}.", "", *_criteria_lines(o)]
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _criteria_lines(c: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    calibrated = c["f1"]["calibrated"]
    lines = [f"- (i) mean abs θ error {c['theta_error']:.4f} ≤ {THETA_ERROR_MAX}: "
             f"{_yes(c['i'])}"]
    lines += [f"- (ii) {t} F1 {calibrated['v3'][t]:.3f} ≥ {F1_MIN[t]}: {_yes(c['ii'][t])}"
              for t in TYPES]
    lines += [f"- (iii) {name}: not below the best baseline by more than {MARGIN}: "
              + ", ".join(f"{t} {_yes(ok)}" for t, ok in by.items())
              for name, by in c["iii"].items()]
    lines += [f"- (iii) ambiguity win at the calibrated point (> {AMBIGUITY_WIN} over the best "
              f"baseline): {_yes(c['ambiguity_win'])}", ""]
    for name, f1 in c["f1"].items():
        lines += [f"### {name}", "", "| method | hidden | ambiguity | weak |",
                  "|---|---:|---:|---:|"]
        lines += [f"| {m} | {f['hidden']:.3f} | {f['ambiguity']:.3f} | {f['weak']:.3f} |"
                  for m, f in f1.items()]
        lines.append("")
    return lines


def run(calibration: Path, out: Path, *, replicates: int = REPLICATES,
        grid: dict[str, Sequence[Any]] | None = None, params_out: Path = PARAMS_FILE) -> str:
    started = time.monotonic()
    base = load_config(calibration)
    total = len(prior_settings(grid))
    chosen, candidates = search(
        base, replicates, grid,
        progress=lambda n: print(f"G0: {n}/{total} prior settings", file=sys.stderr))
    criteria = check(base, chosen.params, replicates)
    out_of_sample = check(base, chosen.params, replicates, offset=replicates)
    per_prior = {dataclasses.replace(x.params, tau_h=0.0, tau_a=0.0, tau_w=0.0): x.nonconverged
                 for x in candidates}
    nonconverged = {"search": sum(sum(n.values()) for n in per_prior.values()),
                    "search_fits": len(per_prior) * len(ARMS) * replicates,
                    "chosen": chosen.nonconverged}
    result = {"verdict": verdict(criteria), "calibration": str(calibration),
              "replicates": replicates, "settings": total, "thresholds": len(thresholds(grid)),
              "seconds": time.monotonic() - started, "base": dataclasses.asdict(base),
              "chosen": {"params": params_dict(chosen.params), "objective": chosen.objective,
                         "arms": chosen.arms},
              "criteria": criteria, "nonconverged": nonconverged,
              "out_of_sample": out_of_sample,
              "candidates": [{"params": params_dict(x.params), "objective": x.objective,
                              "arms": x.arms, "nonconverged": x.nonconverged}
                             for x in candidates]}
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.with_suffix(".json").write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    out.with_suffix(".md").write_text(render(result), encoding="utf-8")
    params_out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    params_out.write_text(json.dumps({"verdict": result["verdict"],
                                      "params": params_dict(chosen.params)}, indent=2) + "\n",
                          encoding="utf-8")
    return str(result["verdict"])


def frozen_block(params: V3Params) -> str:
    """V3Params with G0's values as its defaults, for specdx/infer/anchored.py."""
    fields = "\n".join(f"    {f.name}: float = {getattr(params, f.name)!r}"
                       for f in dataclasses.fields(V3Params))
    return ("# --- G0 frozen parameters: begin ---\nPARAMS_FROZEN = True\n\n\n"
            "@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass V3Params:\n"
            '    """The values G0 chose by the selection rule of spec v3.1 §9, frozen in the '
            'v3\n    pre-registration (experiments/p2-preregistration.md)."""\n\n'
            f"{fields}\n# --- G0 frozen parameters: end ---")


# The pre-registration's names for V3Params' fields (spec v3.1 §3.1, §3.3, §5).
NAMES: dict[str, str] = {
    "pi": "π", "s_mis": "s_mis", "s_amb": "s_amb", "s_empty": "s_∅", "beta_sd": "τ (β prior sd)",
    "mu_alpha": "μ_α", "sigma_alpha": "σ_α", "tau_h": "τ_H", "tau_a": "τ_A", "tau_w": "τ_W",
}


def params_table(params: V3Params) -> str:
    """The chosen values as the v3 pre-registration states them."""
    rows = [f"| {NAMES[f.name]} | `{f.name}` | {getattr(params, f.name)!r} |"
            for f in dataclasses.fields(V3Params)]
    return "\n".join(["| parameter | field | value |", "|---|---|---:|", *rows])


def freeze(path: Path, target: Path = ANCHORED) -> None:
    raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    params = V3Params(**raw["params"])
    text = target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if not _BLOCK.search(text):
        raise ValueError(f"{target} has no G0 frozen parameters block")
    target.write_text(_BLOCK.sub(lambda _: frozen_block(params), text), encoding="utf-8")


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    r = sub.add_parser("run")
    r.add_argument("--calibration", type=Path, required=True)
    r.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    r.add_argument("--replicates", type=int, default=REPLICATES)
    f = sub.add_parser("freeze")
    f.add_argument("params", type=Path)
    sub.add_parser("table")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.command == "freeze":
        freeze(args.params)
        return 0
    if args.command == "table":
        print(params_table(V3Params()))
        return 0
    try:
        outcome = run(args.calibration, args.out, replicates=args.replicates)
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
        print(f"error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    print(outcome)
    return {PASS: 0, FAIL: 1}[outcome]


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

- Produces (`experiments/p2_replay.py`, loaded by its tests from its path; (ii) is judged on the smoke run only, by the author's ruling now in spec v3.1 §8.2): `THETA_FLOOR = 0.5`, `NO_COLLAPSE = ("gpt-oss:20b", "qwen3:30b-a3b")`, `R_SO = "golden:so:SO-0031:trang_thai"`, `R_SO_CELL`, `ReplayError`, `P1_RUNS = 200`, `Replay` (`name`, `runs`, `data`, `rasch`, `fit`, `diagnosis`, `unmatched`), `p1_runs`, `unmatched_rows(base, data)`, `replay(name, base, runs, population, params)`, `theta_holds(r) -> tuple[bool, str]`, `r_so_status(r) -> str` (reported, missed, not observable), `verdict(smoke) -> bool`, `render(replays, params) -> str`, `main(argv) -> int` (0 PASS, 1 FAIL, 2 error; `--provisional` only for a plan pre-check).

```python
"""G1's replay script (experiments/p2_replay.py): its criteria, its read-only guards and its
exit statuses, without the run cache."""

import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from specdx.infer import anchored
from specdx.infer.act import Rasch
from specdx.infer.anchored import AnchoredFit, V3Params
from specdx.infer.cells import Cell, CellData, Reading
from specdx.infer.typing import CellDiagnosis
from specdx.types import Defect, Scenario

SCRIPT = Path(__file__).parents[1] / "experiments" / "p2_replay.py"


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def g1():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("p2_replay", SCRIPT)
    assert spec is not None and spec.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[spec.name] = module
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def _replay(g1, theta, readings=(), defects=()):
    cell = Cell(g1.R_SO_CELL, "value", "chờ duyệt", g1.R_SO, "so.trang_thai", "so.trang_thai",
                ("DE-SM-02:draft_quote",))
    data = CellData({cell.id: cell} if readings else {}, list(readings))
    fit = AnchoredFit({m: 0.0 for m in theta}, theta, {}, {}, {}, True, 1, V3Params())
    return g1.Replay("smoke", [], data, Rasch({}, {}, {}, {}), fit,
                     CellDiagnosis(list(defects)), {})


def test_g1_needs_no_collapse_and_r_so_where_it_was_read(g1) -> None:
    good = {"gpt-oss:20b": 0.97, "qwen3:30b-a3b": 0.91}
    read = [Reading(g1.R_SO_CELL, "k1", "gpt-oss:20b", "openai", "grn12", "draft")]
    found = [Defect("hidden", (g1.R_SO,), ("grn12",), ("k1",), 1.0, "x")]
    assert g1.r_so_status(_replay(g1, good, read, found)) == "reported"
    assert g1.verdict(_replay(g1, good, read, found))
    assert g1.r_so_status(_replay(g1, good, read)) == "missed"
    assert not g1.verdict(_replay(g1, good, read))
    assert g1.r_so_status(_replay(g1, good)) == "not observable"
    assert g1.verdict(_replay(g1, good))  # not observable does not fail (ii)
    collapsed = {"gpt-oss:20b": 0.97, "qwen3:30b-a3b": 0.08}
    assert not g1.theta_holds(_replay(g1, collapsed))[0]
    assert not g1.verdict(_replay(g1, collapsed, read, found))
    assert not g1.theta_holds(_replay(g1, {"gpt-oss:20b": 0.97}))[0]  # a missing model fails
    assert g1.THETA_FLOOR == 0.5 and g1.NO_COLLAPSE == ("gpt-oss:20b", "qwen3:30b-a3b")


def test_the_replay_never_refutes(g1) -> None:
    result = g1.NoRefutation().refute(frozenset({"c"}), Scenario("s", 15, 12, 0), [])
    assert not result.refuted and not result.attempted


def test_rows_matching_no_golden_row_are_counted_per_column(g1) -> None:
    base = SimpleNamespace(cells=SimpleNamespace(rows={
        "po": SimpleNamespace(golden={("po-0015",): "PO-0015"})}))
    cells = {c.id: c for c in [
        Cell("grn12|po:PO-0015:sl_dat", "value", "15", "x", "po.sl_dat", "po.sl_dat", ("s",)),
        Cell("grn12|po:po-0016:sl_dat", "value", None, None, "po.sl_dat", "po.sl_dat", ("s",)),
        Cell("happy|po:de-fn-04:sl_dat", "value", None, None, "po.sl_dat", "po.sl_dat", ("s",)),
        Cell("golden:po:PO-0015:sl_dat", "value", "{ordered}", "x", "po.sl_dat", "po.sl_dat",
             ("s",))]}
    assert g1.unmatched_rows(base, CellData(cells, [])) == {"po.sl_dat": 2}


def test_g1_refuses_provisional_parameters_and_a_broken_mapping(g1, monkeypatch,
                                                                 tmp_path) -> None:
    out = tmp_path / "g1.md"
    monkeypatch.setattr(anchored, "PARAMS_FROZEN", False)
    assert g1.main(["--out", str(out)]) == 2 and not out.exists()
    monkeypatch.setattr(anchored, "PARAMS_FROZEN", True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(g1, "load_cache", lambda path: {})
    assert g1.main(["--out", str(out)]) == 2 and not out.exists()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the replay**

<!-- apply: create experiments/p2_replay.py -->
```python
"""G1 (spec v3.1 §8.2): the v3.1 fit, with the parameters frozen at G0, on cached base runs.

Read-only by construction: it reads the run cache (runs.jsonl) and never executes a run,
never opens the database (no Workbench) and never calls Ollama. The Tell PDFs are
extracted into a temporary directory, so nothing under SPECDX_WORK is written; the only
file written is --out. Runs are mapped to the base specification by recomputing
Runner.key's run key, with the mapping specdx/sim/calibrate.py shares with the calibration
(smoke_runs, mapped, P1_SETTINGS, NoRefutation; ruling F7):

- the P1b smoke run: the screened population (experiments/p1b-gate.json) on the smoke
  report's scenarios (results/p1b-smoke-base.json), under the current context digest
  (RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION 3); 60 runs;
- the P1 base runs: the P1 population (experiments/p1-gate.json) on P1's scenarios, under
  P1's context digest (RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION 2 and P1's executor settings); 200 runs.

No refutation is attempted (no run may execute), so a hidden candidate is typed hidden as
an unattempted refutation would type it (v1 §11 item 13).

Criteria (spec v3.1 §8.2): (i) θ_m ≥ 0.5 for gpt-oss:20b and qwen3:30b-a3b on the smoke
run; (ii) R-SO (golden:so:SO-0031:trang_thai, whose cell is grn12|so:SO-0031:trang_thai) is
reported as a hidden requirement, judged on the P1b smoke run only (author ruling,
2026-10-14: P1's two acted readings at that cell equal the anchor), and "not observable"
when no acted behaviour read its cell. The P1 base runs are fitted and reported as a
no-collapse check without a criterion.

Exit status: 0 PASS, 1 FAIL, 2 error (a broken mapping, or provisional parameters).
Usage: SPECDX_WORK=/path/to/work .venv/bin/python experiments/p2_replay.py --out PATH
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import dataclasses
import json
import sys
import tempfile
from collections import Counter
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from specdx.config import REPO_ROOT, WORK_DIR
from specdx.infer import anchored
from specdx.infer.act import Act, Rasch, fit_rasch
from specdx.infer.anchored import AnchoredFit, V3Params, fit_anchored
from specdx.infer.cells import CellData
from specdx.infer.items import acted_violations
from specdx.infer.typing import CellDiagnosis, diagnose_v3
from specdx.interpreters.cache import run_key
from specdx.interpreters.population import committee
from specdx.interpreters.prompt import load_variants
from specdx.observe.run import context_digest
from specdx.scenarios.space import boundary_domain, canon_scenarios, enumerate_scenarios
from specdx.sim.calibrate import (
    P1_SCHEMA,
    P1_SETTINGS,
    SMOKE_GATE,
    Base,
    CalibrationError,
    NoRefutation,
    gate_members,
    load_cache,
    model_digests,
    open_base,
    smoke_runs,
)
from specdx.types import Behaviour
from specdx.workbench import tell_payload

P1_GATE = REPO_ROOT / "experiments" / "p1-gate.json"
P1_RUNS = 200
THETA_FLOOR = 0.5  # (i)
NO_COLLAPSE: tuple[str, ...] = ("gpt-oss:20b", "qwen3:30b-a3b")
R_SO = "golden:so:SO-0031:trang_thai"  # (ii): SO-0031 stays Chờ duyệt after APR-0102
R_SO_CELL = "grn12|so:SO-0031:trang_thai"


class ReplayError(Exception):
    """The cache does not map as expected, or the parameters are not G0's: no verdict."""


@dataclass
class Replay:
    """unmatched: per table.column, the cells of rows that match no golden row (§12's
    inspection of the row-matching rule)."""

    name: str
    runs: list[Behaviour]
    data: CellData
    rasch: Rasch
    fit: AnchoredFit
    diagnosis: CellDiagnosis
    unmatched: dict[str, int]


def unmatched_rows(base: Base, data: CellData) -> dict[str, int]:
    """Value cells of rows that match no golden row, counted per table.column."""
    out: Counter[str] = Counter()
    for cid, cell in data.cells.items():
        if cell.kind != "value" or "|" not in cid:
            continue
        table, rest = cid.split("|", 1)[1].split(":", 1)
        name = rest.rsplit(":", 1)[0]
        if name not in set(base.cells.rows[table].golden.values()):
            out[cell.location] += 1
    return dict(sorted(out.items()))


def p1_runs(base: Base, cache: Mapping[str, Behaviour],
            digests: Mapping[str, str]) -> list[Behaviour]:
    """P1's population on P1's scenarios: one per parameter vector of the boundary domain
    without T literals, a canon name winning over a synthesised id."""
    by_signature = {s.signature(): s for s in enumerate_scenarios(boundary_domain(base.pack))}
    for s in canon_scenarios(base.pack).values():
        by_signature[s.signature()] = s
    context = context_digest(tell_payload(load_variants()), P1_SETTINGS, schema=P1_SCHEMA)
    out = []
    for _, scenario in sorted(by_signature.items()):
        params = json.dumps(scenario.params(), sort_keys=True)
        for interp in committee(gate_members(P1_GATE), (0, 1, 2), (1, 2, 3)):
            if interp.model not in digests:
                continue
            key = run_key(base.variant.digest(), base.steps_digest, context, params,
                          interp.key, "", digests[interp.model])
            if (b := cache.get(key)) is not None:
                out.append(dataclasses.replace(b, scenario=scenario, interpreter=interp))
    if len(out) != P1_RUNS:
        raise ReplayError(f"P1 base: {len(out)} runs mapped, expected {P1_RUNS}")
    return out


def replay(name: str, base: Base, runs: list[Behaviour],
           population: Mapping[str, tuple[str, ...]], params: V3Params) -> Replay:
    lens = base.lens
    acts: list[Act] = []
    violated: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {}
    for b in runs:
        violated[b.run_key] = acted_violations(lens.views(b, lens.violations(b)))
        acts.extend(lens.acts(b))
    rasch = fit_rasch(acts)
    data = base.cells.extract(runs)
    models = {m: f for f, ms in population.items() for m in ms}
    fit = fit_anchored(data.cells, data.readings, models=models, params=params)
    diagnosis = diagnose_v3(runs, violated, data, fit, NoRefutation(),
                            observable=rasch.observable_items(), relevant=lens.relevant)
    return Replay(name, runs, data, rasch, fit, diagnosis, unmatched_rows(base, data))


def theta_holds(r: Replay) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """(i): no collapse. Every named model has θ_m ≥ THETA_FLOOR."""
    low = [m for m in NO_COLLAPSE if r.fit.theta.get(m, 0.0) < THETA_FLOOR]
    shown = ", ".join(f"{m} {r.fit.theta.get(m, float('nan')):.3f}" for m in NO_COLLAPSE)
    return not low, f"θ: {shown}"


def r_so_status(r: Replay) -> str:
    """(ii): reported, missed, or not observable (no acted behaviour read the cell)."""
    if not any(x.cell == R_SO_CELL for x in r.data.readings):
        return "not observable"
    reported = any(d.type == "hidden" and d.location == (R_SO,) for d in r.diagnosis.defects)
    return "reported" if reported else "missed"


def verdict(smoke: Replay) -> bool:
    return theta_holds(smoke)[0] and r_so_status(smoke) != "missed"


def _cell_line(r: Replay) -> str:
    post = r.fit.cells.get(R_SO_CELL)
    if post is None:
        return "no reading"
    readings = Counter((x.model, x.value) for x in r.data.readings if x.cell == R_SO_CELL)
    forms = ", ".join(f"{k} {v:.3f}" for k, v in post.forms.items())
    return (f"anchor {post.anchor!r}; readings {dict(sorted(readings.items()))}; "
            f"P(A ≠ {{d}}) = {post.p_defect:.3f}; forms {forms}; MAP {sorted(post.map_set)}")


def render(replays: list[Replay], params: V3Params) -> str:
    smoke = replays[0]
    passed = verdict(smoke)
    ok_i, why_i = theta_holds(smoke)
    out = ["<!-- generated by experiments/p2_replay.py -->", "",
           f"# G1: {'PASS' if passed else 'FAIL'}", "",
           f"Parameters (G0, frozen: {anchored.PARAMS_FROZEN}): {dataclasses.asdict(params)}.",
           "", "## Criteria (spec v3.1 §8.2, on the P1b smoke run)", "",
           f"- (i) θ_m ≥ {THETA_FLOOR} for {' and '.join(NO_COLLAPSE)}: "
           f"{'yes' if ok_i else 'no'} ({why_i}).",
           f"- (ii) R-SO (`{R_SO}`) reported as a hidden requirement: {r_so_status(smoke)}.",
           ""]
    for r in replays:
        kinds = Counter(c.kind for c in r.data.cells.values())
        anchored_cells = sum(c.anchor is not None for c in r.data.cells.values())
        typed = Counter(d.type for d in r.diagnosis.defects)
        out += [f"## {r.name}", "",
                f"{len(r.runs)} runs; {len(r.data.cells)} cells ({kinds['value']} value, "
                f"{kinds['event']} event, {kinds['constraint']} constraint; {anchored_cells} "
                f"anchored); {len(r.data.readings)} readings; "
                f"{len(r.rasch.observable_items())} of {len(r.rasch.delta)} items observable.",
                "", f"EM: converged {r.fit.converged} after {r.fit.iterations} iterations "
                f"from start {r.fit.start}; log posterior per start: "
                + ", ".join(f"{k} {v:.1f}" for k, v in r.fit.starts.items()) + ".", "",
                "| model | α | θ | readings |", "|---|---:|---:|---:|"]
        reads = Counter(x.model for x in r.data.readings)
        out += [f"| {m} | {r.fit.alpha[m]:.3f} | {r.fit.theta[m]:.3f} | {reads[m]} |"
                for m in sorted(r.fit.alpha)]
        out += ["", "Family effect: " + ", ".join(
            f"{f} {v:+.3f}" for f, v in sorted(r.fit.family_effect.items())) + ".", "",
            f"Typed without refutation (C/H/A/W): {typed['contradiction']}/{typed['hidden']}/"
            f"{typed['ambiguity']}/{typed['weak']}; unobservable {len(r.diagnosis.unobservable)}.",
            "", f"R-SO cell `{R_SO_CELL}`: {_cell_line(r)}.", "",
            "Rows matching no golden row (spec v3 §12: inspect the row-matching rule):", ""]
        out += [f"- {loc}: {n} cells" for loc, n in r.unmatched.items()] or ["- none"]
        out.append("")
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--provisional", action="store_true",
                        help="run with V3Params' provisional defaults (a plan pre-check, "
                             "never G1)")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        if not anchored.PARAMS_FROZEN and not args.provisional:
            raise ReplayError("V3Params is not frozen: run G0 and freeze its parameters first")
        params = V3Params()
        cache = load_cache(WORK_DIR / "runs.jsonl")
        digests = model_digests(cache)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as pdf:
            base = open_base(Path(pdf))
            replays = [
                replay("P1b smoke run (base)", base, smoke_runs(base, cache, digests),
                       gate_members(SMOKE_GATE), params),
                replay("P1 base runs", base, p1_runs(base, cache, digests),
                       gate_members(P1_GATE), params)]
    except (ReplayError, CalibrationError) as exc:
        print(f"error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.out.write_text(render(replays, params), encoding="utf-8")
    return 0 if verdict(replays[0]) else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

### Task 13: Run G0, freeze its parameters, and run the study at them (operational, CPU only)

G0 calibrates the simulation from base runs, runs the pre-registered grid and selection rule at 10 replicates, and checks spec v3.1 §8.2's criteria. It needs no GPU and makes no model call. The full grid is 36 prior settings × 3 arms × 10 replicates (1,080 fits), plus the criteria at four points on the selection replicates and the same criteria on the next ten seeds for information only: about 24 minutes on this machine (the pre-check's full preview). The simulation study at the frozen parameters takes about 10 minutes more.

Both long commands run as background jobs that write their exit status to their log; the steps poll with bounded waits, never `tail -f` (ruling F26).

**Files:**
- Create (by commands): `results/p2-calibration.json`, `results/p2-g0.json`, `results/p2-g0.md`, `experiments/p2-g0-params.json`, `results/p2-sim.json`, `results/p2-sim.md`
- Modify (by command): `specdx/infer/anchored.py` (the marked block only)
- Create: `tests/test_g0_frozen.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 11's `python -m specdx.sim.calibrate`, `python -m specdx.sim.g0 run|freeze|table`; Task 8's `specdx simulate`.
- Produces: `PARAMS_FROZEN = True` and `V3Params()` equal to G0's choice; the files above. Task 14 refuses to run without them.

```bash

```
Then poll. Each check returns within nine minutes; repeat it until the log's last line is `exit N`:
```bash

```
The progress lines read `G0: n/36 prior settings`. When it has finished, the line before `exit N` is the verdict: `PASS` with `exit 0`, or `FAIL` with `exit 1`; `exit 2` is an error. Repeat the polling line until it prints `exit 0` (about 10 minutes). `results/p2-sim.md` then holds eight sweeps, none labelled exploratory. The study is reporting only: no value is chosen from it.

```python
"""V3Params carries the values G0 chose (spec v3 §9), and G0 passed."""
```

- Produces:

It pins every new fixed value of spec v3.1 §9 (κ = 10 and the flat pool, difficulty grouping, multi-start, 10 replicates, τ ∈ {1, 2}, four models, the revised G0–G3 criteria), records planning resolutions 1–18 and the rulings that changed behaviour (ruling F10), states that G0's criteria are judged on the selection replicates (F22), and discloses the G0 previews and every change made after the first (F23, F24);
