- [ ] **Step 1: Write the pre-registration**

<!-- apply: create experiments/p2-preregistration.md -->
```markdown
# P2 (SpecDx v3.1) pre-registration

- **Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-specdx-v3-design.md` (v3.1), a delta on `2026-10-11-specdx-v2-design.md` (v2), itself a delta on `2026-10-06-specdx-design.md` (v1). The v3.1 amendments and their evidence are in its §13 items 8–14 and `results/p2-spikes.md`.
- **Plan:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-14-specdx-p2a.md`, as amended by the author's rulings F1–F26 on the pre-flight scan (2026-10-15).
- **Rule:** this file is committed after G1 and before G2 (spec v3.1 §9). Nothing in it changes after any P2b result is seen. `tests/test_preregistration_v3.py` pins every named constant below to the code, and the values chosen at G0 to §4's table; `tests/test_g0_frozen.py` pins them to `experiments/p2-g0-params.json`.
- **Integrity (spec §8.3):** every threshold and prior was chosen by simulation and base data only (G0, G1). The development mutants are used once, at gate v3. The test split stays untouched.

## 1. Values unchanged from v1 and v2

| Value | Registered |
|---|---|
| Temperature | 0.7 |
| Tell variants / seeds | 0–2 / 1–3 |
| Budgets (population / probe / localise) | 200 / 100 / 80 |
| Scorability | k = 3 |
| Candidate values per cell | K_max = 6, plus OTHER |
| Hitting-set tolerance / maximum size | τ = 0.05 / 3 |
| Development split | `dev_per_type` 5, `rng_seed` 20261006 |
| Harness nudges per step | 2 |
| Observability | ω = 0.3, m_min = 3 |
| Rasch L2 penalty | λ = 1.0 |
| T-boundary scenarios per specification | at most 12 |
| Workers | at most `OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL` |
| Gate rule | contradiction and hidden: SpecDx typed F1 > max(B2, B3) untyped F1; ambiguity and weak: SpecDx typed F1 ≥ 0.4; a type with fewer than 3 scorable mutants is a loss; at least 3 wins of 4; exact-arithmetic F1 |

## 2. Values fixed by spec v3.1

| Value | Registered |
|---|---|
| Entailment sets | \|A_c\| ≤ 2: one value, a pair, or ∅; 22 candidate sets with six values |
| Candidate values (§3.1) | d_c first, then the most frequent readings, ties by the normalised string, K_max = 6 in all; OTHER is never in A_c |
| Normalisation (§2.5) | NFC, trim, whitespace collapsed, casefold, numerals canonicalised; no semantic mapping |
| Row matching (§2.6) | by natural key; a key none of whose golden values occurs in T as a token is executor-generated, and its rows match on the remaining LOCKED foreign-key columns |
| Cell scope (§2.4) | value cells at the steps that draft their table; constraint cells at the steps v2's relevance rule gives |
| Pooling (§2.7) | a lifted golden clause or a cross-cutting clause is one cell across the scenarios where it applies, read in the lifted vocabulary |
| Errors (§3.3) | ε_c ~ Dirichlet(κ·u_c), u_c flat over V_c, κ = 10, fixed |
| Difficulty (§3.2) | p = σ(α_m − β_{g(c)}); g(c) is a value cell's column (pooled over rows and scenarios), an event cell's kind, a constraint cell's clause; β_g ~ N(0, τ²) |
| EM (§3.4) | multi-start from T3's median start and a common α of 0, 1, 2 and 3, keeping the highest log posterior; tolerance 1e-6 on the largest parameter change, at most 200 iterations, non-convergence reported |
| Ambiguity (§5.3) | P(\|A_c\| = 2) ≥ τ_A; no rank-correlation rule and no accuracy-contrast gate; on an anchored cell an ambiguity and its hidden requirement co-occur (§3.1, §5.1, §5.3) |
| Screen and population (§7) | act rate ≥ 0.3, valid-call rate ≥ 0.8, ≤ 2 per family, ≤ 4 in all; at least 2 families and at least 4 acting models, otherwise STOP (G2) |
| G0 (§8.1–§8.2) | at least 10 replicates per arm; τ ∈ {1, 2} in the grid; the regime map (accuracy 0.6–0.95 × Δ 0–0.2 × M 2 and 4, win, tie or loss with a margin of 0.05), 5 per point of the regime map |
| GO criteria | G0–G3 as in §5 below |

## 3. Planning-time resolutions and rulings (P2a plan)

These were settled while planning P2a, before G0 ran, or by the author's rulings F1–F26 on the plan's pre-flight scan (2026-10-15). Each is in the plan's list with its task.

1. **Lifted vocabulary.** A pooled value cell's anchor is the clause's placeholder (`{ordered}`); a reading becomes the placeholder `lift_value` gives in its scenario. When the reading equals several parameters (happy and discount8 order and receive 15), it becomes the anchor's own placeholder if the anchor's parameter is among them.
2. **Booleans.** A bool column's t/f (golden CSVs) and true/false (end states) are one value, as C's own comparison treats them.
3. **Scope of tables no step drafts.** apr and evt rows are written through tools; their cells are read at the steps whose work-order slice keys the row (`apr=APR-0102`, `evt=SO-0031`). Key and matching columns are row identity, not readings.
4. **Readings.** A behaviour reads a cell once, if it acted at any in-scope item; several matching rows give the smallest normalised value.
5. **Difficulty groups.** A value cell's group is its column, pooled over rows and scenarios; an event cell's is its event kind (`evt:DRAFTED`), the event table's analogue of a column; a constraint cell's is its clause.
6. **The M-step.** α and β: r as fractional successes out of q_c = P(A_c ≠ ∅) trials per reading, summed over each difficulty group. ε_c: the mode of Dirichlet(1 + κ·u_c + expected error counts).
7. **Refutation of a pooled candidate.** It runs in the scenario with the most acted readings outside d_c, ties to the smaller scenario id.
8. **Singleton hitting sets defer to the cell path.** It is the only source of hidden requirements and of contradictions of size 1; hitting sets of two or more clauses feed multi-clause contradictions. `spent` holds the multi-clause candidates that cost a probe run, or would have, and found nothing.
9. **Defect locations.** Findings reuse v2's location formats, so the matching code is unchanged. A hidden finding's subtype (mis-key, ambiguous-pick, unstated) is informational and never scored.
10. **Provisional parameters.** V3Params' defaults were labelled provisional until G0 froze its choice into the marked block; κ = 10 is a constant outside that block.
11. **Typing without an accuracy contrast, and co-occurrence.** An ambiguity is P(|A_c| = 2) ≥ τ_A for any number of models; the hidden subtype follows the most probable defect form (§5.1's "MAP form"). On an anchored cell an ambiguity and its hidden requirement co-occur (ruling F1): a reported ambiguity brings its hidden candidate (ambiguous-pick, scored max(P(A_c ≠ {d_c}), P(|A_c| = 2))), and a hidden candidate whose most probable form is a pair brings its ambiguity. An unanchored cell reports the ambiguity alone.
12. **The ddmin oracle.** It re-runs every population model with an acted behaviour, highest θ_m first, under each Tell variant at seed 1, and tests P(|A_c| = 2) ≥ τ_A on the re-runs' readings with α, β_{g(c)}, ε_c and V_c held at their fitted values.
13. **G2 on the cached screen.** `specdx screen --from results/p1b-screen.json` applies the current admission rules to the cached measurements, with no run, database or model.
14. **Simulation design.** A defect form and a difficulty belong to a column; truths are pairs (ambiguity), anchored cells with A_c ≠ {d_c} (hidden) and unanchored determined cells (weak); the defect rate is planted at 0.15; the regime map spreads accuracies by Δ around the mean, clipped to 0.51–0.995. Limitation (ruling F24): a simulated difficulty group is a single-form column whose cells differ only by scenario, while a real value column pools rows of different forms and anchoring under one β; G0 does not exercise that case.
15. **Calibration.** Base runs only. Accuracy, the plurality that selects its cells, and the dispersion of logit accuracy are measured on the population's readings; the dispersion is kept within 0.25–3; the error concentration is the nearest of six that reproduces the mean top-error share; the cell mix and |V| are shares of columns, as the generator draws them. Readings per cell follow from the planned budget (⌊200 / (models × 9)⌋ scenarios); the base runs' readings per cell are reported beside the simulated ones. A statistic without data raises instead of falling back (rulings F12, F13).
16. **G0 (iii)'s "population's own M and Δ"** is the regime-map point nearest the calibrated population: its number of models, the grid accuracy nearest its mean accuracy and the grid spread nearest its accuracy range.
17. **θ_m and w_b.** B2 and the refuter's interpreter order use θ_m (§8.3, §11: per-model quantities); w_b weights the hitting sets (§3.5) (ruling F17).
18. **§5.2's V(b).** It is v2 §3.4's acted violation set, over every clause of C, golden values and events included: the literal "constraint cells" would drop the golden-and-invariant conflicts that are the C2 mutants' truths (ruling F18).

## 4. Values chosen at G0

Selection rule (spec §9, fixed in `specdx/sim/g0.py` before G0 ran): the grid π ∈ {0.05, 0.1, 0.2}; (s_mis, s_amb, s_∅) ∈ {(0.6, 0.2, 0.2), (0.4, 0.3, 0.3), (0.2, 0.4, 0.4)}; τ ∈ {1, 2}; (μ_α, σ_α) ∈ {(0, 1.5), (1, 1)}; τ_H ∈ {0.5, 0.7, 0.9}; τ_A ∈ {0.3, 0.5, 0.7}; τ_W ∈ {0.6, 0.8, 0.9}. Ten replicates per arm. The objective is the mean simulated typed F1 over hidden requirement, ambiguity and weak check, at the calibrated parameters (`results/p2-calibration.json`) and under the A3 and A5 arms, equally weighted. Ties go to higher P(|A| = 1) on an anchored cell, then smaller π, then grid order.

G0's criteria are judged on the selection replicates, the same ten seeds per arm that chose the parameters (ruling F22). `results/p2-g0.md` also reports the criteria on the next ten seeds, for information only, and counts the fits that did not converge (ruling F2).

<!-- G0 table: begin -->
<!-- G0 table: end -->

G0's report is `results/p2-g0.md`; G1's is `results/p2-g1.md`. Both passed. The simulation study at the frozen parameters (§8.1, every sweep and the regime map) is `results/p2-sim.md`.

## 5. GO steps and gate v3

| Step | Pass criterion |
|---|---|
| G0 | (i) mean \|θ̂ − θ\| ≤ 0.05; (ii) typed F1 ≥ 0.6 for hidden requirement, ≥ 0.4 for ambiguity and ≥ 0.4 for weak check; (iii) at the calibrated point, under the A3 and A5 arms, and at the population's own M and Δ, v3.1 is never below the best baseline by more than 0.05 on any type, and at the calibrated point it beats the best baseline on ambiguity by more than 0.05. If G0 fails, stop and report |
| G1 | (i) θ_m ≥ 0.5 for gpt-oss:20b and qwen3:30b-a3b; (ii) R-SO reported as a hidden requirement, judged on the P1b smoke run only (author ruling, 2026-10-14), or "not observable" if no acted behaviour read its cell |
| G2 | `specdx screen --from results/p1b-screen.json` admits at least 2 families and at least 4 acting models; the population is frozen into `experiments/p2-gate.json` |
| G3 | the base specification on the 48 GB host, budget 60: every P1b smoke check passes, and θ_m ≥ 0.5 for each population model; convergence is reported, not a criterion (ruling F15) |
| Gate v3 | the 20 development mutants under §1's gate rule; B2 uses the interpreter with the highest θ_m |

A failing GO step stops P2 with a report; no parameter is re-tuned.

## 6. Population (spec §7) and disclosure

The 0.3 act rate was chosen during design, knowing which cached screen results it admits. The screen is outcome-free: it reads no mutant, truth or diagnosis. Applied to `results/p1b-screen.json` it admits gpt-oss:20b (openai), qwen3:30b-a3b and qwen2.5:14b (qwen) and mistral-small3.2:24b (mistral), four acting models in three families; qwen3:14b (0.407) is ranked out by the per-family cap. The four-model requirement and the other v3.1 amendments were decided after the P2a plan's pre-check and two simulation spikes, before G0, with no mutant seen (spec §13 items 8–14).

**G0 previews (ruling F23).** Before execution began, a full G0 (36 × 27 settings, 10 replicates) ran on the plan's pre-check code: `results/p2-spikes/g0-preview-full.md`, PASS, choosing π 0.1, shares (0.6, 0.2, 0.2), τ 2, (μ_α, σ_α) = (1, 1), τ_H 0.9, τ_A 0.3 and τ_W 0.8, with weak-check F1 0.403. After it, two rulings changed what G0 runs:
- F1: on an anchored cell an ambiguity and its hidden requirement co-occur (§3 item 11);
- F12: the calibration's plurality and dispersion use the population's readings only, and the cell mix is counted per column (§3 item 15).

Both correct the code to the spec and to the plan's own resolution; neither tunes a value. A second full preview ran on the amended code during the plan's re-check, and the plan's header records its outcome. No grid, threshold, criterion or seed was changed after either preview.
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The pinning test**

<!-- apply: create tests/test_preregistration_v3.py -->
```python
"""Every value of the v3.1 pre-registration (spec v3.1 §9, experiments/p2-preregistration.md)
is the value the code uses, including those G0 chose. Changing one after a P2b result is
seen breaks this test."""

import dataclasses
import importlib.util
import inspect
import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from specdx.active.loop import SpecDxConfig
from specdx.eval import matching, observability, screen
from specdx.eval.pilot import GateConfig, load_population
from specdx.infer import act, anchored, cells, hitting
from specdx.infer.anchored import V3Params, fit_anchored
from specdx.infer.cells import Cell, Reading
from specdx.interpreters import executor, harness
from specdx.scenarios import tguided
from specdx.sim import calibrate, g0, study

ROOT = Path(__file__).parents[1]
DOC = ROOT / "experiments" / "p2-preregistration.md"
_CFG = SpecDxConfig.__dataclass_fields__


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("value", "registered"), [
    (executor.TEMPERATURE, 0.7), (harness.MAX_NUDGES, 2),
    (act.OMEGA, 0.3), (act.M_MIN, 3), (act.RASCH_L2, 1.0),
    (tguided.MAX_TBOUNDARY, 12), (observability.SCORABLE_K, 3),
    (matching.MIN_SCORABLE, 3), (matching.ABSOLUTE_BAR, 0.4),
    (inspect.signature(matching.gate_verdict).parameters["required"].default, 3),
    (inspect.signature(hitting.soft_hitting_sets).parameters["max_size"].default, 3),
    (_CFG["k_max"].default, 6), (_CFG["tau"].default, 0.05),
    (anchored.K_MAX, 6), (len(anchored.SETS), 22), (max(len(s) for s in anchored.SETS), 2),
    (anchored.EM_TOL, 1e-6), (anchored.EM_MAX_ITER, 200), (anchored.STARTS, (0.0, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0)),
    (screen.ACT_RATE_MIN, 0.3), (screen.VALID_RATE_MIN, 0.8), (screen.PER_FAMILY, 2),
    (screen.MAX_MODELS, 4), (screen.MIN_FAMILIES, 2), (screen.MIN_MODELS, 4),
    (anchored.KAPPA, 10.0),
    ({f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(V3Params)},
     {"pi", "s_mis", "s_amb", "s_empty", "beta_sd", "mu_alpha", "sigma_alpha", "tau_h", "tau_a",
      "tau_w"}),
    (cells.CONSTRAINT_KINDS,
     frozenset({"invariant", "expectation", "blank", "reconcile", "guard_pay", "step"})),
    (g0.PI, (0.05, 0.1, 0.2)),
    (g0.SHARES, ((0.6, 0.2, 0.2), (0.4, 0.3, 0.3), (0.2, 0.4, 0.4))),
    (g0.BETA_SD, (1.0, 2.0)), (g0.ALPHA_PRIOR, ((0.0, 1.5), (1.0, 1.0))),
    (g0.TAU_H, (0.5, 0.7, 0.9)), (g0.TAU_A, (0.3, 0.5, 0.7)), (g0.TAU_W, (0.6, 0.8, 0.9)),
    (g0.ARMS, ("base", "a3", "a5")), (g0.REPLICATES, 10),
    (g0.THETA_ERROR_MAX, 0.05), (g0.F1_MIN, {"hidden": 0.6, "ambiguity": 0.4, "weak": 0.4}),
    (g0.AMBIGUITY_WIN, 0.05), (study.MARGIN, 0.05),
    (study.REGIME_ACCURACY, (0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 0.95)), (study.REGIME_DELTA, (0.0, 0.1, 0.2)),
    (study.REGIME_MODELS, (2, 4)),
    ((study.min_replicates("arms"), study.min_replicates("regime")), (10, 5)),
    (calibrate.BUDGET, 200), (calibrate.DEFECT_RATE, 0.15),
])
def test_the_code_uses_the_v3_pre_registered_values(value, registered) -> None:
    assert value == registered


def test_errors_pull_to_a_flat_pool_and_difficulty_is_grouped() -> None:
    cells_ = {c: Cell(c, "value", "d", f"golden:{c}", "t.x", g, ("s",))
              for c, g in (("c1", "t.x"), ("c2", "t.x"), ("c3", "t.y"))}
    readings = [Reading(c, f"{m}|{c}|{i}", m, m, "s", "d" if i % 4 else "e")
                for c in cells_ for m in ("m1", "m2") for i in range(8)]
    fit = fit_anchored(cells_, readings, models={"m1": "m1", "m2": "m2"}, params=V3Params())
    assert fit.cells["c1"].beta == fit.cells["c2"].beta  # one β per group (v3.1 §3.2)
    assert fit.cells["c1"].beta != fit.cells["c3"].beta
    flat = [Reading("c1", f"m1|{i}", "m1", "m1", "s", "d") for i in range(3)]
    alone = fit_anchored({"c1": cells_["c1"]}, flat, models={"m1": "m1"}, params=V3Params())
    # ε_c = (counts + κ·u_c)/(N + κ) with u_c flat over V_c and OTHER: the only error counts are
    # the anchor readings' share under ∅, 3·P(A_c = ∅)
    post = alone.cells["c1"]
    e = 3 * post.sets[frozenset()]
    kappa = anchored.KAPPA
    assert post.error["d"] == pytest.approx((kappa / 2 + e) / (kappa + e), abs=1e-4)
    assert post.error[anchored.OTHER] == pytest.approx((kappa / 2) / (kappa + e), abs=1e-4)


def test_the_pre_registration_states_the_values_g0_chose() -> None:
    # tests/test_g0_frozen.py (Task 13) pins V3Params to G0's choice; this pins the document.
    assert g0.params_table(V3Params()) in DOC.read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_the_g1_criteria_are_the_pre_registered_ones() -> None:
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("p2_replay", ROOT / "experiments" /
                                                  "p2_replay.py")
    assert spec is not None and spec.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[spec.name] = module
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    assert module.THETA_FLOOR == 0.5
    assert module.NO_COLLAPSE == ("gpt-oss:20b", "qwen3:30b-a3b")
    assert module.R_SO == "golden:so:SO-0031:trang_thai"


def test_the_committed_g1_record_passed_without_collapse() -> None:
    # Spec v3.1 §11's regression on the P1b smoke data is G1 (Tasks 12 and 14): its committed
    # record must stay a PASS with both named models at θ_m ≥ 0.5.
    text = (ROOT / "results" / "p2-g1.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert text.splitlines()[2] == "# G1: PASS"
    theta = re.search(r"\(θ: gpt-oss:20b ([0-9.]+), qwen3:30b-a3b ([0-9.]+)\)", text)
    assert theta is not None and min(float(theta[1]), float(theta[2])) >= 0.5
    assert re.search(r"reported as a hidden requirement: (reported|not observable)\.", text)


def test_the_gate_v3_configuration_is_the_p1_split_and_budgets(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    monkeypatch.chdir(ROOT)  # the screen path is relative to the repository root
    path = ROOT / "experiments" / "p2-gate.json"
    config = GateConfig.load(path)
    p1 = GateConfig.load(ROOT / "experiments" / "p1-gate.json")
    assert (config.dev_per_type, config.rng_seed) == (p1.dev_per_type, p1.rng_seed) == (
        5, 20261006)
    assert (config.budget, config.probe_budget, config.localize_budget) == (200, 100, 80)
    assert (config.variants, config.seeds) == ((0, 1, 2), (1, 2, 3))
    assert config.screen == Path("results/p2-screen.json")
    # The gate runs on the frozen V3Params, never on parameters a JSON carries.
    carried = tmp_path / "p2-gate.json"
    carried.write_text(json.dumps({**json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")),
                                   "params": {**dataclasses.asdict(V3Params()), "pi": 0.9}}),
                       encoding="utf-8")
    assert GateConfig.load(carried).params == V3Params()
    if config.models_by_family:  # frozen at G2: it must be the re-admitted population
        assert config.models_by_family == load_population(ROOT / "results" / "p2-screen.json")
    else:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="empty"):
            config.check_population()


def test_the_pre_registration_states_each_value() -> None:
    text = DOC.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for needle in ("| Temperature | 0.7 |", "0–2 / 1–3", "200 / 100 / 80", "k = 3", "K_max = 6",
                   "τ = 0.05 / 3", "`dev_per_type` 5, `rng_seed` 20261006", "ω = 0.3, m_min = 3",
                   "λ = 1.0", "at most 12", "OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL", r"\|A_c\| ≤ 2",
                   "u_c flat over V_c, κ = 10, fixed", "an event cell's kind",
                   "a constraint cell's clause", "common α of 0, 1, 2 and 3",
                   "tolerance 1e-6", "at most 200 iterations", "no rank-correlation rule",
                   "co-occur", "≥ 0.3", "at least 4 acting models", "at least 10 replicates",
                   "5 per point of the regime map", "τ ∈ {1, 2}", r"mean \|θ̂ − θ\| ≤ 0.05",
                   "≥ 0.6 for hidden requirement",
                   "≥ 0.4 for ambiguity and ≥ 0.4 for weak check",
                   "population's own M and Δ", "by more than 0.05",
                   "θ_m ≥ 0.5 for gpt-oss:20b and qwen3:30b-a3b", "R-SO",
                   "author ruling, 2026-10-14", "at least 2 families", "budget 60",
                   "highest θ_m", "π ∈ {0.05, 0.1, 0.2}", "τ_W ∈ {0.6, 0.8, 0.9}",
                   "Ten replicates", "equally weighted", "smaller π", "qwen3:14b (0.407)",
                   "no parameter is re-tuned", "test split stays untouched", "Both passed",
                   # §3: every planning resolution and ruling of the plan (rulings F10, F17,
```

````markdown
## 11. P2b: G2, G3 and gate v3 on a 48 GB host

P2a (no GPU) froze the v3.1 parameters at G0 and passed G1; `experiments/p2-preregistration.md` holds every value. P2b runs on a 48 GB GPU reached over an SSH tunnel, with SpecDx and PostgreSQL on this machine.

**G2, the population (no GPU, no run).** Re-admit the cached P1b screen under the 0.3 act rate, which must admit at least 2 families and at least 4 acting models, and freeze its population into the gate configuration:

```bash
.venv/bin/specdx screen --from results/p1b-screen.json --out results/p2-screen
jq --slurpfile s results/p2-screen.json '.models_by_family = $s[0].population' \
  experiments/p2-gate.json > work/p2-gate.json && mv work/p2-gate.json experiments/p2-gate.json
.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_preregistration_v3.py
```

Exit status 3 is the G2 stop rule of spec v3.1 §7: fewer than 2 families, or fewer than 4 acting models, were admitted. P2 then stops and reports. Otherwise commit `results/p2-screen.*` and `experiments/p2-gate.json`.

**The tunnel and the host.** On the GPU host: Ollama 0.30.4 with `OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL=2`, `OLLAMA_MAX_LOADED_MODELS=2` and `OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE=-1`, and the population's models pulled. Here:

```bash
ssh -N -L 11435:localhost:11434 user@gpu-host &        # keep it open for the whole run
export SPECDX_OLLAMA_URL=http://localhost:11435
.venv/bin/specdx doctor --profile gpu48                 # digests must equal the cached runs'
```

**G3, the smoke run (hours).** The base specification at budget 60, then the P1b smoke checks (plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-specdx-p1b.md`, Task 18 Step 5) and θ_m ≥ 0.5 for every population model:

```bash
jq '.budget = 60' experiments/p2-gate.json > work/p2-smoke.json
SPECDX_WORK=/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX-paper/work .venv/bin/specdx run --variant base \
  --config work/p2-smoke.json --out results/p2-smoke-base.json --workers 2 2> work/p2-smoke.log
jq -r '.theta | to_entries[] | "\(.key)\t\(.value)\t"
  + (if .value >= 0.5 then "ok" else "BELOW 0.5" end)' results/p2-smoke-base.json
jq '{converged, iterations, fit_start}' results/p2-smoke-base.json
```

A model below 0.5 or a failed smoke check stops P2 before the gate (spec v3.1 §8.2, G3). `converged` is reported with the result, never a stop (§3.4; ruling F15). Report the measured seconds per run to the author before launching the gate.

**Gate v3 (days).** Detached, resumable as in §6 and §7:

```bash
SPECDX_WORK=/home/ethan/AILab/TonomyX-paper/work setsid nohup .venv/bin/specdx gate \
  --config experiments/p2-gate.json --out results/p2-gate.json --workers 2 \
  > work/p2-gate.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &
echo $! > work/p2-gate.pid
```

## Bắt đầu nhanh (tiếng Việt)
````

<!-- apply: replace docs/RUNNING.md -->
```markdown
3. Kiểm tra máy và chọn cấu hình: `.venv/bin/specdx doctor`. Lệnh này gợi ý một profile (gpu16, gpu24, gpu48, gpu80) và in các lệnh `ollama pull` còn thiếu.
4. Nếu Ollama chạy ở máy GPU khác: trên máy đó chạy `OLLAMA_HOST=0.0.0.0:11434 OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL=2 ollama serve`; trên máy này đặt `export SPECDX_OLLAMA_URL=http://may-gpu:11434` (nhiều máy thì cách nhau bằng dấu phẩy). Lưu ý: `--workers` không được vượt quá `OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL` của máy Ollama (mặc định 1), vì yêu cầu phải xếp hàng có thể hết thời gian chờ và bị ghi vĩnh viễn vào cache như hành vi của mô hình; nếu không chắc, dùng `--workers 1`.
5. Sàng lọc năng lực: `.venv/bin/specdx screen --profile gpu16 --out results/p1b-screen --workers 1`. Mã thoát 3 nghĩa là STOP: ít hơn 2 họ mô hình đạt, P1b dừng. Các lệnh ở đây dùng `--workers 1`; chỉ dùng `--workers 2` khi máy Ollama có `OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL` ≥ 2 và cả hai mô hình cùng vừa trong GPU (mục 4).
6. Ghi quần thể vào cấu hình gate (lệnh `jq` ở mục 5), rồi chạy gate nền: `setsid nohup .venv/bin/specdx gate --config experiments/p1b-gate.json --out results/p1b-gate.json --workers 1 > work/p1b-gate.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &`.
7. Tạm dừng: `kill -STOP <pid>`; chạy tiếp: `kill -CONT <pid>`. Không bao giờ chạy hai tiến trình trên cùng một cơ sở dữ liệu. Nếu bị ngắt, chạy lại đúng lệnh cũ: các lần chạy đã xong được lấy từ bộ nhớ đệm.
```

Nếu Ollama chạy ở máy GPU khác: trên máy đó chạy `OLLAMA_HOST=0.0.0.0:11434 OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL=2 ollama serve`; trên máy này đặt `export SPECDX_OLLAMA_URL=http://may-gpu:11434` (nhiều máy thì cách nhau bằng dấu phẩy). Lưu ý: `--workers` không được vượt quá `OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL` của máy Ollama (mặc định 1), vì yêu cầu phải xếp hàng có thể hết thời gian chờ và bị ghi vĩnh viễn vào cache như hành vi của mô hình; nếu không chắc, dùng `--workers 1`.
```markdown
5. Sàng lọc năng lực: `.venv/bin/specdx screen --profile gpu16 --out results/p1b-screen --workers 1`. Mã thoát 3 nghĩa là STOP: theo spec v3.1 §7 (act rate ≥ 0,3), ít hơn 2 họ mô hình hoặc ít hơn 4 mô hình hành động được nhận, công việc dừng. Các lệnh ở đây dùng `--workers 1`; chỉ dùng `--workers 2` khi máy Ollama có `OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL` ≥ 2 và cả hai mô hình cùng vừa trong GPU (mục 4).
6. Ghi quần thể vào cấu hình gate (lệnh `jq` ở mục 5), rồi chạy gate nền: `setsid nohup .venv/bin/specdx gate --config experiments/p1b-gate.json --out results/p1b-gate.json --workers 1 > work/p1b-gate.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &`.
7. Tạm dừng: `kill -STOP <pid>`; chạy tiếp: `kill -CONT <pid>`. Không bao giờ chạy hai tiến trình trên cùng một cơ sở dữ liệu. Nếu bị ngắt, chạy lại đúng lệnh cũ: các lần chạy đã xong được lấy từ bộ nhớ đệm.
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Run the tests**

Run: `.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_preregistration_v3.py tests/test_docs.py`
Expected: `67 passed`.

- [ ] **Step 7: Run the suite and lint**

Run: `.venv/bin/python -m pytest && .venv/bin/ruff check .`
Expected: `602 passed`, `All checks passed!`.

- [ ] **Step 8: Commit**

```bash
git add experiments/p2-preregistration.md experiments/p2-gate.json tests/test_preregistration_v3.py \
  docs/RUNNING.md
git commit -m "docs: the v3 pre-registration, frozen after G1 and before G2; gate v3 config; P2b guide"
```

P2a ends here. P2b (G2, G3 and gate v3 on the 48 GB host) follows `docs/RUNNING.md` §11.

## For the controller

- **Two full G0 previews have been seen (ruling F23).** Neither is G0, and neither chose anything.
  - On the plan's first pre-check code (`results/p2-spikes/g0-preview-full.md`, commit `4ec6037`): PASS, choosing π 0.1, shares (0.6, 0.2, 0.2), τ 2, (μ_α, σ_α) = (1, 1), τ_H 0.9, τ_A 0.3, τ_W 0.8; weak-check F1 0.403, a margin of 0.003.
  - On this amended code (header): **FAIL**, choosing π 0.1, shares (0.6, 0.2, 0.2), τ 2.0, (μ_α, σ_α) = (1.0, 1.0), τ_H 0.5, τ_A 0.5, τ_W 0.9. At the calibrated point, hidden / ambiguity / weak F1 0.861 / 0.837 / 0.343 for v3.1, against 0.742 / 0.714 / 0.316 for majority vote, 0.807 / 0.000 / 0.000 for anchor-only and 0.517 / 0.000 / 0.066 for v2's stage 2; mean |θ̂ − θ| 0.013; failed: (ii) weak. Out of sample, for information only: PASS, v3.1 at the calibrated point 0.789 / 0.776 / 0.427.
  - Rulings F1 (co-occurrence) and F12 (calibration) changed what G0 runs after the first preview; pre-registration §6 discloses both previews and these changes. G0 in Task 13 is deterministic and reproduces the second preview unless code it exercises changes. If G0 fails, Task 13 Step 5 stops P2 with the record: v3.1 has no fallback, and nothing is re-tuned after a G0 result.
- **Why the amended preview fails: sensitivity previews (2026-10-15, scratch copy, the full grid each; information only, nothing chosen from them).**
  - F1 alone (the amended code on the unamended calibration): PASS, with exactly the first preview's choice and numbers (weak 0.403). Co-occurrence does not move G0.
  - F12 (a) alone (accuracy and dispersion from the population's readings, the cell mix per cell as before): PASS, weak 0.401, choosing π 0.1, shares (0.4, 0.3, 0.3), τ 2, (μ_α, σ_α) = (1, 1), τ_H 0.5, τ_A 0.5, τ_W 0.9.
  - F12 (c), the cell mix per column, makes G0 fail on (ii) weak alone, under every reading of a mixed column. Real value columns are anchored in some scenarios only: 38 of the 92 value columns have one anchored cell of four, because a golden row's clause applies in one scenario. The generator anchors a whole column, so a rule is needed; this plan's resolution 15 counts a column as anchored when at least half its cells are (anchored share 0.03 of value columns, against 0.19 of value cells per cell): weak 0.343, no grid setting reaches 0.4. The mean anchored fraction per column (0.16): weak 0.306, no setting reaches 0.4. Anchored when any cell is (0.53): weak 0.236. Per column, the pooled share also moves from 0.17 to 0.36 and the constraint share from 0.16 to 0.34.
  - Weak-check F1 is noisy across seeds: the amended preview's out-of-sample weak is 0.427 against 0.343 on the selection replicates.
  - As amended, Task 13 Step 5 will stop P2. The mixed-column rule of F12 (c), or F12 (c) itself, needs the author's ruling before Task 11 executes; this plan does not change it after seeing these results.
- **A behaviour to watch in G0.** Under κ = 10, a silent cell whose own errors are uneven (ε_c drawn from Dirichlet(10·u) over five values) is often typed as a pair. This is because ε_c starts at the flat pool, and EM then keeps the pair. With errors on the pool (T2′'s limit), all four forms are recovered (Task 5's test). The calibrated generator's concentrated errors make silent cells read as mis-keys instead. G0's A3 arm and criterion (iii) carry this risk.
